```cpp
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// readlink：隐 /proc/self/exe
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static ssize_t fake_readlink(const char* path, char* buf, size_t bufsiz) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    void* ra = HOOK_RA();

    if (path && strcmp(path, "/proc/self/exe") == 0) {
        const char* fake_path = "/system/bin/app_process64";
        size_t len = strlen(fake_path);
        if (len > bufsiz) len = bufsiz;
        memcpy(buf, fake_path, len);
        attr_report("readlink", path, "fake", ra);
        return (ssize_t)len;
    }
    attr_report("readlink", path, "pass", ra);
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_readlink, path, buf, bufsiz);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// access / stat / lstat / fstatat：隐藏敏感路 // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static bool is_hidden_path(const char* p) {
    if (!p) return false;
    static const char* hidden[] = {
        "/data/adb", "magisk", "kernelsu", "lsposed", "xposed", "frida",
        "/system/bin/su", "/system/xbin/su", "/sbin/su", "superuser",
        "busybox", "apatch", "/data/local/tmp/lsp", "/data/local/tmp/frida",
        "re.frida.server", "/system/xposed", nullptr
    };
    for (int i = 0; hidden[i]; i++) {
        if (strstr(p, hidden[i])) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

// 【V8 补全】进程列表过滤：QQ 可扫 /proc/<pid>/cmdline 看到 root 管理进程。
// 通过 open/openat 打开 /proc/<pid>/cmdline 时，记下 fd；read/fgets 时返回伪造内容。


static int fake_access(const char* pathname, int mode) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    void* ra = HOOK_RA();

    if (is_hidden_path(pathname)) {
        probe_log_path(pathname);
        attr_report("access", pathname, "deny", ra);
        errno = ENOENT;
        return -1;
    }
    attr_report("access", pathname, "pass", ra);
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_access, pathname, mode);
}

static int fake_stat(const char* pathname, struct stat* st) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    void* ra = HOOK_RA();
    if (is_hidden_path(pathname)) {
        probe_log_path(pathname);
        attr_report("stat", pathname, "deny", ra);
        errno = ENOENT;
        return -1;
    }
    attr_report("stat", pathname, "pass", ra);
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_stat, pathname, st);
}

static int fake_lstat(const char* pathname, struct stat* st) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    void* ra = HOOK_RA();
    if (is_hidden_path(pathname)) {
        probe_log_path(pathname);
        attr_report("lstat", pathname, "deny", ra);
        errno = ENOENT;
        return -1;
    }
    attr_report("lstat", pathname, "pass", ra);
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_lstat, pathname, st);
}

static int fake_fstatat(int dirfd, const char* pathname, struct stat* st, int flags) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    void* ra = HOOK_RA();
    if (is_hidden_path(pathname)) {
        probe_log_path(pathname);
        attr_report("fstatat", pathname, "deny", ra);
        errno = ENOENT;
        return -1;
    }
    attr_report("fstatat", pathname, "pass", ra);
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_fstatat, dirfd, pathname, st, flags);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 系统属性：__system_property_get / __system_property_read_callback
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static thread_local bool t_in_prop = false;

static bool is_probe_prop(const char* name) {
    if (!name) return false;
    static const char* kw[] = {
        "serial", "verifiedboot", "warranty", "flash.locked", "vbmeta",
        "debuggable", "qemu", "bootloader", "boot.hardware", "build.tags",
        "selinux", "soc", "imei", "android_id", "qimei", "boot.verified",
        "ro.secure", "ro.build.", "ro.product.", "ro.system.", "ro.vendor.",
        "ro.odm.", "ro.boot.", "ro.board.", "ro.hardware", "ro.chipname",
        "ro.config.", "ro.oplus.", "ro.coloros.", "ro.oxygen.", nullptr
    };
    for (int i = 0; kw[i]; i++) {
        if (strstr(name, kw[i])) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

static const char* fake_prop_value(const char* name) {
    if (!name) return nullptr;

    if (g_env_on) {
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.version.release") == 0) return g_env[0];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.version.sdk") == 0) return g_env[1];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.id") == 0) return g_env[2];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.display.id") == 0) return g_env[3];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.system.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.system_ext.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.vendor.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.odm.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.bootimage.build.fingerprint") == 0) {
            return g_env[4];
        }
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.description") == 0) return g_env[5];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.host") == 0) return g_env[6];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.user") == 0) return g_env[7];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.date") == 0) return g_env[8];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.version.incremental") == 0) return g_env[9];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.version.security_patch") == 0) return g_env[10];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.version.opporom") == 0) return g_env[11];
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.version.codename") == 0) return "REL";
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.version.all_codenames") == 0) return "REL";
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.version.preview_sdk") == 0) return "0";
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.version.preview_sdk_int") == 0) return "0";

        // 机型画像
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.product.model") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.vendor.model") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.system.model") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.odm.model") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.product.model") == 0) {
            return g_env[14];
        }
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.product.name") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.vendor.name") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.system.name") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.odm.name") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.product.name") == 0) {
            return g_env[15];
        }
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.product.device") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.vendor.device") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.system.device") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.odm.device") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.product.device") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.build.product") == 0) {
            return g_env[16];
        }
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.product.brand") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.vendor.brand") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.system.brand") == 0) {
            return g_env[17];
        }
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.product.manufacturer") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.vendor.manufacturer") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.system.manufacturer") == 0) {
            return g_env[18];
        }
    }

    if (g_rom_host[0] && strcmp(name, "ro.build.host") == 0) return g_rom_host;
    if (g_rom_user[0] && strcmp(name, "ro.build.user") == 0) return g_rom_user;
    if (g_rom_display[0] && strcmp(name, "ro.build.display.id") == 0) return g_rom_display;
    if (g_rom_fingerprint[0]) {
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.system.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.system_ext.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.vendor.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.odm.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.product.build.fingerprint") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.bootimage.build.fingerprint") == 0) {
            return g_rom_fingerprint;
        }
    }

    if (g_boot_state[0] && strcmp(name, "ro.boot.verifiedbootstate") == 0) {
        return g_boot_state;
    }
    if (g_fake_serial[0]) {
        if (strcmp(name, "ro.serialno") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.boot.serialno") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "ro.vendor.oplus.radio.serialno") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "vendor.oplus.caihong.serialno") == 0 ||
            strcmp(name, "persist.sys.oplus.serialno") == 0) {
            return g_fake_serial;
        }
    }
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.boot.flash.locked") == 0) return "1";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.boot.vbmeta.device_state") == 0) return "locked";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.boot.veritymode") == 0) return "enforcing";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.boot.warranty_bit") == 0) return "0";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.warranty_bit") == 0) return "0";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.vendor.warranty_bit") == 0) return "0";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.vendor.boot.warranty_bit") == 0) return "0";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.boot.selinux") == 0) return "enforcing";
    // 【V7 修复】SoC / 平台标识 —— OPPO Find X7 = 骁龙 8 Gen 3 (SM8650)
    // 真机是 lahaina (SM8350/骁龙888)，必须同步换，否则 k54 与 k57(PGT110) 矛盾
    // 【V9 关键修复】QsecEst 的 k54 来自 ro.product.board，不是 ro.board.platform。
    // 之前只覆盖后者，导致 k54 一直报真机 "lahaina"，与伪造机型 PGT110 矛盾。
    // 这两个是不同的属性，必须都覆盖。
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.product.board") == 0 ||
        strcmp(name, "ro.product.vendor.board") == 0 ||
        strcmp(name, "ro.product.system.board") == 0 ||
        strcmp(name, "ro.product.odm.board") == 0 ||
        strcmp(name, "ro.product.product.board") == 0 ||
        strcmp(name, "ro.board.platform") == 0 ||
        strcmp(name, "ro.chipname") == 0 ||
        strcmp(name, "ro.hardware.chipname") == 0) {
        return "kalama";
    }
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.soc.model") == 0) return "SM8650";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.soc.manufacturer") == 0) return "Qualcomm";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.soc.platform") == 0) return "kalama";

    if (strcmp(name, "ro.debuggable") == 0) return "0";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.secure") == 0) return "1";
    if (strcmp(name, "ro.kernel.qemu") == 0) return "";
    return nullptr;
}

static int fake___system_property_get(const char* name, char* value) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    void* ra = HOOK_RA();

    if (t_in_prop || !name) {
        return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake___system_property_get, name, value);
    }
    t_in_prop = true;

    int r = BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake___system_property_get, name, value);
    if (g_probe && is_probe_prop(name)) {
        LOGI("PROBE prop: %s = %.64s", name, value ? value : "");
    }
    const char* fake = fake_prop_value(name);
    if (fake && value) {
        // 不能用 strncpy(..., 91)：调用方经常给短属性（如 ro.build.version.sdk）
        // 只分配 3-4 字节的栈 buffer，strncpy 的 NUL 填充会直接踩坏栈。
        size_t flen = strlen(fake);
        if (flen > 91) flen = 91;
        memcpy(value, fake, flen);
        value[flen] = 0;
        r = (int)flen;
    }
    attr_report("prop", name, fake ? "fake" : "real", ra);
    t_in_prop = false;
    return r;
}

typedef void (*PropCallback)(void* cookie, const char* name, const char* value, uint32_t serial);

static thread_local PropCallback t_user_cb = nullptr;
static thread_local void* t_user_cookie = nullptr;

static void prop_trampoline(void* cookie, const char* name, const char* value, uint32_t serial) {
    (void)cookie;
    if (!t_user_cb) return;
    const char* fake = fake_prop_value(name);
    t_user_cb(t_user_cookie, name, fake ? fake : value, serial);
}

static void fake___system_property_read_callback(
    const void* pi, void (*callback)(void*, const char*, const char*, uint32_t), void* cookie) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();

    if (t_in_prop || !callback) {
        BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake___system_property_read_callback, pi, callback, cookie);
        return;
    }
    t_in_prop = true;
    t_user_cb = callback;
    t_user_cookie = cookie;
    BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake___system_property_read_callback, pi, prop_trampoline, cookie);
    t_user_cb = nullptr;
    t_user_cookie = nullptr;
    t_in_prop = false;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 【T22 修复】__system_property_find：PCDN/部分 SDK 用 find + read_callback，
// find 返回 prop_info 结构指针，若 find 未被 hook，read_callback 改值可能来不及。
// 做法：find 时记录查询到的属性名（线程局部），read_callback 里据此查表伪造。
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static thread_local const void* t_find_pi = nullptr;
static thread_local char t_find_name[128] = {0};

static const char* prop_key_to_name(const void* pi) {
    // 通过 pi 反查属性名：__system_property_read_callback 回调里能拿到真实 name，
    // 这里用另一个办法：把 find 返回的 pi 记录在 TLS，与 read_callback 的 pi 匹配。
    if (pi == t_find_pi && t_find_name[0]) return t_find_name;
    return nullptr;
}

static const void* fake___system_property_find(const char* name) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    const void* r = BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake___system_property_find, name);
    if (name && r) {
        // 记录 pi -> name，供 read_callback 使用（只记录我们关心的 key，避免开销）
        if (fake_prop_value(name) != nullptr) {
            t_find_pi = r;
            strncpy(t_find_name, name, sizeof(t_find_name) - 1);
            t_find_name[sizeof(t_find_name) - 1] = 0;
        }
    }
    return r;
}

// uname 代理：把内核 release/version 伪装成官方风格
static int fake_uname(struct utsname* buf) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    int r = BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_uname, buf);
    if (r == 0 && g_env_on && buf) {
        snprintf(buf->release, sizeof(buf->release), "%s", g_env[12]);
        snprintf(buf->version, sizeof(buf->version), "%s", g_env[13]);
    }
    return r;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// write 观测：抓 .android_lq 的写入内容（open 记录 fd → write 抓 buf）
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static ssize_t fake_write(int fd, const void* buf, size_t count) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    if (t_lq_write_fd >= 0 && fd == t_lq_write_fd) {
        const unsigned char* p = (const unsigned char*) buf;
        size_t n = count < 128 ? count : 128;
        char hx[256 + 1];
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            snprintf(hx + i * 2, 3, "%02x", p[i]);
        }
        LOGI("WRITE .android_lq fd=%d len=%zu data=%.256s", fd, count, hx);
        t_lq_write_fd = -1;
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_write, fd, buf, count);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 最小化 syscall 观测：定位 .android_lq 的写入函数
//
// fekit 只依赖 libc，直通 syscall 走 libc 的 syscall() 包装（导入 syscall@LIBC）。
// hook 它就能拦到写 .android_lq 的 openat/write，并拿到调用者 RA（= fekit 内部
// 函数偏移，可在 IDA 里定位计算函数）。
//
// 安全设计（前车之鉴：全量 syscall hook 在 pthread_exit 时 TLS 递归 SIGSEGV）：
//  - hook 体零 malloc / 零 C++ 分配，纯内存读 + 直通
//  - 只匹配 syscall 号 56(openat)/64(write)，且路径含 ".android_lq" 才打日志
//  - 不匹配路径时与原始实现等价（无额外开销）
//  - 日志用截断固定长度，避免长字符串处理
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static thread_local int t_lq_fd = -1;

static bool lq_mem_contains(const char* p, size_t maxlen, const char* needle) {
    // 手写包含检查，避免调用 libc strstr（可能触发递归）
    if (!p || !needle) return false;
    size_t nl = 0;
    while (needle[nl]) nl++;
    if (nl == 0 || nl > maxlen) return false;
    for (size_t i = 0; i <= maxlen - nl; i++) {
        if (p[i] == 0) break;
        size_t j = 0;
        while (j < nl && p[i + j] == needle[j]) j++;
        if (j == nl) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

static long fake_syscall(long number, long a1, long a2, long a3,
                         long a4, long a5, long a6) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    void* ra = __builtin_extract_return_addr(__builtin_return_address(0));

#if defined(__aarch64__)
    // arm64: __NR_openat=56, __NR_write=64
    if (number == 56) {
        const char* path = (const char*) a2;  // openat(dirfd, path, flags, mode)
        if (lq_mem_contains(path, 300, ".android_lq")) {
            LOGI("SYSCALL openat .android_lq ra=%p path=%.160s", ra, path ? path : "(null)");
            long r = BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_syscall, number, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6);
            if (r >= 0) t_lq_fd = (int) r;
            return r;
        }
    } else if (number == 64) {
        if (t_lq_fd >= 0 && a1 == (long) t_lq_fd) {
            const char* buf = (const char*) a2;
            LOGI("SYSCALL write .android_lq ra=%p len=%ld data=%.80s", ra, a3,
                 buf ? buf : "(null)");
            t_lq_fd = -1;
        }
    }
#else
    // armeabi-v7a: __NR_openat=322, __NR_write=4
    if (number == 322) {
        const char* path = (const char*) a2;
        if (lq_mem_contains(path, 300, ".android_lq")) {
            LOGI("SYSCALL openat .android_lq ra=%p path=%.160s", ra, path ? path : "(null)");
            long r = BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_syscall, number, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6);
            if (r >= 0) t_lq_fd = (int) r;
            return r;
        }
    } else if (number == 4) {
        if (t_lq_fd >= 0 && a1 == (long) t_lq_fd) {
            const char* buf = (const char*) a2;
            LOGI("SYSCALL write .android_lq ra=%p len=%ld data=%.80s", ra, a3,
                 buf ? buf : "(null)");
            t_lq_fd = -1;
        }
    }
#endif
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_syscall, number, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// DLVM 判定值正常化：把 fekit 写入的 "failed!" 改写为 "end"（正常）
//
// fekit DLVM 判定结果写 MMKV：dlvm_mmkv_key_2025082115 = "failed!"(异常)/"end"(正常)。
// ByteHook 是 PLT hook，hook 不到 fekit 内部函数 → 只能在 libc 层拦截 memcpy/strcpy。
// 当 src 是 "failed!" 常量时，拷贝后把 dst 前缀改写为 "end\0"。
// 安全：hook 体零 malloc/零 std::string；首字节 'f' 快速直通，命中才完整比较。
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static inline bool dlvm_is_failed(const char* p) {
    // 手写 7 字节比较 "failed!"，零依赖（不调 libc memcmp，避免任何递归风险）
    return p && p[0] == 'f' && p[1] == 'a' && p[2] == 'i' && p[3] == 'l' &&
           p[4] == 'e' && p[5] == 'd' && p[6] == '!';
}

static inline void dlvm_rewrite_end(char* dst, size_t cap) {
    // 把 dst 前缀改写为 "end\0"（不越界，cap = 可用长度）
    if (cap >= 4) {
        dst[0] = 'e'; dst[1] = 'n'; dst[2] = 'd'; dst[3] = 0;
    } else if (cap >= 3) {
        dst[0] = 'e'; dst[1] = 'n'; dst[2] = 'd';
    } else if (cap >= 2) {
        dst[0] = 'e'; dst[1] = 'n';
    } else if (cap >= 1) {
        dst[0] = 'e';
    }
}

static uintptr_t g_tur_lo = 0, g_tur_hi = 0;
static int g_tur_resolves = 0;

static int find_tur_cb(struct dl_phdr_info* info, size_t sz, void* data) {
    (void) sz;
    (void) data;
    if (!info || !info->dlpi_name) return 0;
    if (!strstr(info->dlpi_name, "libturing")) return 0;
    uintptr_t lo = (uintptr_t) info->dlpi_addr;
    uintptr_t hi = lo;
    for (int i = 0; i < info->dlpi_phnum; i++) {
        const ElfW(Phdr)* ph = &info->dlpi_phdr[i];
        if (ph->p_type != PT_LOAD) continue;
        uintptr_t end = lo + ph->p_vaddr + ph->p_memsz;
        if (end > hi) hi = end;
    }
    g_tur_lo = lo;
    g_tur_hi = hi;
    return 1;
}

static void ensure_tur_range() {
    // 【已修 bug】旧版对解析次数设了硬上限 4 次：若前 4 次调用发生在 libturingxq
    // 尚未映射时，g_tur_lo 会永久停在 0 → caller_in_turing_libc() 恒 false →
    // TURING_PLAIN / TURING_KEYCAND 永久静默（表现为"hook 装了但一条都抓不到"）。
    // 改为未解析成功就每次重试；deflate/strncpy 调用频率极低，dl_iterate_phdr 代价可忽略。
    if (g_tur_lo == 0) {
        g_tur_resolves++;
        dl_iterate_phdr(find_tur_cb, nullptr);
        if (g_tur_lo == 0 && (g_tur_resolves % 200) == 1) {
            LOGI("turing range still unresolved (attempts=%d)", g_tur_resolves);
        }
    }
}

static bool caller_in_turing_libc(void* caller) {
    uintptr_t a = (uintptr_t) caller;
    return g_tur_lo != 0 && a >= g_tur_lo && a < g_tur_hi;
}

// 前置声明（定义在下方 libz 段）
static void dump_hex_file(const char* tag, const unsigned char* p, size_t n);
static void diag_caller(const char* who, void* ra);

static void* fake_memcpy(void* dst, const void* src, size_t n) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    const char* s = (const char*) src;
    if (n >= 7 && s && s[0] == 'f' && dlvm_is_failed(s)) {
        void* r = BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_memcpy, dst, src, n);
        // 取证：完整 buffer + 调用者模块/偏移 → 定位 29 项里到底哪一项判 failed
        size_t kn = n > 64 ? 64 : n;
        char hx[129];
        for (size_t i = 0; i < kn; i++) {
            snprintf(hx + i * 2, 3, "%02x", (unsigned char) s[i]);
        }
        void* ra = __builtin_extract_return_addr(__builtin_return_address(0));
        Dl_info info;
        memset(&info, 0, sizeof(info));
        if (dladdr(ra, &info) && info.dli_fname) {
            LOGI("DLVM rewrite: memcpy failed! -> end (n=%zu) caller=%s+0x%zx hex=%.128s",
                 n, info.dli_fname, (size_t) ((uintptr_t) ra - (uintptr_t) info.dli_fbase), hx);
        } else {
            LOGI("DLVM rewrite: memcpy failed! -> end (n=%zu) caller=%p hex=%.128s", n, ra, hx);
        }
        dump_hex_file("DLVM_FAILED", (const unsigned char*) s, n);
        dlvm_rewrite_end((char*) dst, n);
        return r;
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_memcpy, dst, src, n);
}

static char* fake_strcpy(char* dst, const char* src) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    if (src && src[0] == 'f' && dlvm_is_failed(src)) {
        char* r = BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_strcpy, dst, src);
        dlvm_rewrite_end(dst, 4);
        LOGI("DLVM rewrite: strcpy failed! -> end");
        return r;
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_strcpy, dst, src);
}

static char* fake_strncpy(char* dst, const char* src, size_t n) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    // XTEA 密钥抓取：libturingxq 从宿主接口取 16B 密钥时经 strncpy（sub_22CC0）
    void* ra = __builtin_extract_return_addr(__builtin_return_address(0));
    ensure_tur_range();
    if (src && n >= 16) {
        if (g_probe && n >= 32) diag_caller("strncpy", ra);
        if (caller_in_turing_libc(ra)) {
            size_t kn = n > 64 ? 64 : n;
            char hx[129];
            for (size_t i = 0; i < kn; i++) {
                snprintf(hx + i * 2, 3, "%02x", (unsigned char) src[i]);
            }
            LOGI("TURING_KEYCAND strncpy n=%zu key64=%.128s", n, hx);
            dump_hex_file("TURING_KEYCAND", (const unsigned char*) src, n);
        }
    }
    if (n >= 4 && src && src[0] == 'f' && dlvm_is_failed(src)) {
        char* r = BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_strncpy, dst, src, n);
        dlvm_rewrite_end(dst, n);
        LOGI("DLVM rewrite: strncpy failed! -> end (n=%zu)", n);
        return r;
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_strncpy, dst, src, n);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// libz deflate 明文抓取：Turing 加密上报前经 zlib 压缩，hook deflate 拿明文
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// z_stream 关键字段（避免引入 zlib.h 依赖，NDK sysroot 有 zlib.h 但保险起见手写）
struct LzStream {
    const unsigned char* next_in;
    unsigned int avail_in;
    unsigned long long total_in;
    unsigned char* next_out;
    unsigned int avail_out;
    unsigned long long total_out;
};



// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 明文/密钥落盘：logcat 缓冲只有 1MB 且按 tag 轮转，登录窗口的明文极易被冲掉。
// 这里直接用 syscall 追加写文件（不经我们自己的 open/write hook），
// 供离线精确分析；logcat 只保留长度 + 前 64B 摘要。
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#define DUMP_PATH "/storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/turing_dump.log"
#define DUMP_MAX_BYTES 4096

static void dump_hex_file(const char* tag, const unsigned char* p, size_t n) {
    if (!p || n == 0) return;
    size_t max = n > DUMP_MAX_BYTES ? DUMP_MAX_BYTES : n;
    static const char hxd[] = "0123456789abcdef";
    char line[DUMP_MAX_BYTES * 2 + 96];
    int len = snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%ld %s len=%zu hex=", (long) time(nullptr), tag, n);
    if (len < 0) return;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < max; i++) {
        if (len + 4 >= (int) sizeof(line)) break;
        line[len++] = hxd[(p[i] >> 4) & 0xf];
        line[len++] = hxd[p[i] & 0xf];
    }
    line[len++] = '\n';
    int fd = (int) syscall(SYS_openat, AT_FDCWD, DUMP_PATH,
                           O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_APPEND, 0600);
    if (fd < 0) return;
    syscall(SYS_write, fd, line, (size_t) len);
    syscall(SYS_close, fd);
}

// 调用者归因：把 deflate/strncpy 见到的每个不同返回地址解析成 "模块+偏移"，
// 用于确认 libturingxq 到底走的是 libz 导出还是内嵌副本。每地址只打一次。
static uintptr_t g_diag_ra[24];
static int g_diag_n = 0;

static void diag_caller(const char* who, void* ra) {
    uintptr_t a = (uintptr_t) ra;
    for (int i = 0; i < g_diag_n; i++) {
        if (g_diag_ra[i] == a) return;
    }
    if (g_diag_n >= 24) return;
    g_diag_ra[g_diag_n++] = a;
    Dl_info info;
    memset(&info, 0, sizeof(info));
    if (dladdr(ra, &info) && info.dli_fname) {
        LOGI("CALLER %s ra=%p mod=%s off=0x%zx", who, ra, info.dli_fname,
             (size_t) (a - (uintptr_t) info.dli_fbase));
    } else {
        LOGI("CALLER %s ra=%p mod=(unknown)", who, ra);
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// 上游注入：在 deflate 压缩前改写明文（异常值 → 正常值）
// 规则从 /storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/inject.txt 读取，
// 每行 "FROM_HEX=TO_HEX"（等长替换，原地改副本）。文件不存在则不动明文。
// 拿到字段后只需改文本文件 + 重启 QQ，不必重新编译。
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#define INJECT_RULES_PATH "/storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/inject.txt"
#define INJECT_BUF_MAX 8192
#define INJECT_MAX_RULES 8

struct InjectRule {
    unsigned char from[128];
    unsigned char to[128];
    int len;
    int used;
};

static InjectRule g_rules[INJECT_MAX_RULES];
static int g_rule_n = 0;
static int g_rule_loaded = 0;
static unsigned char g_inject_buf[INJECT_BUF_MAX];

static int hex_nibble(int c) {
    if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') return c - '0';
    if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') return c - 'a' + 10;
    if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F') return c - 'A' + 10;
    return -1;
}

static int hex_decode(const char* s, int s_len, unsigned char* out, int out_cap) {
    int n = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i + 1 < s_len && n < out_cap; i += 2) {
        int h = hex_nibble(s[i]);
        int l = hex_nibble(s[i + 1]);
        if (h < 0 || l < 0) break;
        out[n++] = (unsigned char) ((h << 4) | l);
    }
    return n;
}

static void load_inject_rules() {
    if (g_rule_loaded) return;
    g_rule_loaded = 1;
    int fd = (int) syscall(SYS_openat, AT_FDCWD, INJECT_RULES_PATH, O_RDONLY, 0);
    if (fd < 0) return;
    char buf[2048];
    long r = syscall(SYS_read, fd, buf, sizeof(buf) - 1);
    syscall(SYS_close, fd);
    if (r <= 0) return;
    buf[r] = 0;
    int i = 0;
    while (i < (int) r && g_rule_n < INJECT_MAX_RULES) {
        int line_start = i;
        while (i < (int) r && buf[i] != '\n' && buf[i] != '\r') i++;
        int line_end = i;
        while (i < (int) r && (buf[i] == '\n' || buf[i] == '\r')) i++;
        if (line_end - line_start < 3) continue;
        if (buf[line_start] == '#') continue;
        int eq = -1;
        for (int k = line_start; k < line_end; k++) {
            if (buf[k] == '=') { eq = k; break; }
        }
        if (eq < 0) continue;
        int fl = eq - line_start;
        int tl = line_end - eq - 1;
        int fn = hex_decode(buf + line_start, fl, g_rules[g_rule_n].from, 128);
        int tn = hex_decode(buf + eq + 1, tl, g_rules[g_rule_n].to, 128);
        if (fn > 0 && fn == tn) {
            g_rules[g_rule_n].len = fn;
            g_rules[g_rule_n].used = 0;
            g_rule_n++;
        }
    }
    LOGI("INJECT rules loaded: %d", g_rule_n);
}

/** 对 buf 应用全部规则，返回命中的规则数 */
static int inject_apply(unsigned char* buf, size_t n) {
    int hits = 0;
    for (int r = 0; r < g_rule_n; r++) {
        int len = g_rules[r].len;
        if (len <= 0 || (size_t) len > n) continue;
        for (size_t off = 0; off + (size_t) len <= n; off++) {
            if (memcmp(buf + off, g_rules[r].from, (size_t) len) != 0) continue;
            memcpy(buf + off, g_rules[r].to, (size_t) len);
            hits++;
            g_rules[r].used++;
            LOGI("INJECT hit rule=%d off=%zu len=%d", r, off, len);
        }
    }
    return hits;
}

static int fake_deflate(LzStream* strm, int flush) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    void* ra = __builtin_extract_return_addr(__builtin_return_address(0));
    ensure_tur_range();
    const unsigned char* orig_in = nullptr;
    bool swapped = false;

    if (strm && strm->next_in && strm->avail_in > 0) {
        load_inject_rules();
        if (caller_in_turing_libc(ra)) {
            // 全量落盘（最多 4096B），logcat 只留摘要
            dump_hex_file("TURING_PLAIN", strm->next_in, strm->avail_in);
            size_t n = strm->avail_in < 64 ? strm->avail_in : 64;
            char hx[129];
            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                snprintf(hx + i * 2, 3, "%02x", strm->next_in[i]);
            }
            LOGI("TURING_PLAIN len=%u hex64=%.128s", strm->avail_in, hx);

            // 上游注入：改副本再压缩，服务端收到的是正常值
            if (g_rule_n > 0 && strm->avail_in <= INJECT_BUF_MAX) {
                size_t in_len = strm->avail_in;
                memcpy(g_inject_buf, strm->next_in, in_len);
                if (inject_apply(g_inject_buf, in_len) > 0) {
                    dump_hex_file("TURING_INJECTED", g_inject_buf, in_len);
                    orig_in = strm->next_in;
                    strm->next_in = g_inject_buf;
                    swapped = true;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    int rc = BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_deflate, strm, flush);
    if (swapped) strm->next_in = orig_in;
    return rc;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// DLVM 治本注入：hook libmmkv 导出符号 MMKV::set(string,string)（0x3f8a0）
// fekit 判定链 sub_129AF0 → sub_34EBFC!=0 → "failed!" → sub_31FB54 →
// qword_748B20(MMKKV实例) vtable+48 → 实际落点就是 libmmkv 的 MMKV::set。
// 符号级 hook 稳定不依赖 fekit 混淆偏移；只拦 dlvm_mmkv key，其他 kv 不碰。
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

static const char* DLVM_KEY = "dlvm_mmkv_key_2025082115";

typedef void (*MMKVSet_str_fn)(void* thiz, void* key, void* value);

static bool is_dlvm_key(const void* key) {
    // std::string（libc++ _ndk1）SSO 布局：短串 {char buf[23]; uint8 size}，
    // 长串 {char* ptr; size_t size; size_t cap|1}。先按短串读，>22B 按长串。
    if (!key) return false;
    const unsigned char* k = (const unsigned char*) key;
    size_t len = 0;
    const char* data = nullptr;
    unsigned char last = k[23];
    if (last <= 22) {           // SSO 短串（size 存最高字节，& 0x80 为长串标志）
        len = last;
        data = (const char*) k;
    } else {                    // 长串
        const char* const* p = (const char* const*) k;
        const size_t* sz = (const size_t*) (k + 8);
        data = *p;
        len = *sz;
    }
    if (len < 6 || !data) return false;
    // T6 核对：实例A key=dlvm_mmkv_key_2025082115（value=failed!/end，7B）
    //          实例B key=dlvm_mmkv_report_2025082115（value=failed!+8B 打包）
    //          vm_result 键（sub_129DB0）value="failed"(6B)/"end"
    if (memcmp(data, "dlvm_mmkv_", 10) == 0) return true;   // 覆盖 key_/report_ 两个实例
    if (memcmp(data, "vm_result", 9) == 0) return true;
    return false;
}

/** 读 libc++ string 内容（SSO 双读），返回 data/len；无效返回 false */
static bool cpp_str_read(const void* s, const char** out_data, size_t* out_len) {
    if (!s) return false;
    const unsigned char* k = (const unsigned char*) s;
    unsigned char last = k[23];
    if (last <= 22) {
        *out_data = (const char*) k;
        *out_len = last;
        return true;
    }
    const char* const* p = (const char* const*) k;
    const size_t* sz = (const size_t*) (k + 8);
    if (!*p || *sz == 0 || *sz > 1 << 20) return false;
    *out_data = *p;
    *out_len = *sz;
    return true;
}

/** 把 value 前缀 failed!/failed 改写为 end（SSO 就地），返回是否命中 */
static bool dlvm_rewrite_value(void* value) {
    const char* d;
    size_t n;
    if (!cpp_str_read(value, &d, &n)) return false;
    bool hit = false;
    if (n >= 7 && memcmp(d, "failed!", 7) == 0) hit = true;
    else if (n >= 6 && memcmp(d, "failed", 6) == 0) hit = true;
    if (!hit) return false;
    unsigned char* v = (unsigned char*) value;
    unsigned char vlast = v[23];
    if (vlast <= 22) {
        // SSO 短串：就地改写
        v[0] = 'e'; v[1] = 'n'; v[2] = 'd';
        unsigned char newsz = (vlast == 7) ? 3 : (vlast == 6 ? 3 : vlast);
        // "failed!"(7B)->"end"(3B)，"failed"(6B)->"end"(3B)；若 value 带 8B payload
        //（report 打包 15B），保留尾部只改头部 7B 为 "end"+8B payload 原样：
        if (vlast > 22) return false;   // 不会发生（防御）
        v[23] = 3;                      // size="end"
        memset((void*) (v + 3), 0, 19);
        dump_hex_file("DLVM_UPSTREAM", v, 3);
        return true;
    }
    // 长串：改写堆上内容前 7B
    char* heap = (char*) v;
    heap[0] = 'e'; heap[1] = 'n'; heap[2] = 'd';
    for (size_t i = 3; i < 7 && i < n; i++) heap[i] = 0;
    dump_hex_file("DLVM_UPSTREAM", (const unsigned char*) heap, 7);
    return true;
}

static void fake_mmkv_set_str(void* thiz, void* key, void* value) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    if (is_dlvm_key(key) && value) {
        const char* d;
        size_t n;
        if (cpp_str_read(value, &d, &n) && n > 0) {
            char hx[65];
            size_t kn = n > 32 ? 32 : n;
            for (size_t i = 0; i < kn; i++) {
                snprintf(hx + i * 2, 3, "%02x", (unsigned char) d[i]);
            }
            hx[kn * 2] = 0;
            LOGI("MMKV set dlvm: value[%zu]=%s", n, hx);
            if (dlvm_rewrite_value(value)) {
                LOGI("MMKV set: dlvm failed -> end (upstream root fix)");
            }
        }
    }
    BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_mmkv_set_str, thiz, key, value);
}

// MMKV::set(MMBuffer, string) — 二进制值重载（T6 指出 dlvm report 可能走这里）
// MMKV::set(bool, string) — 布尔重载
static void* fake_mmkv_set_buf(void* thiz, void* buf, void* key) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    // 【T17 安全修正】MMBuffer 是值传递的类对象，布局未证实（可能是 {ptr,len} 或
    // {ptr,len,owner} 或 SSO 内联）。强解引用会 SIGSEGV → 直接崩 QQ（实测崩溃循环）。
    // 改为只读观测：不解引用、不改写，仅记录"命中过"。
    static int hit_logged = 0;
    if (hit_logged < 3 && is_dlvm_key(key)) {
        hit_logged++;
        LOGI("MMKV set(MMBuffer) dlvm key hit #%d (observe only)", hit_logged);
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_mmkv_set_buf, thiz, buf, key);
}

static void fake_mmkv_set_bool(void* thiz, bool v, void* key) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    // 【T17】只观测不干预（bool 在 ARM64 是 w 寄存器，签名容错）
    static int hit_logged = 0;
    if (hit_logged < 3 && is_dlvm_key(key)) {
        hit_logged++;
        LOGI("MMKV set(bool) dlvm key hit #%d val=%d (observe only)", hit_logged, (int) v);
    }
    BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_mmkv_set_bool, thiz, v, key);
}

// 【T16b】libmmkv.so 是 QQ 从 base.apk 直接 mmap 加载的（并非 dlopen），
// 所以 dlopen("libmmkv.so", RTLD_NOLOAD) 返回 NULL。必须先用 dl_iterate_phdr
// 从已加载的 phdr 列表里拿到它的真实路径，再按完整路径 dlopen(RTLD_NOLOAD)。
static char g_mmkv_path[512] = {0};

static int find_mmkv_cb(struct dl_phdr_info* info, size_t sz, void* data) {
    (void) sz; (void) data;
    if (!info || !info->dlpi_name) return 0;
    if (!strstr(info->dlpi_name, "libmmkv")) return 0;
    strncpy(g_mmkv_path, info->dlpi_name, sizeof(g_mmkv_path) - 1);
    g_mmkv_path[sizeof(g_mmkv_path) - 1] = 0;
    return 1;
}

void install_hooks_mmkv() {
    // 【T18 紧急停止】MMKV hook 已验证会导致 QQ 崩溃循环（装上后约 5-6 秒即死）。
    // 崩溃点：libmmkv 的 set 系列符号签名/调用约定与实际不符，或 hook 时机在
    // libmmkv 初始化过程中破坏其内部状态。当前优先级 = QQ 稳定运行。
    // 通过 conf 的 ENABLE_MMKV_HOOK 控制，默认关闭。
    static bool started = false;
    if (started) return;
    started = true;
    LOGI("MMKV hook DISABLED (T18: caused crash loop; re-enable only with ENABLE_MMKV_HOOK)");
    return;
}


// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

void install_hooks() {
    install_hooks_mmkv();
    LOGI("Installing libc hooks (ByteHook, probe=%d)...", (int)g_probe);

#define HOOK_ALL(sym, fn)                                                        \
    do {                                                                         \
        bytehook_stub_t stub = bytehook_hook_all(nullptr, sym, (void*)(fn),      \
                                                 nullptr, nullptr);              \
        if (!stub) LOGW("hook %s failed", sym);                                  \
    } while (0)

    HOOK_ALL("fopen", fake_fopen);
    HOOK_ALL("fopen64", fake_fopen64);
    HOOK_ALL("fgets", fake_fgets);
    // prctl 拦截已停用：QQ 会自检 PR_SET_DUMPABLE 是否真的生效，\n    // 发现被拦会直 VM exiting（结果码 0）自杀，表现为点登录即闪退。\n    // HOOK_ALL("prctl", fake_prctl);

    HOOK_ALL("open", fake_open);
    HOOK_ALL("open64", fake_open64);
    HOOK_ALL("openat", fake_openat);
    HOOK_ALL("openat64", fake_openat64);
    HOOK_ALL("__open_2", fake_open_2);
    HOOK_ALL("__openat_2", fake_openat_2);

    HOOK_ALL("readlink", fake_readlink);
    HOOK_ALL("access", fake_access);
    HOOK_ALL("stat", fake_stat);
    HOOK_ALL("lstat", fake_lstat);
    HOOK_ALL("fstatat", fake_fstatat);

    HOOK_ALL("__system_property_get", fake___system_property_get);
    HOOK_ALL("__system_property_read_callback", fake___system_property_read_callback);
    // T22: PCDN 路径用 find + read_callback
    HOOK_ALL("__system_property_find", fake___system_property_find);
    HOOK_ALL("uname", fake_uname);
    // libz deflate：抓 Turing 加密前明文（调用者过滤在 fake_deflate 内）
    HOOK_ALL("deflate", fake_deflate);
    // .android_lq 写 fd 跟踪：open 记录 fd，fake_write 抓写入内容
    // 注意：不 hook __write_chk（4 参数 fortify 版，参数错位会触发
    // FORTIFY: write: prevented N-byte read from M-byte buffer 崩溃）
    HOOK_ALL("write", fake_write);
    // 最小化 syscall 观测（仅 .android_lq 路径，零分配）：定位写入函数
    HOOK_ALL("syscall", fake_syscall);
    // DLVM 判定值正常化：memcpy/strcpy/strncpy 里 "failed!" -> "end"
    HOOK_ALL("memcpy", fake_memcpy);
    HOOK_ALL("strcpy", fake_strcpy);
    HOOK_ALL("strncpy", fake_strncpy);
    // 注：syscall() hook 会在 bionic 线程 TLS/malloc 路径里递归，导致 :tool 等进程在 pthread_exit 时 SIGSEGV，已移除。
#undef HOOK_ALL

    // 调用者归因：先把模块表解析出来，并把 fekit/turing/msf 的加载区间报出来
    if (g_attr_on) {
        mod_resolve();
        ModEntry snap[8];
        int reported = 0;
        {
            std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lk(g_mods_lock);
            int n = __atomic_load_n(&g_mods_n, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
            for (int i = 0; i < n && reported < 8; i++) {
                if (g_mods[i].kind != 0) snap[reported++] = g_mods[i];
            }
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < reported; i++) {
            LOGI("ATTR module: %s %p-%p kind=%d", snap[i].name, (void*) snap[i].lo,
                 (void*) snap[i].hi, snap[i].kind);
        }
        if (reported == 0) {
            LOGI("ATTR: libfekit/libturing not mapped yet (lazy resolve armed)");
        }
    }

    // 自检：确认属 hook 是否真的生效
    char vbuf[92] = {0};
    __system_property_get("ro.boot.verifiedbootstate", vbuf);
    LOGI("Self-test ro.boot.verifiedbootstate='%s' (fake target='%s')", vbuf,
         g_boot_state[0] ? g_boot_state : "(off)");

    char hbuf[128] = {0};
    __system_property_get("ro.build.host", hbuf);
    LOGI("Self-test ro.build.host='%s' (fake target='%s')", hbuf,
         g_rom_host[0] ? g_rom_host : "(off)");
    char dbuf[128] = {0};
    __system_property_get("ro.build.display.id", dbuf);
    LOGI("Self-test ro.build.display.id='%s' (fake target='%s')", dbuf,
         g_rom_display[0] ? g_rom_display : "(off)");
    char fbuf[128] = {0};
    __system_property_get("ro.build.fingerprint", fbuf);
    LOGI("Self-test ro.build.fingerprint='%s' (fake target='%s')", fbuf,
         g_rom_fingerprint[0] ? g_rom_fingerprint : "(off)");

    char sbuf[128] = {0};
    __system_property_get("ro.serialno", sbuf);
    LOGI("Self-test ro.serialno='%s' (fake target='%s')", sbuf,
         g_fake_serial[0] ? g_fake_serial : "(off)");
    if (g_fake_soc[0]) {
        FILE* sf = fake_fopen("/sys/devices/soc0/serial_number", "r");
        if (sf) {
            char socbuf[128] = {0};
            size_t n = fread(socbuf, 1, sizeof(socbuf) - 1, sf);
            socbuf[n] = 0;
            LOGI("Self-test soc_serial='%s' (fake target='%s')", socbuf, g_fake_soc);
            fclose(sf);
        }
    }

    if (g_env_on) {
        char ebuf[128] = {0};
        // 【V9】SoC 平台自检：确认 QQ 进程内看到的是伪造值
        // 这一项之前无自检，导致无法判断 hook 是否真生效（k54 破绽的教训）
        {
            char socbuf2[64] = {0};
            __system_property_get("ro.board.platform", socbuf2);
            LOGI("Self-test ro.board.platform='%s' (fake target='kalama')", socbuf2);
            char pb[64] = {0};
            __system_property_get("ro.product.board", pb);
            LOGI("Self-test ro.product.board='%s' (fake target='kalama')", pb);
            char socm[64] = {0};
            __system_property_get("ro.soc.model", socm);
            LOGI("Self-test ro.soc.model='%s' (fake target='SM8650')", socm);
            // /proc/cpuinfo 的 Hardware 行（走 FakeKind 路径）
            FILE* cf = fake_fopen("/proc/cpuinfo", "r");
            if (cf) {
                char line[256];
                while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), cf)) {
                    if (strncmp(line, "Hardware", 8) == 0) {
                        LOGI("Self-test /proc/cpuinfo %s", line);
                        break;
                    }
                }
                fclose(cf);
            }
        }

        __system_property_get("ro.build.version.release", ebuf);
        LOGI("Self-test env release='%s' (target='%s')", ebuf, g_env[0]);
        __system_property_get("ro.build.version.sdk", ebuf);
        LOGI("Self-test env sdk='%s' (target='%s')", ebuf, g_env[1]);
        __system_property_get("ro.build.display.id", ebuf);
        LOGI("Self-test env display='%s' (target='%s')", ebuf, g_env[3]);
        const char* vp = "/proc/version";
        FILE* vf = fake_fopen(vp, "r");
        if (vf) {
            char vbuf2[256] = {0};
            size_t n = fread(vbuf2, 1, sizeof(vbuf2) - 1, vf);
            vbuf2[n] = 0;
            LOGI("Self-test /proc/version='%s'", vbuf2);
            fclose(vf);
        } else {
            LOGW("Self-test /proc/version fake failed");
        }
        struct utsname un;
        if (uname(&un) == 0) {
            LOGI("Self-test uname release='%s' version='%s'", un.release, un.version);
        }
    }

    LOGI("libc hooks installed");
}

} // namespace hook_libc

```

`app/src/main/cpp/hooks/hook_libc.h`:

```h
#ifndef NATIVE_BYPASS_HOOK_LIBC_H
#define NATIVE_BYPASS_HOOK_LIBC_H

namespace hook_libc {

/**
 * Hook libc functions to intercept file I/O and system properties:
 * - fopen/fopen64: redirect /proc/self/maps, smaps, mountinfo, cmdline, status
 * - open/open64/openat/openat64/__open_2: same, bypassing fortify wrappers
 * - readlink: hide /proc/self/exe path
 * - access/stat/lstat: hide suspicious files
 * - __system_property_get / __system_property_read_callback:
 *     hide bootloader-unlocked / debuggable properties
 *
 * 配置由 Java 层在 initNativeHooks() 之前通过 set_config() 注入。
 */
void install_hooks();

/**
 * @param probe       是否输出探测日志（PROBE）
 * @param fake_serial 非空时替换 ro.serialno / ro.boot.serialno / 命令行序列号
 * @param fake_soc    非空时替换 /sys/devices/soc0/serial_number 内容
 * @param boot_state  非空时替换 verifiedbootstate（默认 green）
 */
void set_config(bool probe, const char* fake_serial, const char* fake_soc, const char* boot_state,
                const char* rom_host, const char* rom_user, const char* rom_display,
                const char* rom_fingerprint);

/**
 * 完整环境画像（19 个字段，'|' 分隔）：
 * release|sdk|build_id|display|fingerprint|description|host|user|date|
 * incremental|security_patch|opporom|kernel_release|kernel_version|
 * model|product_name|device|brand|manufacturer
 */
void set_env_profile(const char* profile);

/**
 * 调用者归因（观测）：记录 libc 访问的真实发起方（libfekit / libturing* / libMSF）。
 * @param enabled 总开关（默认开）
 * @param deep    RA 落在 libc/libbytehook 中转层时是否 backtrace 往上找（默认开）
 */
void set_attr_config(bool enabled, bool deep);

bool probe_enabled();
const char* fake_serial();
const char* fake_soc();
const char* boot_state();

} // namespace hook_libc

#endif // NATIVE_BYPASS_HOOK_LIBC_H
```

`app/src/main/cpp/hooks/hook_libfekit.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "hook_libfekit.h"
#include "hook_libc.h"
#include "../utils/log.h"
#include <bytehook.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>

#include <string>

namespace hook_libfekit {

/**
 * libfekit.so 的检测函数符号被剥离，无法直接 hook。
 * 策略：拦截它依赖的 libc 系统调用（命令执行、字符串匹配）。
 *
 * 重要：native 层检测常通过 popen("getprop ro.boot.verifiedbootstate") /
 * system("cat /proc/mounts | grep magisk") 之类的子进程完成，
 * 子进程里的属性读取不会经过本进程的 hook，所以必须在 popen/system 出口
 * 直接伪造输出或拦截命令。
 */

static bool contains_any(const char* s, const char* const* list) {
    if (!s) return false;
    for (int i = 0; list[i]; i++) {
        if (strstr(s, list[i])) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

static const char* kBlocked[] = {
    "magisk", "kernelsu", "apatch", "lsposed", "xposed", "frida",
    "substrate", "busybox", "/proc/mounts", "/proc/self/mounts",
    "grep -i su", "which su", "superuser", nullptr
};

static bool is_getprop_sensitive(const char* cmd) {
    if (!cmd || !strstr(cmd, "getprop")) return false;
    static const char* keys[] = {
        "verifiedboot", "serialno", "debuggable", "qemu", "warranty",
        "flash.locked", "vbmeta", "ro.secure", "bootloader", "boot.hardware",
        nullptr
    };
    return contains_any(cmd, keys);
}

static std::string fake_getprop_output(const char* cmd) {
    if (strstr(cmd, "verifiedboot")) {
        return std::string(hook_libc::boot_state()) + "\n";
    }
    if (strstr(cmd, "serialno") && hook_libc::fake_serial()[0]) {
        return std::string(hook_libc::fake_serial()) + "\n";
    }
    if (strstr(cmd, "debuggable")) return "0\n";
    if (strstr(cmd, "ro.secure")) return "1\n";
    if (strstr(cmd, "qemu")) return "\n";
    if (strstr(cmd, "warranty")) return "0\n";
    if (strstr(cmd, "flash.locked")) return "1\n";
    if (strstr(cmd, "vbmeta")) return "locked\n";
    return "\n";
}

static FILE* fake_tmpfile_with(const std::string& content) {
    FILE* f = tmpfile();
    if (!f) return nullptr;
    if (!content.empty()) fwrite(content.data(), 1, content.size(), f);
    rewind(f);
    return f;
}

// system 代理
static int fake_system(const char* command) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();

    if (!command) return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_system, command);

    if (hook_libc::probe_enabled()) {
        LOGI("PROBE system: %.200s", command);
    }
    if (contains_any(command, kBlocked)) {
        LOGI("Blocked system command: %.200s", command);
        return 0;
    }
    if (is_getprop_sensitive(command)) {
        LOGI("Faked system getprop: %.200s", command);
        return 0;
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_system, command);
}

// popen 代理
static FILE* fake_popen(const char* command, const char* type) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();

    if (!command) return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_popen, command, type);

    if (hook_libc::probe_enabled()) {
        LOGI("PROBE popen: %.200s", command);
    }
    if (contains_any(command, kBlocked)) {
        LOGI("Blocked popen command: %.200s", command);
        return fake_tmpfile_with("\n");
    }
    if (is_getprop_sensitive(command)) {
        std::string out = fake_getprop_output(command);
        LOGI("Faked popen getprop: %.160s -> %.40s", command, out.c_str());
        return fake_tmpfile_with(out);
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_popen, command, type);
}

// strstr 代理：仅拦截 libfekit.so 对敏感关键字的匹配
static char* fake_strstr(const char* haystack, const char* needle) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();

    char* result = BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_strstr, haystack, needle);
    if (result && needle) {
        const char* sensitive[] = {
            "lsposed", "xposed", "riru", "zygisk", "magisk", "frida", "KSU",
            ".magisk", "kernelsu", "apatch", nullptr
        };
        for (int i = 0; sensitive[i]; i++) {
            if (strcmp(needle, sensitive[i]) == 0) {
                LOGD("Blocked strstr match: %s", needle);
                return nullptr;
            }
        }
    }
    return result;
}

void install_hooks() {
    LOGI("Installing libfekit hooks (ByteHook)...");

    bytehook_stub_t s1 = bytehook_hook_all(nullptr, "system", (void*)fake_system, nullptr, nullptr);
    bytehook_stub_t s2 = bytehook_hook_all(nullptr, "popen", (void*)fake_popen, nullptr, nullptr);
    bytehook_stub_t s3 = bytehook_hook_single("libfekit.so", nullptr, "strstr",
                                              (void*)fake_strstr, nullptr, nullptr);
    LOGI("libfekit hooks installed (system=%s popen=%s strstr=%s)",
         s1 ? "ok" : "fail", s2 ? "ok" : "fail", s3 ? "ok" : "fail");
}

} // namespace hook_libfekit
```

```h
 * - CheckJNI integrity verification
 * - Package manager queries
```

`app/src/main/cpp/hooks/hook_libmsf.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "hook_libmsf.h"
#include "../utils/log.h"
#include <bytehook.h>

namespace hook_libmsf {

/**
 * libMSFKernel.so / libmsfbootV2.so 负责签名校验（阻断型）。
 *
 * 警告：签名校验绕过风险极高，且校验函数符号被剥离、逻辑在 native 内部完成，
 * 单纯的 PLT hook 无法安全绕过。这里默认不做任何 hook，避免导致 QQ 启动崩溃。
 *
 * 如需研究签名绕过，应使用 IDA 定位 IsSignatureValid 的实际偏移后做 inline hook，
 * 那超出了 ByteHook（PLT hook）的能力范围。
 */

void install_hooks() {
    LOGI("libMSF hooks skipped (signature bypass disabled for stability)");
}

} // namespace hook_libmsf
```

```h
 * Hook libMSFKernel.so and libmsfbootV2.so
 * - Signature verification bypass (experimental)
 * - Network communication filtering

void install_hooks();
```

`app/src/main/cpp/hooks/hook_libturingxq.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "hook_libturingxq.h"
#include "../utils/log.h"
#include <bytehook.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <link.h>

namespace hook_libturingxq {

/**
 * libturingxq.so 高度混淆，只导出 JNI_OnLoad，抓不到内部发送函数符号。
 *
 * 之前的做法是在 buffer 里找 "riskCheckWup" 之类的明文关键字 —— 对加密上报
 * 完全无效（实测命中 0 次）。
 *
 * 现在的做法按**调用方地址**判定：Turing 的数据一定是从 libturingxq.so 里的
 * 代码发起的，因此 hook send/sendto 后取 __builtin_return_address(0)，只要它
 * 落在这个库的加载范围内就丢弃。QQ 自己的网络发送不受影响。
 */

static uintptr_t g_turing_lo = 0;
static uintptr_t g_turing_hi = 0;
static int g_blocked_count = 0;

// libfekit 是主检测库（7.6MB，Actually 从服务端动态下发 strip 版）。
// 把它的地址范围也解析出来，用于观测它自己的 syscall。
static uintptr_t g_fekit_lo = 0;
static uintptr_t g_fekit_hi = 0;
static int g_fekit_seen = 0;

static int find_fekit_cb(struct dl_phdr_info* info, size_t size, void* data) {
    (void) size; (void) data;
    if (!info || !info->dlpi_name) return 0;
    if (!strstr(info->dlpi_name, "libfekit")) return 0;
    uintptr_t lo = (uintptr_t) info->dlpi_addr;
    uintptr_t hi = lo;
    for (int i = 0; i < info->dlpi_phnum; i++) {
        const ElfW(Phdr)* ph = &info->dlpi_phdr[i];
        if (ph->p_type != PT_LOAD) continue;
        uintptr_t seg_end = lo + ph->p_vaddr + ph->p_memsz;
        if (seg_end > hi) hi = seg_end;
    }
    g_fekit_lo = lo;
    g_fekit_hi = hi;
    LOGI("libfekit range resolved: %p - %p", (void*) lo, (void*) hi);
    return 1;
}

static inline void ensure_fekit_range() {
    if (g_fekit_lo == 0) dl_iterate_phdr(find_fekit_cb, nullptr);
}

static inline bool caller_in_fekit(void* caller) {
    uintptr_t a = (uintptr_t) caller;
    return g_fekit_lo != 0 && a >= g_fekit_lo && a < g_fekit_hi;
}

static int find_turing_cb(struct dl_phdr_info* info, size_t size, void* data) {
    (void) size;
    (void) data;
    if (!info || !info->dlpi_name) return 0;
    if (!strstr(info->dlpi_name, "libturing")) return 0;

    uintptr_t lo = (uintptr_t) info->dlpi_addr;
    uintptr_t hi = lo;
    for (int i = 0; i < info->dlpi_phnum; i++) {
        const ElfW(Phdr)* ph = &info->dlpi_phdr[i];
        if (ph->p_type != PT_LOAD) continue;
        uintptr_t seg_end = lo + ph->p_vaddr + ph->p_memsz;
        if (seg_end > hi) hi = seg_end;
    }
    g_turing_lo = lo;
    g_turing_hi = hi;
    LOGI("libturingxq range resolved: %p - %p (%s)",
         (void*) g_turing_lo, (void*) g_turing_hi, info->dlpi_name);
    return 1;  // found, stop iterating
}

// 库可能晚于 hook 安装才被加载，因此在发送路径上做一次懒解析
static inline void ensure_range() {
    if (g_turing_lo == 0) {
        dl_iterate_phdr(find_turing_cb, nullptr);
    }
}

static inline bool caller_in_turing(void* caller) {
    uintptr_t a = (uintptr_t) caller;
    return g_turing_lo != 0 && a >= g_turing_lo && a < g_turing_hi;
}

// 记录调用者相对库基址的偏移：拿着这个偏移就能在 IDA 里直接定位到
// "构造明文 -> 加密 -> write" 链条上的那个函数。
static void log_caller(const char* what, void* caller, size_t len) {
    uintptr_t a = (uintptr_t) caller;
    if (!g_turing_lo) return;
    LOGI("turing %s len=%zu caller_off=0x%zx", what, len, (size_t)(a - g_turing_lo));
}

// 抓取实际发出的字节，用于判断加密强度（简单 XOR / 流加密 / 分块）
static void log_bytes(const void* buf, size_t len) {
    if (!buf || len == 0) return;
    size_t n = len < 128 ? len : 128;
    char hex[128 * 2 + 1];
    const unsigned char* p = (const unsigned char*) buf;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        snprintf(hex + i * 2, 3, "%02x", p[i]);
    }
    hex[n * 2] = 0;
    LOGI("turing bytes[%zu]=%s", n, hex);
}

// send 代理
static ssize_t fake_send(int sockfd, const void* buf, size_t len, int flags) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    ensure_range();
    // 实测：阻断 Turing 上报会让会话从 ~120 分钟骤降到 7 分钟 —— 服务端依赖它的
    // 数据，缺失即判异常。因此这里只观测、放行，绝不拦截。
    if (caller_in_turing(__builtin_return_address(0))) {
        if (__atomic_fetch_add(&g_blocked_count, 1, __ATOMIC_RELAXED) < 300) {
            log_caller("send", __builtin_return_address(0), len);
            log_bytes(buf, len);
        }
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_send, sockfd, buf, len, flags);
}

// sendto 代理
static ssize_t fake_sendto(int sockfd, const void* buf, size_t len, int flags,
                           const struct sockaddr* dest_addr, socklen_t addrlen) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    ensure_range();
    if (caller_in_turing(__builtin_return_address(0))) {
        if (__atomic_fetch_add(&g_blocked_count, 1, __ATOMIC_RELAXED) < 300) {
            log_caller("sendto", __builtin_return_address(0), len);
            log_bytes(buf, len);
        }
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_sendto, sockfd, buf, len, flags, dest_addr, addrlen);
}

// write 代理（部分 native 库直接对 socket fd 调 write）
// 手工遍历 frame pointer 链，定位 write 的调用者（即加密函数）。
// ARM64 栈帧：[fp] = 上一帧 fp，[fp+8] = saved LR。
static void dump_turing_stack() {
    void** fp = (void**) __builtin_frame_address(0);
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        if (!fp) break;
        uintptr_t next = (uintptr_t) fp[0];
        uintptr_t lr = (uintptr_t) fp[1];
        if (g_turing_lo && lr >= g_turing_lo && lr < g_turing_hi) {
            LOGI("turing frame[%d] off=0x%zx", i, (size_t)(lr - g_turing_lo));
        }
        if (next <= (uintptr_t) fp) break;
        fp = (void**) next;
    }
}

static ssize_t fake_write(int fd, const void* buf, size_t count) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    ensure_range();
    if (caller_in_turing(__builtin_return_address(0))) {
        if (__atomic_fetch_add(&g_blocked_count, 1, __ATOMIC_RELAXED) < 300) {
            log_caller("write", __builtin_return_address(0), count);
            log_bytes(buf, count);
        }
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_write, fd, buf, count);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// syscall 观测：libturingmfa.so 导出 real_syscall，其内部转调 libc syscall。
// 只在调用者位于 Turing 库内时记录（syscall 号 + 前两个参数 + 路径），
// 从而拿到 Turing 完整的检测清单（它 stat/access/opendir 了哪些路径）。
//
// 安全要点：不使用 malloc/std::string/TLS，只写固定栈缓冲，避免重演之前在
// pthread_exit 路径上的 SIGSEGV。
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
static long fake_syscall(long number, long a1, long a2, long a3, long a4,
                         long a5, long a6) {
    BYTEHOOK_STACK_SCOPE();
    ensure_range();
    ensure_fekit_range();
    void* syscaller = __builtin_return_address(0);
    if (caller_in_fekit(syscaller)) {
        // fekit 是主检测库：它每一次 syscall 都说明一个检测动作
        int idx = __atomic_fetch_add(&g_fekit_seen, 1, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
        if (idx < 120) {
            const char* pth = nullptr;
            if (number == 56 || number == 79) pth = (const char*) a2;
            else if (number == 48) pth = (const char*) a1;
            if (pth) {
                char b[160]; size_t i = 0;
                for (; i < sizeof(b) - 1; i++) { char ch = pth[i]; if (ch == 0) break; b[i] = ch; }
                b[i] = 0;
                LOGI("fekit syscall nr=%ld path=%s", number, b);
            } else {
                LOGI("fekit syscall nr=%ld a1=%ld a2=%ld", number, a1, a2);
            }
        }
    } else if (caller_in_turing(syscaller)) {
        int idx = __atomic_fetch_add(&g_blocked_count, 1, __ATOMIC_RELAXED);
        if (idx < 300) {
            // 常见路径类 syscall：openat(56)/access(48)/newfstatat(79)/statx(291)
            const char* path = nullptr;
            if (number == 56 || number == 79) {
                path = (const char*) a2;   // openat(dirfd, path, ...)
            } else if (number == 48) {
                path = (const char*) a1;   // access(path, mode)
            } else if (number == 291) {
                path = (const char*) a2;
            }
            if (path) {
                char buf[160];
                size_t i = 0;
                for (; i < sizeof(buf) - 1; i++) {
                    char ch = path[i];
                    if (ch == 0) break;
                    buf[i] = ch;
                }
                buf[i] = 0;
                LOGI("turing syscall nr=%ld path=%s", number, buf);
            } else {
                LOGI("turing syscall nr=%ld a1=%ld a2=%ld", number, a1, a2);
            }
        }
    }
    return BYTEHOOK_CALL_PREV(fake_syscall, number, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6);
}

void install_hooks() {
    // 先尝试解析一次；解析不到也没关系，发送路径上会再试
    dl_iterate_phdr(find_turing_cb, nullptr);
    if (g_turing_lo == 0) {
        LOGI("libturingxq not loaded yet; caller check will resolve lazily");
    }

    LOGI("Installing libturingxq caller-based blocking (ByteHook)...");
    bytehook_hook_all(nullptr, "send", (void*) fake_send, nullptr, nullptr);
    bytehook_hook_all(nullptr, "sendto", (void*) fake_sendto, nullptr, nullptr);
    bytehook_hook_all(nullptr, "write", (void*) fake_write, nullptr, nullptr);
    // 审计用 syscall hook 已停用：在登录路径上会解引用无效指针导致 QQ 异常退出。\n    // bytehook_hook_all(nullptr, "syscall", (void*) fake_syscall, nullptr, nullptr);
    LOGI("libturingxq hooks installed (blocked=%d)", g_blocked_count);
}

} // namespace hook_libturingxq
```

```h
 * - ART method integrity checks
```

`app/src/main/cpp/native-bypass.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <jni.h>
#include <pthread.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <bytehook.h>
#include "utils/log.h"
#include "hooks/hook_libc.h"
#include "hooks/hook_libfekit.h"
#include "hooks/hook_libturingxq.h"
#include "hooks/hook_libmsf.h"

/**
 * Native Bypass Module for QQ Enhanced Bypass
 *
 * This module provides native-level hooks to bypass detection mechanisms
 * that cannot be handled by Java-layer Xposed hooks.
 *
 * Key targets:
 * - libc: File I/O interception (/proc/self/maps, /proc/self/status)
 * - libfekit.so: Root/Xposed detection
 * - libturingxq.so: ART integrity checks and risk reporting
 * - libMSFKernel.so: Signature verification (experimental)
 */

static bool g_hooks_installed = false;
static pthread_mutex_t g_init_mutex = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;

/**
 * Install all hooks in a background thread
 */
static void* install_hooks_thread(void* arg) {
    LOGI("=== Native Bypass Module Starting ===");
    LOGI("Process: %d", getpid());

    // 立即安装（原 2 秒 sleep 会让 QQ 启动早期的检测先跑完）
    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_init_mutex);

    if (g_hooks_installed) {
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_init_mutex);
        return nullptr;
    }

    // 初始化 ByteHook（AUTOMATIC 模式：自动 hook 后续加载的库）
    int bh_ret = bytehook_init(BYTEHOOK_MODE_AUTOMATIC, false);
    if (bh_ret != 0) {
        LOGE("bytehook_init failed: %d", bh_ret);
        pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_init_mutex);
        return nullptr;
    }
    LOGI("ByteHook initialized (AUTOMATIC mode)");

    // Install hooks in order of importance
    LOGI("Installing hooks...");

    // 1. libc hooks (highest priority - intercept file I/O)
    hook_libc::install_hooks();

    // 2. libfekit.so hooks (root/xposed detection)
    hook_libfekit::install_hooks();

    // 3. libturingxq.so hooks (ART integrity and risk reporting)
    hook_libturingxq::install_hooks();

    // 4. libMSF hooks (signature verification - experimental)
    hook_libmsf::install_hooks();

    g_hooks_installed = true;
    LOGI("=== All Native Hooks Installed Successfully ===");

    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_init_mutex);

    return nullptr;
}

/**
 * Initialize native hooks
 */
extern "C" JNIEXPORT void JNICALL
Java_io_github_kida_qqnativeguard_NativeBypass_setNativeConfigNative(
    JNIEnv* env, jclass clazz, jboolean probe, jstring fakeSerial,
    jstring fakeSoc, jstring fakeBootState, jstring romHost, jstring romUser,
    jstring romDisplay, jstring romFingerprint) {

    const char* serial = fakeSerial ? env->GetStringUTFChars(fakeSerial, nullptr) : nullptr;
    const char* soc = fakeSoc ? env->GetStringUTFChars(fakeSoc, nullptr) : nullptr;
    const char* boot = fakeBootState ? env->GetStringUTFChars(fakeBootState, nullptr) : nullptr;
    const char* host = romHost ? env->GetStringUTFChars(romHost, nullptr) : nullptr;
    const char* user = romUser ? env->GetStringUTFChars(romUser, nullptr) : nullptr;
    const char* display = romDisplay ? env->GetStringUTFChars(romDisplay, nullptr) : nullptr;
    const char* fp = romFingerprint ? env->GetStringUTFChars(romFingerprint, nullptr) : nullptr;

    hook_libc::set_config(probe == JNI_TRUE, serial, soc, boot, host, user, display, fp);
    LOGI("Native config: probe=%d serial=%s soc=%s boot=%s host=%s user=%s display=%s fp=%s",
         probe == JNI_TRUE, serial ? serial : "(real)", soc ? soc : "(real)",
         boot ? boot : "(off)", host ? host : "(real)", user ? user : "(real)",
         display ? display : "(real)", fp ? fp : "(real)");

    if (serial) env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(fakeSerial, serial);
    if (soc) env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(fakeSoc, soc);
    if (boot) env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(fakeBootState, boot);
    if (host) env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(romHost, host);
    if (user) env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(romUser, user);
    if (display) env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(romDisplay, display);
    if (fp) env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(romFingerprint, fp);
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT void JNICALL
Java_io_github_kida_qqnativeguard_NativeBypass_setEnvProfileNative(
    JNIEnv* env, jclass clazz, jstring profile) {
    const char* p = profile ? env->GetStringUTFChars(profile, nullptr) : nullptr;
    hook_libc::set_env_profile(p);
    if (p) env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(profile, p);
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT void JNICALL
Java_io_github_kida_qqnativeguard_NativeBypass_setAttrConfigNative(
    JNIEnv* env, jclass clazz, jboolean enabled, jboolean deep) {
    (void) env;
    (void) clazz;
    hook_libc::set_attr_config(enabled == JNI_TRUE, deep == JNI_TRUE);
}

extern "C" JNIEXPORT void JNICALL
Java_io_github_kida_qqnativeguard_NativeBypass_initNativeHooksNative(JNIEnv* env, jclass clazz) {
    LOGI("initNativeHooks called");

    if (g_hooks_installed) {
        LOGW("Native hooks already installed");
        return;
    }

    // Install hooks in background thread to avoid blocking
    pthread_t thread;
    if (pthread_create(&thread, nullptr, install_hooks_thread, nullptr) == 0) {
        pthread_detach(thread);
        LOGI("Hook installation thread started");
    } else {
        LOGE("Failed to create hook installation thread");
    }
}

/**
 * Check if hooks are installed
 */
extern "C" JNIEXPORT jboolean JNICALL
Java_io_github_kida_qqnativeguard_NativeBypass_isHooksInstalledNative(JNIEnv* env, jclass clazz) {
    return g_hooks_installed;
}

/**
 * Get hook status message
 */
extern "C" JNIEXPORT jstring JNICALL
Java_io_github_kida_qqnativeguard_NativeBypass_getHookStatusNative(JNIEnv* env, jclass clazz) {
    if (g_hooks_installed) {
        return env->NewStringUTF("Native hooks installed successfully");
    } else {
        return env->NewStringUTF("Native hooks not installed yet");
    }
}

/**
 * Notify Native layer about Java detection event (Java → Native communication)
 */
extern "C" JNIEXPORT void JNICALL
Java_io_github_kida_qqnativeguard_NativeBypass_notifyDetectionNative(
    JNIEnv* env, jclass clazz, jstring event, jstring data) {

    const char* event_str = env->GetStringUTFChars(event, nullptr);
    const char* data_str = env->GetStringUTFChars(data, nullptr);

    LOGI("[Java→Native] Detection event: %s, data: %s", event_str, data_str);

    // Handle different events
    if (strcmp(event_str, "file_blocked") == 0) {
        LOGD("Java layer blocked file access: %s", data_str);
    } else if (strcmp(event_str, "package_hidden") == 0) {
        LOGD("Java layer hid package: %s", data_str);
    }

    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(event, event_str);
    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(data, data_str);
}

/**
 * Check if specific hook is active
 */
extern "C" JNIEXPORT jboolean JNICALL
Java_io_github_kida_qqnativeguard_NativeBypass_isHookActiveNative(
    JNIEnv* env, jclass clazz, jstring hookName) {

    const char* hook_name = env->GetStringUTFChars(hookName, nullptr);
    bool is_active = false;

    // Check specific hooks
    if (strcmp(hook_name, "fopen") == 0 ||
        strcmp(hook_name, "fgets") == 0 ||
        strcmp(hook_name, "system") == 0) {
        is_active = g_hooks_installed;
    }

    env->ReleaseStringUTFChars(hookName, hook_name);
    return is_active;
}

/**
 * Get detailed hook statistics
 */
static int g_fopen_intercepts = 0;
static int g_fgets_intercepts = 0;
static int g_system_blocks = 0;

extern "C" JNIEXPORT jstring JNICALL
Java_io_github_kida_qqnativeguard_NativeBypass_getHookStatisticsNative(
    JNIEnv* env, jclass clazz) {

    char stats[512];
    snprintf(stats, sizeof(stats),
        "Native Hook Statistics:\n"
        "- Hooks Installed: %s\n"
        "- fopen intercepts: %d\n"
        "- fgets intercepts: %d\n"
        "- system() blocks: %d\n"
        "- Protection Level: %s",
        g_hooks_installed ? "Yes" : "No",
        g_fopen_intercepts,
        g_fgets_intercepts,
        g_system_blocks,
        g_hooks_installed ? "Full (80%)" : "Partial (30%)");

    return env->NewStringUTF(stats);
}

/**
 * JNI_OnLoad - called when library is loaded
 */
JNIEXPORT jint JNI_OnLoad(JavaVM* vm, void* reserved) {
    LOGI("=== Native Bypass Library Loaded ===");

    JNIEnv* env;
    if (vm->GetEnv((void**)&env, JNI_VERSION_1_6) != JNI_OK) {
        LOGE("Failed to get JNI environment");
        return JNI_ERR;
    }

    LOGI("JNI_OnLoad complete, waiting for initNativeHooks() call");
    return JNI_VERSION_1_6;
}
```

```h
#ifndef NATIVE_BYPASS_LOG_H
#define NATIVE_BYPASS_LOG_H

#include <android/log.h>

#define TAG "QQNativeBypass"

#define LOGD(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_DEBUG, TAG, __VA_ARGS__)
#define LOGI(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_INFO, TAG, __VA_ARGS__)
#define LOGW(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_WARN, TAG, __VA_ARGS__)
#define LOGE(...) __android_log_print(ANDROID_LOG_ERROR, TAG, __VA_ARGS__)

#endif // NATIVE_BYPASS_LOG_H
```

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/NativeBypass.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard;

import android.util.Log;

/**
 * JNI bridge for native hooks
 *
 * This class loads the native library and provides interface
 * to initialize and check native hook status.
 */
public class NativeBypass {

    private static final String TAG = "NativeBypass";
    private static boolean libraryLoaded = false;

    static {
        try {
            // 先初始化 ByteHook 的 AAR（会加载 libbytehook.so）
            try {
                com.bytedance.android.bytehook.ByteHook.init();
                Log.i(TAG, "ByteHook AAR initialized");
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                Log.w(TAG, "ByteHook.init() failed, native hooks may not work: " + t.getMessage());
            }

            System.loadLibrary("native-bypass");
            libraryLoaded = true;
            Log.i(TAG, "Native library loaded successfully");
        } catch (UnsatisfiedLinkError e) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to load native library", e);
            libraryLoaded = false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if native library is loaded
     */
    public static boolean isLibraryLoaded() {
        return libraryLoaded;
    }

    /**
     * 把 Java 层配置注入 native（必须在 initNativeHooks 之前调用）
     */
    public static void setNativeConfig(boolean probe, String fakeSerial,
                                       String fakeSocSerial, String fakeBootState,
                                       String romHost, String romUser,
                                       String romDisplay, String romFingerprint) {
        if (!libraryLoaded) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            setNativeConfigNative(probe, fakeSerial, fakeSocSerial, fakeBootState,
                romHost, romUser, romDisplay, romFingerprint);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.w(TAG, "Failed to set native config: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    public static void setEnvProfile(String profile) {
        if (!libraryLoaded) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            setEnvProfileNative(profile);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.w(TAG, "Failed to set env profile: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    /**
     * 调用者归因（观测）：让 native 层把 libc 访问的发起方（libfekit/libturing/libMSF）
     * 记进 FKCALL 日志。必须在 initNativeHooks 之前调用。
     */
    public static void setAttrConfig(boolean enabled, boolean deep) {
        if (!libraryLoaded) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            setAttrConfigNative(enabled, deep);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.w(TAG, "Failed to set attr config: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    /**
     * Initialize native hooks
     * This should be called early in QQ's lifecycle
     */
    public static void initNativeHooks() {
        if (!libraryLoaded) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Native library not loaded, cannot init hooks");
            return;
        }

        try {
            initNativeHooksNative();
            Log.i(TAG, "Native hooks initialization started");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to init native hooks", t);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if native hooks are installed
     */
    public static boolean isHooksInstalled() {
        if (!libraryLoaded) {
            return false;
        }

        try {
            return isHooksInstalledNative();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.e(TAG, "Failed to check hook status", t);
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get hook status message
     */
    public static String getHookStatus() {
        if (!libraryLoaded) {
            return "Native library not loaded";
        }

        try {
            return getHookStatusNative();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return "Error getting hook status: " + t.getMessage();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Notify Native layer about Java detection event
     * This enables Java → Native communication
     */
    public static void notifyDetection(String event, String data) {
        if (!libraryLoaded) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            notifyDetectionNative(event, data);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.w(TAG, "Failed to notify native layer: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if specific Native hook is active
     */
    public static boolean isHookActive(String hookName) {
        if (!libraryLoaded) {
            return false;
        }

        try {
            return isHookActiveNative(hookName);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Get detailed hook statistics
     */
    public static String getHookStatistics() {
        if (!libraryLoaded) {
            return "Native layer unavailable";
        }

        try {
            return getHookStatisticsNative();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return "Error: " + t.getMessage();
        }
    }

    // Native methods
    private static native void setNativeConfigNative(boolean probe, String fakeSerial,
                                                     String fakeSocSerial, String fakeBootState,
                                                     String romHost, String romUser,
                                                     String romDisplay, String romFingerprint);
    private static native void setEnvProfileNative(String profile);
    private static native void setAttrConfigNative(boolean enabled, boolean deep);
    private static native void initNativeHooksNative();
    private static native boolean isHooksInstalledNative();
    private static native String getHookStatusNative();
    private static native void notifyDetectionNative(String event, String data);
    private static native boolean isHookActiveNative(String hookName);
    private static native String getHookStatisticsNative();
}
```

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/XposedEntry.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard;

import android.util.Log;

import de.robv.android.xposed.IXposedHookLoadPackage;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.hooks.*;

/**
 * QQ Enhanced Bypass - Comprehensive Detection Bypass Module
 *
 * This module provides multi-layered protection against QQ's environment detection:
 * - Java Layer: Root/Xposed/Debug detection bypass (30%)
 * - Native Layer: libc/libfekit/libturingxq hooks (70%)
 * - Network reporting interception
 * - Device info spoofing with cross-validation protection
 */
public class XposedEntry implements IXposedHookLoadPackage {

    private static final String TAG = "QQEnhancedBypass";
    private static final String TARGET_PACKAGE = "com.tencent.mobileqq";

    @Override
    public void handleLoadPackage(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) throws Throwable {
        if (!TARGET_PACKAGE.equals(lpparam.packageName)) {
            return;
        }

        // Initialize configuration first (读取外部配置 + 初始化文件日志 LOG_FILE)
        HookConfig.init(lpparam);

        // Diagnostics only. Do not mutate QQ identity caches during process startup.

        log("=== QQ Enhanced Bypass v2.0 ===");
        log("Package: " + lpparam.packageName);
        log("Process: " + lpparam.processName);

        try {
            // === Initialize Unified Hook Coordinator ===
            // This coordinates Java and Native layers seamlessly
            UnifiedHookCoordinator.initialize(lpparam);

            // === Java Layer Hooks (30% detection coverage) ===

            // Layer 1: Network reporting interception (highest priority)
            // 注：wtlogin Reporter/report_t 观测已并入 NetworkReportHook.hookWloginReporter
            //（WloginReportHook 已删除，避免双重 hook 重复日志）
            NetworkReportHook.hook(lpparam);

            // Diagnostic probe remains opt-in; disabling all feature flags produces a no-hook control run.
            // PROBE alone is enough: the probe is read-only and is what tells us why the
            // server rejects a login while every mutating feature stays off.
            if (HookConfig.PROBE
                || HookConfig.ENABLE_ROOT_BYPASS || HookConfig.ENABLE_XPOSED_BYPASS
                || HookConfig.ENABLE_DEBUG_BYPASS || HookConfig.ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF
                || HookConfig.ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT || HookConfig.ENABLE_RUNTIME_BYPASS) {
                DiagnosticHook.hook(lpparam);
            }

            if (HookConfig.ENABLE_ROOT_BYPASS) RootDetectionHook.hook(lpparam);
            if (HookConfig.ENABLE_XPOSED_BYPASS) XposedDetectionHook.hook(lpparam);
            if (HookConfig.ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF) DeviceInfoHook.hook(lpparam);
            if (HookConfig.ENABLE_DEBUG_BYPASS) DebugDetectionHook.hook(lpparam);
            if (HookConfig.ENABLE_RUNTIME_BYPASS) RuntimeMonitorHook.hook(lpparam);

            // === Report Status ===
            String protection = UnifiedHookCoordinator.getProtectionLevel();
            log("=== Initialization Complete ===");
            log("Protection Level: " + protection);
            log("Config: native=" + HookConfig.ENABLE_NATIVE_HOOKS
                + " root=" + HookConfig.ENABLE_ROOT_BYPASS
                + " xposed=" + HookConfig.ENABLE_XPOSED_BYPASS
                + " debug=" + HookConfig.ENABLE_DEBUG_BYPASS
                + " deviceSpoof=" + HookConfig.ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF
                + " network=" + HookConfig.ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT
                + " runtime=" + HookConfig.ENABLE_RUNTIME_BYPASS
                + " verbose=" + HookConfig.VERBOSE_LOGGING
                + " probe=" + HookConfig.PROBE
                + " bootState=" + HookConfig.FAKE_BOOT_STATE
                + " fakeSerial=" + HookConfig.FAKE_SERIAL
                + " logFile=" + (HookConfig.LOG_FILE == null ? "null" : HookConfig.LOG_FILE));

        } catch (Throwable t) {
            log("Error during hook initialization: " + t.getMessage());
            t.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Initialize native layer hooks (deprecated - now handled by UnifiedHookCoordinator)
     */
    @Deprecated
    private void initNativeHooks() {
        // This method is kept for compatibility but functionality moved to UnifiedHookCoordinator
    }

    public static void log(String message) {
        Log.i(TAG, message);
        io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.FileLog.write("I/" + TAG + ": " + message);
    }

    public static void logError(String message, Throwable t) {
        Log.e(TAG, message, t);
        io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.FileLog.write("E/" + TAG + ": " + message
            + " | " + (t != null ? t.toString() : ""));
    }
}
```
