`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/config/HookConfig.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config;

import android.util.Log;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.Properties;

import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.FileLog;

/**
 * Central configuration for hook modules
 *
 * 外部配置（Root 设备）：
 * QQ 进程启动时会读取以下路径的纯文本配置文件（按顺序，第一个存在且可读的生效），
 * 用文件中的值覆盖下面编译期默认值；文件中未写的项保持默认值。
 *
 *   1. /storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/qqbypass.conf
 *      （推荐：adb 可直接 push/修改，QQ 对自己的 Android/data 目录有读写权限）
 *   2. /data/local/tmp/qqbypass.conf   （主路径，adb + su 最方便写入）
 *   3. /data/adb/qqbypass.conf         （备选路径，Magisk 数据目录）
 *
 * 配置文件格式（每行一个 key=value，# 开头为注释）：
 *
 *   # 功能开关
 *   ENABLE_NATIVE_HOOKS=true
 *   ENABLE_ROOT_BYPASS=true
 *   ENABLE_XPOSED_BYPASS=true
 *   ENABLE_DEBUG_BYPASS=true
 *   ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF=true
 *   ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT=true
 *   ENABLE_RUNTIME_BYPASS=true
 *
 *   # 详细日志
 *   VERBOSE_LOGGING=false
 *
 *   # 设备信息伪造（留空 = 使用真实设备信息）
 *   FAKE_IMEI=
 *   FAKE_ANDROID_ID=
 *   FAKE_SERIAL=
 *
 * 修改配置文件后需重启 QQ 才生效（与 LSPosed 模块开关一致）。
 */
public class HookConfig {

    public static XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam loadPackageParam;
    public static ClassLoader classLoader;

    // Observation-first defaults: do not alter device identity or intercept app/system behavior.
    public static boolean ENABLE_NATIVE_HOOKS = false;
    public static boolean ENABLE_ROOT_BYPASS = false;
    public static boolean ENABLE_XPOSED_BYPASS = false;
    public static boolean ENABLE_DEBUG_BYPASS = false;
    public static boolean ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF = false;
    public static boolean ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT = false;
    /** T15：观测模式——挂 hook 但永不拦截（shouldBlock 恒 false）。用于"行为正常化但仍取证" */
    public static boolean NETWORK_OBSERVE_ONLY = false;
    public static boolean ENABLE_RUNTIME_BYPASS = false;

    // Verbose logging
    public static boolean VERBOSE_LOGGING = false;

    // Fake device info (used when spoofing is enabled)
    public static String FAKE_IMEI = null; // null = use real device info
    public static String FAKE_ANDROID_ID = null;
    public static String FAKE_SERIAL = null;
    // /sys/devices/soc0/serial_number（高通 SoC 序列号，应用可读，换机不变 → 常被用作设备指纹）
    public static String FAKE_SOC_SERIAL = null;
    // 开机完整性状态：设备已解锁 BL，真实值为 orange；伪造为 green
    public static String FAKE_BOOT_STATE = null;

    // 探测日志：输出 QQ 实际读取的敏感属性/文件（排查风控触发点用）
    public static boolean PROBE = false;

    /**
     * 调用者归因（观测）：把 libc 访问的真实发起方（libfekit/libturing/libMSF）写进
     * FKCALL 日志，用来反推 fekit 字节码 VM 到底读了哪些路径。
     * ATTR_PROBE 默认开；ATTR_DEEP 控制是否对 libc 中转层做 backtrace。
     */
    public static boolean ATTR_PROBE = false;
    public static boolean ATTR_DEEP = false;

    // Never clear QIMEI/device identity state automatically. Keep this key only for
    // backwards-compatible parsing; the resetter is intentionally not invoked.
    public static String RESET_QIMEI = null;

    // ROM 身份归一化：FEKit 的 DeviceTokenV3 会原样上报 ro.build.host/user/display.id/fingerprint；
    // 移植 ROM 的 ReVork/Sabrina Carpenter/CN01 是很明显的“非官方系统”特征。
    // 留空 = 不修改。
    public static String ROM_BUILD_HOST = null;
    public static String ROM_BUILD_USER = null;
    public static String ROM_BUILD_DISPLAY = null;
    public static String ROM_BUILD_FINGERPRINT = null;

    // 直接伪造 Qimei SDK 返回给 QQ 的 qimei16/qimei36（服务端如果按 QIMEI 识别设备，
    // 换一个值就等于换设备）。留空 = 使用真实值。
    public static String FAKE_QIMEI36 = null;
    // OPPO OAID（com.heytap.openid 返回的匿名设备 ID）
    public static String FAKE_OAID = null;

    // Environment overrides are opt-in; default leaves platform values untouched.
    public static boolean ENV_SPOOF = false;
    public static String ENV_RELEASE = "14";
    public static String ENV_SDK = "34";
    public static String ENV_BUILD_ID = "UKQ1.230924.001";
    public static String ENV_DISPLAY = "LE2125_14.0.0.730(EX01)";
    public static String ENV_FINGERPRINT =
        "OPPO/LE2125/OnePlus9Pro:14/UKQ1.230924.001/1727136000:user/release-keys";
    public static String ENV_DESCRIPTION =
        "qssi-user 14 UKQ1.230924.001 1727136000 release-keys";
    public static String ENV_HOST = "build";
    public static String ENV_USER = "builder";
    public static String ENV_DATE = "Tue Sep 24 00:00:00 CST 2024";
    public static String ENV_INCREMENTAL = "1727136000";
    public static String ENV_SECURITY_PATCH = "2024-09-05";
    public static String ENV_OPPOROM = "V14.0.0";
    public static String ENV_KERNEL_RELEASE = "5.4.254-qgki-g9e38a9fabb4d";
    public static String ENV_KERNEL_VERSION = "#1 SMP PREEMPT Tue Sep 24 00:00:00 CST 2024";
    public static String ENV_AAID = "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-abcd-ef1234567890";
    // 机型画像（换机型：LE2123 -> LE2125，同硬件同 SoC）
    public static String ENV_MODEL = "LE2125";
    public static String ENV_PRODUCT_NAME = "OnePlus9Pro";
    public static String ENV_DEVICE = "OnePlus9Pro";
    public static String ENV_BRAND = "OnePlus";
    public static String ENV_MANUFACTURER = "OnePlus";

    // 日志写文件（null/空 = 只打 logcat，不写文件）
    // 例：/data/user/0/com.tencent.mobileqq/qqbypass.log（QQ 可写、root 可读）
    public static String LOG_FILE = null;

    private static final String TAG = "QQEnhancedBypass";

    /** 外部配置文件候选路径：第一个存在且可读的文件生效 */
    private static final String[] EXTERNAL_CONFIG_PATHS = {
        "/storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/qqbypass.conf",
        "/data/local/tmp/qqbypass.conf",
        "/data/adb/qqbypass.conf"
    };

    public static void init(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        loadPackageParam = lpparam;
        classLoader = lpparam.classLoader;
        loadExternalConfig();
        // 读取完外部配置后再初始化文件日志（LOG_FILE 来自配置文件）
        FileLog.init(LOG_FILE);
    }

    /**
     * 读取外部配置文件，覆盖编译期默认值。
     * 只读一次，失败静默降级（保持默认配置），不影响模块启动。
     */
    private static void loadExternalConfig() {
        for (String path : EXTERNAL_CONFIG_PATHS) {
            File file = new File(path);
            if (!file.isFile()) {
                continue;
            }
            try (InputStream in = new FileInputStream(file)) {
                Properties props = new Properties();
                props.load(in);

                ENABLE_NATIVE_HOOKS = getBool(props, "ENABLE_NATIVE_HOOKS", ENABLE_NATIVE_HOOKS);
                ENABLE_ROOT_BYPASS = getBool(props, "ENABLE_ROOT_BYPASS", ENABLE_ROOT_BYPASS);
                ENABLE_XPOSED_BYPASS = getBool(props, "ENABLE_XPOSED_BYPASS", ENABLE_XPOSED_BYPASS);
                ENABLE_DEBUG_BYPASS = getBool(props, "ENABLE_DEBUG_BYPASS", ENABLE_DEBUG_BYPASS);
                ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF = getBool(props, "ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF", ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF);
                ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT = getBool(props, "ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT", ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT);
                NETWORK_OBSERVE_ONLY = getBool(props, "NETWORK_OBSERVE_ONLY", NETWORK_OBSERVE_ONLY);
                ENABLE_RUNTIME_BYPASS = getBool(props, "ENABLE_RUNTIME_BYPASS", ENABLE_RUNTIME_BYPASS);
                VERBOSE_LOGGING = getBool(props, "VERBOSE_LOGGING", VERBOSE_LOGGING);
                PROBE = getBool(props, "PROBE", PROBE);
                ATTR_PROBE = getBool(props, "ATTR_PROBE", ATTR_PROBE);
                ATTR_DEEP = getBool(props, "ATTR_DEEP", ATTR_DEEP);

                FAKE_IMEI = getString(props, "FAKE_IMEI", FAKE_IMEI);
                FAKE_ANDROID_ID = getString(props, "FAKE_ANDROID_ID", FAKE_ANDROID_ID);
                FAKE_SERIAL = getString(props, "FAKE_SERIAL", FAKE_SERIAL);
                FAKE_SOC_SERIAL = getString(props, "FAKE_SOC_SERIAL", FAKE_SOC_SERIAL);
                FAKE_BOOT_STATE = getString(props, "FAKE_BOOT_STATE", FAKE_BOOT_STATE);
                LOG_FILE = getString(props, "LOG_FILE", LOG_FILE);
                RESET_QIMEI = getString(props, "RESET_QIMEI", RESET_QIMEI);
                ROM_BUILD_HOST = getString(props, "ROM_BUILD_HOST", ROM_BUILD_HOST);
                ROM_BUILD_USER = getString(props, "ROM_BUILD_USER", ROM_BUILD_USER);
                ROM_BUILD_DISPLAY = getString(props, "ROM_BUILD_DISPLAY", ROM_BUILD_DISPLAY);
                ROM_BUILD_FINGERPRINT = getString(props, "ROM_BUILD_FINGERPRINT", ROM_BUILD_FINGERPRINT);
                FAKE_QIMEI36 = getString(props, "FAKE_QIMEI36", FAKE_QIMEI36);
                FAKE_OAID = getString(props, "FAKE_OAID", FAKE_OAID);
                ENV_SPOOF = getBool(props, "ENV_SPOOF", ENV_SPOOF);
                ENV_RELEASE = getString(props, "ENV_RELEASE", ENV_RELEASE);
                ENV_SDK = getString(props, "ENV_SDK", ENV_SDK);
                ENV_BUILD_ID = getString(props, "ENV_BUILD_ID", ENV_BUILD_ID);
                ENV_DISPLAY = getString(props, "ENV_DISPLAY", ENV_DISPLAY);
                ENV_FINGERPRINT = getString(props, "ENV_FINGERPRINT", ENV_FINGERPRINT);
                ENV_DESCRIPTION = getString(props, "ENV_DESCRIPTION", ENV_DESCRIPTION);
                ENV_HOST = getString(props, "ENV_HOST", ENV_HOST);
                ENV_USER = getString(props, "ENV_USER", ENV_USER);
                ENV_DATE = getString(props, "ENV_DATE", ENV_DATE);
                ENV_INCREMENTAL = getString(props, "ENV_INCREMENTAL", ENV_INCREMENTAL);
                ENV_SECURITY_PATCH = getString(props, "ENV_SECURITY_PATCH", ENV_SECURITY_PATCH);
                ENV_OPPOROM = getString(props, "ENV_OPPOROM", ENV_OPPOROM);
                ENV_KERNEL_RELEASE = getString(props, "ENV_KERNEL_RELEASE", ENV_KERNEL_RELEASE);
                ENV_KERNEL_VERSION = getString(props, "ENV_KERNEL_VERSION", ENV_KERNEL_VERSION);
                ENV_MODEL = getString(props, "ENV_MODEL", ENV_MODEL);
                ENV_PRODUCT_NAME = getString(props, "ENV_PRODUCT_NAME", ENV_PRODUCT_NAME);
                ENV_DEVICE = getString(props, "ENV_DEVICE", ENV_DEVICE);
                ENV_BRAND = getString(props, "ENV_BRAND", ENV_BRAND);
                ENV_MANUFACTURER = getString(props, "ENV_MANUFACTURER", ENV_MANUFACTURER);

                Log.i(TAG, "External config loaded from " + path);
                return; // 第一个可读的配置文件生效
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                Log.w(TAG, "Failed to read external config " + path + ": " + t.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 解析布尔值：true/1/on/yes 视为 true，其余（含乱写）一律 false。
     * 文件里没写该 key 时返回 fallback（编译期默认值）。
     */
    private static boolean getBool(Properties props, String key, boolean fallback) {
        String value = props.getProperty(key);
        if (value == null) {
            return fallback;
        }
        value = value.trim();
        return "true".equalsIgnoreCase(value)
            || "1".equals(value)
            || "on".equalsIgnoreCase(value)
            || "yes".equalsIgnoreCase(value);
    }

    /**
     * 解析字符串：文件里没写该 key 时返回 fallback；
     * 写了但为空（如 FAKE_IMEI=）时返回 null（= 使用真实设备信息）。
     */
    private static String getString(Properties props, String key, String fallback) {
        String value = props.getProperty(key);
        if (value == null) {
            return fallback;
        }
        value = value.trim();
        return value.isEmpty() ? null : value;
    }

    public static ClassLoader getClassLoader() {
        return classLoader;
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/hooks/DebugDetectionHook.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.hooks;

import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.XposedEntry;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.HookUtils;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.FileReader;

/**
 * Debug & Emulator Detection Bypass
 *
 * Handles multiple detection methods:
 * - TracerPid check in /proc/self/status (5+ implementations)
 * - ro.debuggable property
 * - ro.secure property
 * - ApplicationInfo.FLAG_DEBUGGABLE
 * - Emulator detection (ro.kernel.qemu, goldfish, etc.)
 */
public class DebugDetectionHook {

    public static void hook(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        if (!HookConfig.ENABLE_DEBUG_BYPASS) return;

        XposedEntry.log("Initializing Debug Detection Bypass");

        // Hook 1: TracerPid checks
        hookTracerPidChecks();

        // Hook 2: System properties
        hookDebugProperties();

        // Hook 3: ApplicationInfo flags
        hookApplicationInfo();

        // Hook 4: Emulator detection
        hookEmulatorDetection();

        // Hook 5: TuringFD debug checks
        hookTuringDebugChecks();
    }

    private static void hookTracerPidChecks() {
        // Hook file reading for /proc/self/status
        HookUtils.hookMethod(BufferedReader.class, "readLine",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    String line = (String) param.getResult();
                    if (line != null && line.startsWith("TracerPid:")) {
                        // Always return TracerPid: 0 (not being traced)
                        param.setResult("TracerPid:\t0");
                        XposedEntry.log("Spoofed TracerPid to 0");
                    }
                }
            });

        // Hook TuringFD specific TracerPid checks
        String[] turingClasses = {
            "com.tencent.tfd.sdk.wxa.Blueberry",
            "com.tencent.turingcam.oqKCa",
            "com.tencent.turingfd.sdk.xq.Pomegranate"
        };

        for (String className : turingClasses) {
            HookUtils.hookAllMethods(className, "b",
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        // These methods check TracerPid
                        param.setResult(0); // Return 0 (not debugging)
                    }
                });
        }

        // Hook Camera SDK debug check
        HookUtils.hookMethod("com.tencent.camerasdk.avreport.DeviceInfo", "getDebugInfo",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult("0"); // Not debugging
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookDebugProperties() {
        // Hook SystemProperties.get for debug-related properties
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.os.SystemProperties", "get",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length > 0) {
                        String key = (String) param.args[0];

                        if ("ro.debuggable".equals(key)) {
                            param.setResult("0");
                        } else if ("ro.secure".equals(key)) {
                            param.setResult("1");
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.os.SystemProperties", "getInt",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length > 0) {
                        String key = (String) param.args[0];

                        if ("ro.debuggable".equals(key)) {
                            param.setResult(0);
                        } else if ("ro.secure".equals(key)) {
                            param.setResult(1);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.os.SystemProperties", "getBoolean",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length > 0) {
                        String key = (String) param.args[0];

                        if ("ro.debuggable".equals(key)) {
                            param.setResult(false);
                        } else if ("ro.secure".equals(key)) {
                            param.setResult(true);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookApplicationInfo() {
        // Hook ApplicationInfo.flags to remove FLAG_DEBUGGABLE
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.app.ApplicationPackageManager", "getApplicationInfo",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    Object appInfo = param.getResult();
                    if (appInfo != null) {
                        android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo info = (android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo) appInfo;
                        // Remove FLAG_DEBUGGABLE flag (0x02)
                        info.flags &= ~android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo.FLAG_DEBUGGABLE;
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookEmulatorDetection() {
        // Hook emulator-related properties
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.os.SystemProperties", "get",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length > 0) {
                        String key = (String) param.args[0];

                        // Emulator detection properties
                        if ("ro.kernel.qemu".equals(key)) {
                            param.setResult("0");
                        } else if ("ro.product.device".equals(key)) {
                            String result = (String) param.getResult();
                            if ("goldfish".equals(result) || "vbox86".equals(result)) {
                                param.setResult("unknown"); // Spoof to non-emulator value
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

        // Hook specific emulator detection method
        HookUtils.hookMethod("com.tencent.mobileqq.qrscan.minicode.e", "e",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(false); // Not an emulator
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookTuringDebugChecks() {
        // Hook libfekit.so related Java bridges (if any)
        // Most TuringFD checks are in native code and cannot be hooked here

        // Block risk detection that includes debug status
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.turingfd.sdk.ams.au.TuringFDCoreInfo", "getIsDebug",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(false);
                }
            });
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/hooks/DeviceInfoHook.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.hooks;

import android.os.Build;
import android.telephony.TelephonyManager;

import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedBridge;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.XposedEntry;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.HookUtils;

/**
 * Device Info Hook - Consistency Protection
 *
 * Handles Pandora's multi-path cross-validation:
 * - Ensures consistent values across different API calls
 * - Prevents detection of single-API hooks
 * - Maintains device fingerprint consistency
 *
 * Pandora DeviceInfoMonitor uses 6+ different methods to get the same info:
 * - TelephonyManager.getImei()
 * - TelephonyManager.getDeviceId()
 * - Build.SERIAL (field)
 * - Build.getSerial() (method)
 * - SystemProperties.get()
 * - SubscriptionManager APIs
 */
public class DeviceInfoHook {

    private static String cachedImei = null;
    private static String cachedDeviceId = null;
    private static String cachedSerial = null;
    private static String cachedAndroidId = null;
    private static boolean qimeiFakeLogged = false;
    private static boolean osPropLogged = false;
    private static boolean httpAgentLogged = false;
    private static boolean msfQimeiLogged = false;
    private static boolean oaidLogged = false;

    public static void hook(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        if (!HookConfig.ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF) return;

        XposedEntry.log("Initializing Device Info Hook");

        // Initialize cached values from config or real device
        initCachedValues();

        // Hook all device ID retrieval methods with consistent values
        hookTelephonyManager();
        hookBuildSerial();
        hookSystemProperties();
        hookSystemGetProperty();
        hookAndroidId();
        applyBuildIdentity();
        hookQimeiIdentity();
        hookSettingsIds();
        hookNetworkIds();
        hookOaid();

        // Hook Pandora monitoring framework
        hookPandoraMonitor();
    }

    private static void initCachedValues() {
        // Use configured fake values or null to pass through real values
        cachedImei = HookConfig.FAKE_IMEI;
        cachedDeviceId = HookConfig.FAKE_IMEI; // Same as IMEI for consistency
        cachedSerial = HookConfig.FAKE_SERIAL;
        cachedAndroidId = HookConfig.FAKE_ANDROID_ID;
    }

    private static void hookTelephonyManager() {
        // Hook all IMEI/DeviceId retrieval methods
        String[] methods = {"getImei", "getDeviceId", "getMeid"};

        for (String method : methods) {
            HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.telephony.TelephonyManager", method,
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        // 用 before：QQ 没有 READ_PRIVILEGED_PHONE_STATE 时真实方法会直接抛
                        // SecurityException，after 回调根本不会执行；before 才能稳定伪造。
                        if (cachedImei != null) {
                            if (HookConfig.PROBE) {
                                XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] TelephonyManager." + method
                                    + " -> " + cachedImei + " (before)");
                            }
                            param.setResult(cachedImei);
                        }
                    }
                });
        }

        // Hook getSimSerialNumber
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.telephony.TelephonyManager", "getSimSerialNumber",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (cachedSerial != null) {
                        param.setResult(cachedSerial);
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookBuildSerial() {
        // Build.SERIAL 是 static final 字段，先尝试直接改写（QQ 直接读字段时也一致）
        if (cachedSerial != null) {
            try {
                de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers.setStaticObjectField(
                    Build.class, "SERIAL", cachedSerial);
                XposedEntry.log("Build.SERIAL set to " + cachedSerial);
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                XposedEntry.log("Failed to set Build.SERIAL: " + t.getMessage());
            }
        }

        // Hook Build.getSerial() method instead
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            HookUtils.hookMethod(Build.class, "getSerial",
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        if (cachedSerial != null) {
                            param.setResult(cachedSerial);
                        }
                    }
                });
        }
    }

    private static void hookSystemProperties() {
        // Hook SystemProperties.get() to return consistent values
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.os.SystemProperties", "get",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length > 0 && param.args[0] instanceof String) {
                        String key = (String) param.args[0];

                        if (HookConfig.PROBE && isProbeKey(key)) {
                            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] SystemProperties.get(" + key
                                + ") -> " + param.getResult());
                        }

                        String fake = fakeSystemProperty(key);
                        if (fake != null) {
                            param.setResult(fake);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static void applyBuildIdentity() {
        if (HookConfig.ENV_SPOOF) {
            // 【T12 教训】不伪造 HOST/USER（真机值 ReVork/Sabrina Carpenter 本身正常，
            // 伪造反而产生 abuild 这种真机不可能的值）。
            // 【V6 修正】但 FINGERPRINT 必须与 MODEL/DEVICE/DISPLAY 同步伪造 ——
            // 否则 "display=PGEM10 而 fingerprint=LE2123" 是更明显的矛盾。
            setField(Build.class, "DISPLAY", HookConfig.ENV_DISPLAY);
            setField(Build.class, "ID", HookConfig.ENV_BUILD_ID);
            setField(Build.class, "TAGS", "release-keys");
            setField(Build.class, "TYPE", "user");
            setField(Build.class, "MODEL", HookConfig.ENV_MODEL);
            setField(Build.class, "DEVICE", HookConfig.ENV_DEVICE);
            setField(Build.class, "PRODUCT", HookConfig.ENV_PRODUCT_NAME);
            setField(Build.class, "BRAND", HookConfig.ENV_BRAND);
            setField(Build.class, "MANUFACTURER", HookConfig.ENV_MANUFACTURER);
            // fingerprint 必须跟随机型一起换（V6）
            if (HookConfig.ENV_FINGERPRINT != null && !HookConfig.ENV_FINGERPRINT.isEmpty()) {
                setField(Build.class, "FINGERPRINT", HookConfig.ENV_FINGERPRINT);
            }
            setField(Build.class, "HARDWARE", "qcom");
            setField(Build.VERSION.class, "RELEASE", HookConfig.ENV_RELEASE);
            setIntField(Build.VERSION.class, "SDK_INT", parseInt(HookConfig.ENV_SDK, 34));
            setField(Build.VERSION.class, "INCREMENTAL", HookConfig.ENV_INCREMENTAL);
            setField(Build.VERSION.class, "SECURITY_PATCH", HookConfig.ENV_SECURITY_PATCH);
            setField(Build.VERSION.class, "CODENAME", "REL");
            setIntField(Build.VERSION.class, "PREVIEW_SDK_INT", 0);
            XposedEntry.log("Env profile (V6 unified): release="
                + Build.VERSION.RELEASE + " sdk=" + Build.VERSION.SDK_INT + " id=" + Build.ID
                + " display=" + Build.DISPLAY + " model=" + Build.MODEL
                + " device=" + Build.DEVICE + " fp=" + Build.FINGERPRINT
                + " host(real)=" + Build.HOST);
            return;
        }

        try {
            if (HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_FINGERPRINT != null) {
                XposedHelpers.setStaticObjectField(Build.class, "FINGERPRINT",
                    HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_FINGERPRINT);
            }
            if (HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_HOST != null) {
                XposedHelpers.setStaticObjectField(Build.class, "HOST",
                    HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_HOST);
            }
            if (HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_USER != null) {
                XposedHelpers.setStaticObjectField(Build.class, "USER",
                    HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_USER);
            }
            if (HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_DISPLAY != null) {
                XposedHelpers.setStaticObjectField(Build.class, "DISPLAY",
                    HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_DISPLAY);
            }
            XposedEntry.log("Build identity normalized: host=" + HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_HOST
                + " user=" + HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_USER
                + " display=" + HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_DISPLAY);
            XposedEntry.log("Build after normalize: FINGERPRINT=" + Build.FINGERPRINT
                + " HOST=" + Build.HOST + " USER=" + Build.USER
                + " DISPLAY=" + Build.DISPLAY);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("applyBuildIdentity failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private static void setField(Class<?> cls, String name, String value) {
        if (value == null) return;
        try {
            XposedHelpers.setStaticObjectField(cls, name, value);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("setField " + cls.getSimpleName() + "." + name + " failed: "
                + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private static void setIntField(Class<?> cls, String name, int value) {
        try {
            XposedHelpers.setStaticIntField(cls, name, value);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("setIntField " + cls.getSimpleName() + "." + name + " failed: "
                + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private static int parseInt(String s, int def) {
        try {
            return Integer.parseInt(s.trim());
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return def;
        }
    }

    private static void hookSystemGetProperty() {
        if (!HookConfig.ENV_SPOOF) return;
        XC_MethodHook cb = new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                if (param.args.length == 0 || !(param.args[0] instanceof String)) return;
                String key = (String) param.args[0];
                if ("os.version".equals(key)) {
                    if (!osPropLogged) {
                        osPropLogged = true;
                        XposedEntry.log("System.getProperty(os.version) faked -> "
                            + HookConfig.ENV_KERNEL_RELEASE);
                    }
                    param.setResult(HookConfig.ENV_KERNEL_RELEASE);
                } else if ("os.name".equals(key)) {
                    param.setResult("Linux");
                } else if ("os.arch".equals(key)) {
                    param.setResult("aarch64");
                } else if ("http.agent".equals(key)) {
                    // 【T20 核心修复】AOSP RuntimeInit.commonInit() 在 zygote fork 时
                    // 就把 http.agent 算成静态字符串：
                    //   "Dalvik/" + java.vm.version + " (Linux; U; Android "
                    //   + Build.VERSION.RELEASE + "; " + Build.MODEL + " Build/" + Build.ID + ")"
                    // 该值建立在 hook 之前，改 Build.* 字段影响不到它 → 这里直接重算。
                    // 覆盖 10 个读取点（HTTP 主路径 / 小程序 / 广点通 / Bugly / gatherer）
                    String ua = "Dalvik/" + System.getProperty("java.vm.version", "2.1.0")
                        + " (Linux; U; Android " + Build.VERSION.RELEASE
                        + "; " + Build.MODEL + " Build/" + Build.ID + ")";
                    if (!httpAgentLogged) {
                        httpAgentLogged = true;
                        XposedEntry.log("System.getProperty(http.agent) rebuilt -> " + ua);
                    }
                    param.setResult(ua);
                }
            }
        };
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(System.class, "getProperty", cb);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("System.getProperty hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private static void hookOaid() {
        final String fake = HookConfig.FAKE_OAID;
        if (fake == null || fake.isEmpty()) return;
        try {
            Class<?> impl = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.monitor.OaidMonitor$1",
                HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(impl, "convertToStorageData", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (!oaidLogged) {
                        oaidLogged = true;
                        XposedEntry.log("OAID override -> " + fake);
                    }
                    param.setResult(fake);
                }
            });
            XposedEntry.log("Hooked OaidMonitor.convertToStorageData");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("OAID hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private static void hookNetworkIds() {
        // Wi-Fi / Bluetooth MAC：统一成 Android 现代版本的匿名 MAC
        XC_MethodHook macHook = new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                param.setResult("02:00:00:00:00:00");
            }
        };
        try {
            Class<?> wifiInfo = XposedHelpers.findClass("android.net.wifi.WifiInfo",
                HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(wifiInfo, "getMacAddress", macHook);
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(wifiInfo, "getBSSID", macHook);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("WifiInfo MAC hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
        try {
            Class<?> bt = XposedHelpers.findClass("android.bluetooth.BluetoothAdapter",
                HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(bt, "getAddress", macHook);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("Bluetooth MAC hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }

        // IMSI（订阅者 ID）
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.telephony.TelephonyManager", "getSubscriberId",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult("000000000000000");
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookSettingsIds() {
        if (!HookConfig.ENV_SPOOF) return;
        final String aaid = HookConfig.ENV_AAID;
        XC_MethodHook aaidHook = new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                if (param.args.length < 2 || !(param.args[1] instanceof String)) return;
                String key = (String) param.args[1];
                if ("ad_aaid".equals(key) || "oaid".equals(key)
                    || "mi_health_id".equals(key) || "gcbooster_uuid".equals(key)
                    || "key_mqs_uuid".equals(key)) {
                    param.setResult(aaid);
                }
            }
        };
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.provider.Settings$Global", "getString", aaidHook);
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.provider.Settings$Global", "getStringForUser", aaidHook);
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.provider.Settings$Secure", "getStringForUser", aaidHook);
    }

    private static void hookQimeiIdentity() {
        // QIMEI36 是腾讯服务端下发的设备标识：清本地缓存无效，SDK 联网后照样取回
        // 属于旧设备的那个值，因此必须从读取点强制返回伪造值。
        final String fake36 = HookConfig.FAKE_QIMEI36;
        if (fake36 == null || fake36.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        final String fake16 = fake36.length() >= 16 ? fake36.substring(0, 16) : fake36;

        // 1) QimeiSDK / Qimei 对象
        for (String clsName : new String[]{
                "com.tencent.qimei.sdk.Qimei",
                "com.tencent.qimei.sdk.QimeiSDK",
                "com.tencent.qimei.sdk.QimeiCore"}) {
            try {
                Class<?> c = XposedHelpers.findClass(clsName, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
                XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(c, "getQimei36", new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        param.setResult(fake36);
                    }
                });
                XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(c, "getQimei16", new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        param.setResult(fake16);
                    }
                });
                XposedEntry.log("Qimei identity forced on " + clsName);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            }
        }

        // 2) MSF 侧接口的具体实现（接口方法本身是 abstract，hook 不到）
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(
                XposedHelpers.findClass("com.tencent.qphone.base.util.IMSFQimei",
                    HookConfig.getClassLoader()),
                "getQimei36", new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        param.setResult(fake36);
                    }
                });
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }

        // 3) 兜底：QQ 自己的 QimeiResolver 式入口
        for (String clsName : new String[]{
                "com.tencent.mobileqq.qimei.QQQimeiResolver",
                "com.tencent.mobileqq.msf.core.qimei.MSFQimei"}) {
            try {
                Class<?> c = XposedHelpers.findClass(clsName, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
                XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(c, "getQimei36", new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        param.setResult(fake36);
                    }
                });
                XposedEntry.log("Qimei identity forced on " + clsName);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            }
        }
    }

    /** 与 native 层保持一致的伪造值 */
    private static String fakeSystemProperty(String key) {
        if (key == null) return null;

        if (HookConfig.ENV_SPOOF) {
            switch (key) {
                case "ro.build.version.release": return HookConfig.ENV_RELEASE;
                case "ro.build.version.sdk": return HookConfig.ENV_SDK;
                case "ro.build.id": return HookConfig.ENV_BUILD_ID;
                case "ro.build.display.id": return HookConfig.ENV_DISPLAY;
                case "ro.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.system.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.system_ext.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.vendor.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.odm.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.product.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.bootimage.build.fingerprint":
                    return HookConfig.ENV_FINGERPRINT;
                case "ro.build.description": return HookConfig.ENV_DESCRIPTION;
                // 【T12 修正】ro.build.host / ro.build.user 不再伪造：
                // 真机 ReVork / Sabrina Carpenter 本就是 OnePlus 原厂构建机名，完全正常。
                // 之前的伪造值（"build"/"builder"）反而产生了 abuild 这种真机不可能有的值，
                // 服务端拿指纹库一比即知被篡改 → 改为透传真机值。
                // case "ro.build.host": return HookConfig.ENV_HOST;
                // case "ro.build.user": return HookConfig.ENV_USER;
                case "ro.build.date": return HookConfig.ENV_DATE;
                case "ro.build.version.incremental": return HookConfig.ENV_INCREMENTAL;
                case "ro.build.version.security_patch": return HookConfig.ENV_SECURITY_PATCH;
                case "ro.build.version.opporom": return HookConfig.ENV_OPPOROM;
                case "ro.build.version.codename": return "REL";
                case "ro.build.version.all_codenames": return "REL";
                case "ro.build.version.preview_sdk": return "0";
                case "ro.build.version.preview_sdk_int": return "0";
                case "ro.product.model":
                case "ro.product.vendor.model":
                case "ro.product.system.model":
                case "ro.product.odm.model":
                case "ro.product.product.model":
                    return HookConfig.ENV_MODEL;
                case "ro.product.name":
                case "ro.product.vendor.name":
                case "ro.product.system.name":
                case "ro.product.odm.name":
                case "ro.product.product.name":
                    return HookConfig.ENV_PRODUCT_NAME;
                case "ro.product.device":
                case "ro.product.vendor.device":
                case "ro.product.system.device":
                case "ro.product.odm.device":
                case "ro.product.product.device":
                case "ro.build.product":
                    return HookConfig.ENV_DEVICE;
                case "ro.product.brand":
                case "ro.product.vendor.brand":
                case "ro.product.system.brand":
                    return HookConfig.ENV_BRAND;
                case "ro.product.manufacturer":
                case "ro.product.vendor.manufacturer":
                case "ro.product.system.manufacturer":
                    return HookConfig.ENV_MANUFACTURER;
                default: break;
            }
        }

        if ("ro.build.host".equals(key) && HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_HOST != null) {
            return HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_HOST;
        }
        if ("ro.build.user".equals(key) && HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_USER != null) {
            return HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_USER;
        }
        if ("ro.build.display.id".equals(key) && HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_DISPLAY != null) {
            return HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_DISPLAY;
        }
        if (HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_FINGERPRINT != null) {
            switch (key) {
                case "ro.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.system.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.system_ext.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.vendor.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.odm.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.product.build.fingerprint":
                case "ro.bootimage.build.fingerprint":
                    return HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_FINGERPRINT;
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }

        if (cachedSerial != null && ("ro.serialno".equals(key)
                || "ro.boot.serialno".equals(key)
                || "gsm.serial".equals(key)
                || "ro.vendor.oplus.radio.serialno".equals(key)
                || "vendor.oplus.caihong.serialno".equals(key)
                || "persist.sys.oplus.serialno".equals(key))) {
            return cachedSerial;
        }
        if ("ro.boot.verifiedbootstate".equals(key) && HookConfig.FAKE_BOOT_STATE != null) {
            return HookConfig.FAKE_BOOT_STATE;
        }
        if ("ro.boot.flash.locked".equals(key)) return "1";
        if ("ro.boot.vbmeta.device_state".equals(key)) return "locked";
        if ("ro.boot.veritymode".equals(key)) return "enforcing";
        if ("ro.boot.warranty_bit".equals(key)) return "0";
        if ("ro.warranty_bit".equals(key)) return "0";
        if ("ro.vendor.warranty_bit".equals(key)) return "0";
        if ("ro.vendor.boot.warranty_bit".equals(key)) return "0";
        if ("ro.boot.selinux".equals(key)) return "enforcing";
        if ("ro.debuggable".equals(key)) return "0";
        if ("ro.secure".equals(key)) return "1";
        if ("ro.kernel.qemu".equals(key)) return "";
        return null;
    }

    private static boolean isProbeKey(String key) {
        if (key == null) return false;
        String[] kw = {"serial", "verifiedboot", "warranty", "flash.locked",
            "vbmeta", "debuggable", "qemu", "bootloader", "boot.hardware",
            "build.tags", "selinux", "soc", "imei", "android_id", "qimei"};
        for (String k : kw) {
            if (key.contains(k)) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static void hookAndroidId() {
        // Hook Settings.Secure.getString for android_id
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.provider.Settings.Secure", "getString",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length >= 2) {
                        String key = (String) param.args[1];
                        if (HookConfig.PROBE && "android_id".equals(key)) {
                            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Settings.Secure.getString(android_id) -> "
                                + param.getResult());
                        }
                        if ("android_id".equals(key) && cachedAndroidId != null) {
                            param.setResult(cachedAndroidId);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookPandoraMonitor() {
        // Pandora (com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex) is the single gateway QQ uses to
        // read device identifiers; it exposes several methods per identifier. Only
        // getImei used to be hooked, so AndroidID / Serial / OAID / MEID / IMSI were
        // still being read as their real values -- and reported to the server.
        final String[] pandoraClasses = {
            "com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.DeviceInfoMonitor",
            "com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.monitor.DeviceInfoMonitor",
            "com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.core.DeviceInfoMonitor"
        };
        final String[] pandoraMethods = {
            "getImei", "getMeid", "getDeviceId", "getAndroidId", "getSerial",
            "getSerialNumber", "getOaid", "getImsi", "getSubscriberId",
            "getSimSerialNumber", "getMacAddress", "getSimOperator",
            "getNetworkOperator", "getString",
            // 【T20 修复】getModel 从未被 hook → WebView 路径拿真机 LE2123
            // DeviceInfoMonitor.getModel() 内部有 lastModel 缓存，必须 afterHook
            // 用 setResult 覆盖（本处已是 afterHook，符合要求）
            "getModel",
            // 【T20 修复】反射直调 SystemProperties.get()，绕过 native __system_property_get
            "getStringFormSystemProperties"
        };

        for (final String clsName : pandoraClasses) {
            for (final String m : pandoraMethods) {
                HookUtils.hookAllMethods(clsName, m, new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        Object r = param.getResult();
                        String name = param.method.getName();
                        String fake = pandoraFake(name, param.args, r);
                        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Pandora." + name
                            + (fake != null ? " real=" + r + " -> forced" : " -> " + r));
                        if (fake != null && r instanceof String) {
                            param.setResult(fake);
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        // Hook MonitorReporter to block anomaly reports
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.MonitorReporter", "report",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    // Block all monitoring reports
                    param.setResult(null);
                }
            });

        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.MonitorReporter", "getStrategyAndReport",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    // Return a safe strategy that doesn't trigger checks
                    param.setResult(0);
                }
            });
    }

    /** 按 Pandora 方法名返回一致的伪造值；不确定的返回 null（只记录不替换） */
    private static String pandoraFake(String method, Object[] args, Object realResult) {
        if (method == null) return null;
        switch (method) {
            case "getImei":
            case "getMeid":
            case "getDeviceId":
                return cachedImei != null ? cachedImei : HookConfig.FAKE_IMEI;
            case "getAndroidId":
                return cachedAndroidId != null ? cachedAndroidId : HookConfig.FAKE_ANDROID_ID;
            case "getSerial":
            case "getSerialNumber":
                return cachedSerial != null ? cachedSerial : HookConfig.FAKE_SERIAL;
            case "getOaid":
                return HookConfig.FAKE_OAID;
            case "getImsi":
            case "getSubscriberId":
            case "getSimSerialNumber":
                // 没有独立的伪造值，统一沿用 IMEI 派生串，避免暴露真实 ICCID/IMSI
                return null;
            case "getString":
                if (args != null && args.length > 1 && args[1] instanceof String) {
                    String key = (String) args[1];
                    if ("android_id".equals(key)) {
                        return cachedAndroidId != null ? cachedAndroidId : HookConfig.FAKE_ANDROID_ID;
                    }
                    if ("oaid".equals(key) || "ad_aaid".equals(key)) {
                        return HookConfig.FAKE_OAID;
                    }
                }
                return null;
            case "getModel":
                // 【T20】DeviceInfoMonitor.getModel() → 伪造机型（WebView UA 生产者用它）
                return HookConfig.ENV_MODEL;
            case "getStringFormSystemProperties":
                // 【T20】反射读 SystemProperties，绕过 native hook
                if (args != null && args.length > 0 && args[0] instanceof String) {
                    String k = (String) args[0];
                    if ("ro.product.model".equals(k)) return HookConfig.ENV_MODEL;
                    if ("ro.product.brand".equals(k)) return HookConfig.ENV_BRAND;
                    if ("ro.product.manufacturer".equals(k)) return HookConfig.ENV_MANUFACTURER;
                    if ("ro.product.device".equals(k)) return HookConfig.ENV_DEVICE;
                    if ("ro.build.id".equals(k)) return HookConfig.ENV_BUILD_ID;
                    if ("ro.build.display.id".equals(k)) return HookConfig.ENV_DISPLAY;
                    if ("ro.build.version.release".equals(k)) return HookConfig.ENV_RELEASE;
                    if ("ro.build.version.sdk".equals(k)) return HookConfig.ENV_SDK;
                    if ("ro.build.fingerprint".equals(k)) return HookConfig.ENV_FINGERPRINT;
                    if ("ro.serialno".equals(k)) return HookConfig.FAKE_SERIAL;
                }
                return null;
            default:
                return null;
        }
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/hooks/DiagnosticHook.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.hooks;

import android.app.Instrumentation;
import android.content.ComponentName;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.media.MediaDrm;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;

import java.util.Locale;

import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedBridge;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.XposedEntry;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.HookUtils;

/**
 * 诊断探针（PROBE=true 时启用，只读不拦）：
 *
 * 1. 记录 QQ 启动 Activity 的 Intent（重点：mqq.intent.action.KICK_TO_LOGIN 的 extras）
 * 2. 记录 MSFServlet 收到的推送（服务器下发踢人/风控指令时走这里）
 * 3. 记录 ChannelProxyExt / MsfCore 发送的风控相关 SSO 命令
 * 4. 记录 MediaDrm(Widevine deviceUniqueId) 读取 —— 应用可读的硬件级设备 ID
 * 5. 记录 wlogin 设备指纹构建调用
 *
 * 目的：确定“设备存在风险/被踢”到底由客户端哪个上报触发，还是纯服务端标记。
 */
public class DiagnosticHook {

    public static void hook(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        if (!HookConfig.PROBE) return;

        XposedEntry.log("Initializing Diagnostic Probe");
        logBuildIdentity();

        hookStartActivity();
        hookMSFServlet(lpparam);
        hookChannelSend();
        hookMsfSend();
        hookLoginResult();
        hookLoginBusiness();
        hookMediaDrm();
        hookWloginDeviceInfo();
        hookKickHandler(lpparam);
        hookQimeiAndOaid();
        hookTuringRisk();
        // hookTuringManagers();
        // hookTuringClassLoad();
        hookOsCalls();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------- 登录结果（拒绝原因）
    //
    // MSF 的响应对象 FromServiceMsg 携带 resultCode 与业务失败码。登录被服务端
    // 拒绝时 QQ 只弹一个没有正文的"登录失败"框，真正的码在这里：
    //   -10003 / -10004 / -10006 = wtlogin 层拒绝（token/密码/验证）
    //   1000x                    = 服务端安全策略拒绝
    // 只读记录，不做拦截。

    private static void hookLoginResult() {
        final String fsmName = "com.tencent.qphone.base.remote.FromServiceMsg";
        try {
            Class<?> fsm = XposedHelpers.findClass(fsmName, HookConfig.getClassLoader());

            XposedBridge.hookAllConstructors(fsm, new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                    for (Object a : param.args) {
                        if (a instanceof String) {
                            String s = (String) a;
                            if (s.contains("login") || s.contains("Login")
                                || s.contains("wtlogin") || s.contains("wlogin")) {
                                sb.append("cmd=").append(s).append(' ');
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (sb.length() > 0) {
                        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] FromServiceMsg ctor " + sb);
                    }
                }
            });

            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(fsm, "setBusinessFail", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                    sb.append("FromServiceMsg.setBusinessFail");
                    for (Object a : param.args) {
                        sb.append(' ').append(stringify(a));
                    }
                    XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] " + limit(sb.toString(), 800));
                }
            });
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] login result hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }

        // NTLogin reports its verdict through resultCode / businessFailCode rather
        // than setBusinessFail. Also: every *failed* response is recorded regardless
        // of command, because the session termination (ACCOUNT_EXPIRED / w21) is
        // preceded by whichever refresh/check the server refuses. 1000 is success.
        try {
            final Class<?> fsm = XposedHelpers.findClass(fsmName, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            for (final String getter : new String[]{
                    "getResultCode", "getBusinessFailCode", "getBusinessFailMsg"}) {
                XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(fsm, getter, new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        Object self = param.thisObject;
                        if (self == null) return;
                        Object cmdObj;
                        try {
                            cmdObj = XposedHelpers.callMethod(self, "getServiceCmd");
                        } catch (Throwable t) {
                            return;
                        }
                        if (!(cmdObj instanceof String)) return;
                        String cmd = (String) cmdObj;
                        Object res = param.getResult();

                        boolean isLoginCmd = cmd.toLowerCase(Locale.US).contains("login");
                        boolean failed = false;
                        if (res instanceof Integer) {
                            int code = (Integer) res;
                            if (code != 1000 && code != 0) failed = true;
                        } else if (res instanceof String) {
                            failed = !((String) res).isEmpty();
                        }

                        if (isLoginCmd || failed) {
                            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] FromServiceMsg." + getter
                                + (failed ? " FAIL" : "") + " cmd=" + cmd
                                + " -> " + stringify(res));
                        }
                        if (isLoginCmd || failed) {
                            dumpLoginBodyIfNeeded(self, cmd);
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] resultCode hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }

        // Login failure dialog: the class name drifts between QQ versions, so try the
        // known candidates and record whatever ints the dialog is built with.
        for (String clsName : new String[]{
                "com.tencent.mobileqq.activity.LoginFailedDialogWindow",
                "com.tencent.mobileqq.login.LoginFailedDialog",
                "com.tencent.mobileqq.activity.LoginFailedDialog"}) {
            Class<?> dialogCls;
            try {
                dialogCls = XposedHelpers.findClass(clsName, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                continue;
            }
            XposedBridge.hookAllConstructors(dialogCls, new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("LoginFailedDialog ctor");
                    for (Object a : param.args) {
                        if (a instanceof Integer || a instanceof Long || a instanceof String) {
                            sb.append(' ').append(stringify(a));
                        }
                    }
                    XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] " + limit(sb.toString(), 400));
                }
            });
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] hooked " + clsName);
        }

        // wtlogin 层的回调：登录响应最终在这里落地
        for (String cls : new String[]{
                "oicq.wlogin_sdk.request.WtloginHelper",
                "oicq.wlogin_sdk.request.w"}) {
            try {
                XposedHelpers.findClass(cls, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                continue;
            }
            HookUtils.hookAllMethods(cls, "OnLoginResp", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("wtlogin.OnLoginResp");
                    for (Object a : param.args) {
                        sb.append(' ').append(stringify(a));
                    }
                    XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] " + limit(sb.toString(), 800));
                }
            });
        }
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------- 登录业务响应（trpc / LoginRsp）
    //
    // MSF 层已经确认 resultCode=1000，也就是说这一层没有拒绝。真正的"登录失败"
    // 来自 trpc 业务响应体（LoginRsp 的 errCode/errMsg）。先记录哪些类被加载，
    // 再看能不能直接钩到响应对象的读取点。

    private static final java.util.Set<String> CLS_SEEN =
        java.util.Collections.synchronizedSet(new java.util.HashSet<String>());

    // 每个响应对象只 dump 一次响应体
    private static final java.util.Set<Integer> BODY_DUMPED =
        java.util.Collections.synchronizedSet(new java.util.HashSet<Integer>());

    /**
     * trpc 业务响应的 protobuf 体里，string 字段是明文。登录被业务层拒绝时，
     * 错误文案（如"密码错误"/"登录环境异常"）就在这些字节里，不需要知道类名。
     */
    private static void dumpLoginBodyIfNeeded(Object fsm, String cmd) {
        int key = System.identityHashCode(fsm);
        if (!BODY_DUMPED.add(key)) return;
        if (BODY_DUMPED.size() > 512) BODY_DUMPED.clear();
        byte[] buf;
        try {
            Object b = XposedHelpers.callMethod(fsm, "getWupBuffer");
            if (!(b instanceof byte[])) return;
            buf = (byte[]) b;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return;
        }
        if (buf.length == 0) return;
        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] body cmd=" + cmd + " len=" + buf.length);
        // Dump the whole buffer in 200-byte chunks: the login verdict (risk level,
        // errCode, retry hints) may sit anywhere in the payload, and the first
        // 160 bytes alone were not enough to tell encrypted from structured data.
        for (int off = 0; off < buf.length; off += 200) {
            int end = Math.min(off + 200, buf.length);
            StringBuilder hex = new StringBuilder();
            for (int i = off; i < end; i++) {
                hex.append(String.format(Locale.US, "%02x", buf[i]));
            }
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] body@" + off + " " + hex);
        }
        StringBuilder ascii = new StringBuilder();
        for (byte value : buf) {
            char ch = (char) (value & 0xFF);
            ascii.append(ch >= 0x20 && ch < 0x7F ? ch : '.');
        }
        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] body ascii=" + limit(ascii.toString(), 700));
        // UTF-8 明文片段（中文错误文案）
        try {
            String utf = new String(buf, "UTF-8");
            StringBuilder cn = new StringBuilder();
            for (int i = 0; i < utf.length(); i++) {
                char ch = utf.charAt(i);
                if (ch >= 0x4E00 && ch <= 0x9FFF) cn.append(ch);
                else if (cn.length() > 0 && cn.charAt(cn.length() - 1) != ' ') cn.append(' ');
            }
            if (cn.length() > 0) {
                XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] body cjk=" + limit(cn.toString().trim(), 400));
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static void hookLoginBusiness() {
        // 1) 记录 ecdh/login 相关的类名，确定真实响应类型
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(ClassLoader.class, "loadClass", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length == 0 || !(param.args[0] instanceof String)) return;
                    String name = (String) param.args[0];
                    String lower = name.toLowerCase(Locale.US);
                    if (!(lower.contains("ecdh") || lower.contains("loginrsp")
                        || lower.contains("ntlogin") || lower.contains("wtlogin"))) {
                        return;
                    }
                    if (CLS_SEEN.size() < 200 && CLS_SEEN.add(name)) {
                        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] loadClass " + name);
                    }
                }
            });
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] loadClass hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }

        // 2) 若响应类已存在，直接读它的错误码字段
        for (final String clsName : new String[]{
                "trpc.login.ecdh.LoginRsp",
                "trpc.login.ecdh.EcdhService",
                "com.tencent.mobileqq.wtlogin.EcdhService"}) {
            Class<?> c;
            try {
                c = XposedHelpers.findClass(clsName, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                continue;
            }
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] found class " + clsName);
            for (final String getter : new String[]{
                    "getErrCode", "getErrMsg", "getErrType", "getErrInfo", "getRet"}) {
                try {
                    XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(c, getter, new XC_MethodHook() {
                        @Override
                        protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] " + clsName + "." + getter
                                + " -> " + stringify(param.getResult()));
                        }
                    });
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static void hookKsRiskPlaceholder() {
        // reserved
    }

    private static final java.util.Set<String> OS_SEEN =
        java.util.Collections.synchronizedSet(new java.util.HashSet<String>());

    private static void hookOsCalls() {
        // Qimei 的 native 会通过 JNI 调 android.system.Os.stat/statvfs
        try {
            Class<?> os = XposedHelpers.findClass("android.system.Os",
                HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            for (String method : new String[]{"stat", "lstat", "statvfs", "readlink"}) {
                XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(os, method, new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        if (param.args.length == 0 || !(param.args[0] instanceof String)) return;
                        String path = (String) param.args[0];
                        if (OS_SEEN.size() < 300 && OS_SEEN.add(path)) {
                            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Os." + method + ": " + path);
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Os hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }

        // java.lang.System.getProperty（Qimei 可能读 os.version 等）
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(System.class, "getProperty", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length == 0 || !(param.args[0] instanceof String)) return;
                    String key = (String) param.args[0];
                    if ((key.contains("os.") || key.contains("java.") || key.contains("user."))
                        && OS_SEEN.size() < 300 && OS_SEEN.add("prop:" + key)) {
                        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] System.getProperty(" + key + ") -> "
                            + param.getResult());
                    }
                }
            });
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] System.getProperty hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------ TuringFD 风险结果

    private static void hookTuringRisk() {
        try {
            Class<?> aa = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "com.tencent.turingfd.sdk.xq.TNative$aa", HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            String[] methods = {
                "g209311_B9DBF55887B6AEA3",  // riskCheckWup：key0=风险码，key1=上报数据
                "h209311_B9DBF55887B6AEA3",
                "m209311_B9DBF55887B6AEA3",
                "n209311_B9DBF55887B6AEA3",  // DeviceTokenV3
            };
            for (String m : methods) {
                XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(aa, m, new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Turing." + m + " -> "
                            + describeSparse(param.getResult()));
                    }
                });
            }
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Hooked TuringFD native risk methods");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] TuringFD risk hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------- 包内探测逻辑（运行时枚举）
    //
    // Turing 的采集数据不是自己读的：ITuringDeviceInfoProvider / ITuringPkgProvider
    // 由 QQ 侧实现后再喂给它。所以直接 hook QQ 的 turingfd 包，看它到底提供了什么。
    private static final java.util.Set<String> TURING_SEEN =
        java.util.Collections.synchronizedSet(new java.util.HashSet<String>());

    private static void hookTuringManagers() {
        String[] classes = {
            "com.tencent.mobileqq.turingfd.TuringSdkManager",
            "com.tencent.mobileqq.turingfd.a", "com.tencent.mobileqq.turingfd.b",
            "com.tencent.mobileqq.turingfd.c", "com.tencent.mobileqq.turingfd.d",
            "com.tencent.mobileqq.turingfd.e", "com.tencent.mobileqq.turingfd.f",
            "com.tencent.mobileqq.turingfd.g", "com.tencent.mobileqq.turingfd.h",
            "com.tencent.mobileqq.turingfd.i",
        };
        for (final String clsName : classes) {
            Class<?> k;
            try {
                k = XposedHelpers.findClass(clsName, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                continue;
            }
            int hooked = 0;
            for (java.lang.reflect.Method m : k.getDeclaredMethods()) {
                final String mName = m.getName();
                try {
                    XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, new XC_MethodHook() {
                        @Override
                        protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                            String key = clsName + "#" + mName;
                            if (TURING_SEEN.size() < 400 && TURING_SEEN.add(key)) {
                                XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] TuringMgr " + clsName.substring(clsName.lastIndexOf('.') + 1)
                                    + "." + mName + " -> " + stringify(param.getResult()));
                            }
                        }
                    });
                    hooked++;
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                }
            }
            if (hooked > 0) {
                XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] hooked TuringMgr " + clsName + " (" + hooked + " methods)");
            }
        }
    }

    // 记录 Turing 相关类的加载：从「谁实现了 Provider 接口」倒推真正的采集入口
    private static final java.util.Set<String> TCLS =
        java.util.Collections.synchronizedSet(new java.util.HashSet<String>());

    private static void hookTuringClassLoad() {
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(ClassLoader.class, "loadClass", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length == 0 || !(param.args[0] instanceof String)) return;
                    String n = (String) param.args[0];
                    String low = n.toLowerCase(Locale.US);
                    if (!(low.contains("turing") || low.contains("tfd.sdk")
                        || low.contains("deviceinfoprovider") || low.contains("pkgprovider"))) {
                        return;
                    }
                    if (TCLS.size() < 400 && TCLS.add(n)) {
                        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] TuringClass loaded: " + n);
                    }
                }
            });
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Turing class-load watcher armed");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Turing class-load hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private static String describeSparse(Object result) {
        if (result == null) return "null";
        if (!(result instanceof android.util.SparseArray)) {
            if (result instanceof byte[]) return "byte[" + ((byte[]) result).length + "]";
            return String.valueOf(result);
        }
        android.util.SparseArray<?> s = (android.util.SparseArray<?>) result;
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("SparseArray{");
        for (int i = 0; i < s.size(); i++) {
            int key = s.keyAt(i);
            Object v = s.valueAt(i);
            if (i > 0) sb.append(", ");
            sb.append(key).append('=');
            if (v instanceof byte[]) {
                sb.append("byte[").append(((byte[]) v).length).append(']');
            } else {
                sb.append(limit(String.valueOf(v), 120));
            }
        }
        sb.append('}');
        return sb.toString();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ QIMEI / OAID

    private static void hookQimeiAndOaid() {
        // Qimei 对象
        try {
            Class<?> qimei = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "com.tencent.qimei.sdk.Qimei", HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            for (String m : new String[]{"getQimei16", "getQimei36"}) {
                XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(qimei, m, new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Qimei." + m + " -> " + param.getResult());
                    }
                });
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Qimei hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }

        // IMSFQimei 接口实现
        try {
            Class<?> iface = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "com.tencent.qphone.base.util.IMSFQimei", HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(iface, "getQimei36", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] IMSFQimei.getQimei36 -> " + param.getResult());
                }
            });
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] IMSFQimei hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }

        // OAID
        try {
            Class<?> oaid = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.monitor.OaidMonitor", HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            for (java.lang.reflect.Method m : oaid.getDeclaredMethods()) {
                final String name = m.getName();
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] OaidMonitor." + name + " -> " + param.getResult());
                    }
                });
            }
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Hooked OaidMonitor (" + oaid.getDeclaredMethods().length + " methods)");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] OaidMonitor hook failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------- Kick 处理

    private static void hookKickHandler(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        try {
            Class<?> c = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "mqq.app.MainService$MyErrorHandler", lpparam.classLoader);

            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(c, "onKicked", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    Object from = param.args.length > 1 ? param.args[1] : null;
                    Object sameDevice = param.args.length > 2 ? param.args[2] : null;
                    XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] onKicked isSameDevice=" + sameDevice
                        + " from=" + describeMsg(from));
                }
            });

            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(c, "onInterceptKicked", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    Object from = param.args.length > 1 ? param.args[1] : null;
                    XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] onInterceptKicked bool1="
                        + (param.args.length > 2 ? param.args[2] : null)
                        + " bool2=" + (param.args.length > 3 ? param.args[3] : null)
                        + " from=" + describeMsg(from));
                }
            });

            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(c, "popupNotification", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("[PROBE] popupNotification");
                    for (Object arg : param.args) {
                        sb.append(" | ").append(arg instanceof String ? arg : String.valueOf(arg));
                    }
                    XposedEntry.log(limit(sb.toString(), 3000));
                }
            });

            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Hooked MainService$MyErrorHandler kick methods");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.logError("[PROBE] Kick handler hook failed", t);
        }
    }

    private static String describeMsg(Object msg) {
        if (msg == null) return "null";
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(msg.getClass().getSimpleName());
        try {
            Object cmd = XposedHelpers.callMethod(msg, "getServiceCmd");
            sb.append("{cmd=").append(cmd);
            Object rc = XposedHelpers.callMethod(msg, "getResultCode");
            sb.append(",rc=").append(rc);
            Object uin = XposedHelpers.callMethod(msg, "getUin");
            sb.append(",uin=").append(uin);
            Object attrs = XposedHelpers.callMethod(msg, "getAttributes");
            if (attrs instanceof java.util.Map) {
                for (Object e : ((java.util.Map<?, ?>) attrs).entrySet()) {
                    java.util.Map.Entry<?, ?> en = (java.util.Map.Entry<?, ?>) e;
                    sb.append(", ").append(en.getKey()).append('=').append(stringify(en.getValue()));
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            sb.append("{err=").append(t.getClass().getSimpleName()).append('}');
        }
        sb.append('}');
        return limit(sb.toString(), 2000);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Activity

    private static void hookStartActivity() {
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(Instrumentation.class, "execStartActivity",
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        for (Object arg : param.args) {
                            if (arg instanceof Intent) {
                                Intent intent = (Intent) arg;
                                if (isKickOrLoginIntent(intent)) {
                                    dumpIntent("startActivity", intent);
                                }
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Hooked Instrumentation.execStartActivity");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.logError("[PROBE] execStartActivity hook failed", t);
        }
    }

    private static void logBuildIdentity() {
        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Build identity: FINGERPRINT=" + Build.FINGERPRINT);
        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Build identity: DISPLAY=" + Build.DISPLAY
            + " HOST=" + Build.HOST + " USER=" + Build.USER
            + " TAGS=" + Build.TAGS + " TYPE=" + Build.TYPE);
        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Build identity: MANUFACTURER=" + Build.MANUFACTURER
            + " BRAND=" + Build.BRAND + " MODEL=" + Build.MODEL
            + " DEVICE=" + Build.DEVICE + " PRODUCT=" + Build.PRODUCT
            + " BOOTLOADER=" + Build.BOOTLOADER + " HARDWARE=" + Build.HARDWARE);
        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Build identity: RELEASE=" + Build.VERSION.RELEASE
            + " SDK=" + Build.VERSION.SDK_INT
            + " INCREMENTAL=" + Build.VERSION.INCREMENTAL
            + " SECURITY_PATCH=" + Build.VERSION.SECURITY_PATCH);
    }

    private static boolean isKickOrLoginIntent(Intent intent) {
        if (intent == null) return false;
        String action = intent.getAction();
        String a = action == null ? "" : action.toLowerCase(Locale.US);
```
