```java
        if (a.contains("kick") || a.contains("login") || a.contains("account")
            || a.contains("offline") || a.contains("logout")) {
            return true;
        }
        ComponentName cn = intent.getComponent();
        return cn != null && cn.getClassName() != null
            && cn.getClassName().toLowerCase(Locale.US).contains("login");
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------- MSF Servlet

    private static void hookMSFServlet(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("mqq.app.MSFServlet", "onReceive",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    for (Object arg : param.args) {
                        if (arg instanceof Intent) {
                            dumpIntent("MSFServlet.onReceive", (Intent) arg);
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------- SSO 发送

    private static void hookChannelSend() {
        for (String method : new String[]{"sendMessageInner", "send"}) {
            HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.mobileqq.channel.ChannelProxyExt", method,
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        if (param.args.length == 0 || !(param.args[0] instanceof String)) return;
                        String cmd = (String) param.args[0];
                        if (isRiskCommand(cmd)) {
                            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] SSO send cmd=" + cmd
                                + " args=" + param.args.length
                                + " payloadLen=" + payloadLength(param.args));
                            scanPayload(cmd, param.args);
                        }
                    }
                });
        }
    }

    private static final java.util.Set<String> SENT_SEEN =
        java.util.Collections.synchronizedSet(new java.util.HashSet<String>());

    private static void hookMsfSend() {
        String[] classes = {
            "com.tencent.qphone.base.remote.MsfCore",
            "com.tencent.mobileqq.msf.core.MsfCore"
        };
        for (String className : classes) {
            HookUtils.hookAllMethods(className, "sendSsoMsg",
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        // sendSsoMsg 收到的 payload 仍是业务层 protobuf 明文，加密在
                        // MSF native 的 WUP 层才发生 —— 这是唯一能看到"上报了什么
                        // 设备特征"的位置。
                        String cmd = null;
                        Object msgObj = null;
                        for (Object a : param.args) {
                            if (a == null) continue;
                            if (cmd == null && a instanceof String) cmd = (String) a;
                            if (a.getClass().getName().endsWith("ToServiceMsg")) msgObj = a;
                        }
                        byte[] payload = null;
                        if (msgObj != null) {
                            try {
                                Object b = XposedHelpers.callMethod(msgObj, "getWupBuffer");
                                if (b instanceof byte[]) payload = (byte[]) b;
                            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                            }
                            if (cmd == null) {
                                try {
                                    Object c = XposedHelpers.callMethod(msgObj, "getServiceCmd");
                                    if (c instanceof String) cmd = (String) c;
                                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        if (payload == null) {
                            for (Object a : param.args) {
                                if (a instanceof byte[]) {
                                    payload = (byte[]) a;
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        if (cmd == null) return;
                        final int len = payload == null ? 0 : payload.length;
                        String key = cmd + "/" + len;
                        if (SENT_SEEN.size() < 500 && SENT_SEEN.add(key)) {
                            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] SEND cmd=" + cmd + " len=" + len);
                            if (payload != null && len > 0 && isDumpWorthy(cmd)) {
                                dumpSentPayload(cmd, payload);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });
        }
    }

    private static boolean isDumpWorthy(String cmd) {
        String c = cmd.toLowerCase(Locale.US);
        String[] kw = {
            "device", "report", "risk", "turing", "qimei", "0xd79", "secure",
            "sec_", "auth", "login", "sig", "oaid", "androidid", "imei", "token"
        };
        for (String k : kw) {
            if (c.contains(k)) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    /** 打印发送 payload 的明文：hex 分段 + 可打印串 + CJK 片段 */
    private static void dumpSentPayload(String cmd, byte[] buf) {
        int limit = Math.min(buf.length, 1400);
        for (int off = 0; off < limit; off += 200) {
            int end = Math.min(off + 200, limit);
            StringBuilder hex = new StringBuilder();
            for (int i = off; i < end; i++) {
                hex.append(String.format(Locale.US, "%02x", buf[i]));
            }
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] SEND@" + off + " " + hex);
        }
        StringBuilder ascii = new StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < limit; i++) {
            char ch = (char) (buf[i] & 0xFF);
            ascii.append(ch >= 0x20 && ch < 0x7F ? ch : '.');
        }
        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] SEND ascii=" + ascii);
        try {
            String utf = new String(buf, 0, limit, "UTF-8");
            StringBuilder cn = new StringBuilder();
            for (int i = 0; i < utf.length(); i++) {
                char ch = utf.charAt(i);
                if (ch >= 0x4E00 && ch <= 0x9FFF) cn.append(ch);
            }
            if (cn.length() > 0) {
                XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] SEND cjk=" + limit0(cn.toString(), 300));
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static String limit0(String s, int n) {
        return s.length() <= n ? s : s.substring(0, n) + "...";
    }

    private static boolean isRiskCommand(String cmd) {
        if (cmd == null) return false;
        String c = cmd.toLowerCase(Locale.US);
        String[] kw = {
            "risk", "turing", "qimei", "device_lock", "device_token", "mobile_security",
            "o3.report", "0xd79", "kick", "punish", "lock_punish", "sec_check",
            "secevent", "login_safe", "safe_login",
            // session keep-alive path: the server refuses one of these right before
            // ACCOUNT_EXPIRED, so they must be visible in the probe log.
            "trpc.login", "wtlogin", "refresh", "token", "ticket", "a2",
            "online", "session", "verify", "heartbeat", "timestamp", "guide"
        };
        for (String k : kw) {
            if (c.contains(k)) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static int payloadLength(Object[] args) {
        int total = 0;
        for (int i = 1; i < args.length; i++) {
            if (args[i] instanceof byte[]) {
                total += ((byte[]) args[i]).length;
            }
        }
        return total;
    }

    private static final String[] PAYLOAD_KEYWORDS = {
        "magisk", "lsposed", "xposed", "frida", "kernelsu", "verifiedboot",
        "orange", "qimei", "imei", "android_id", "root", "hook", "turing",
        "emulator", "virtual", "debug", "risk", "proxy", "vpn"
    };

    /** 扫描上报包内容（可能是明文 protobuf，也可能 gzip 压缩） */
    private static void scanPayload(String cmd, Object[] args) {
        for (int i = 1; i < args.length; i++) {
            if (!(args[i] instanceof byte[])) continue;
            byte[] raw = (byte[]) args[i];
            if (raw.length == 0) continue;

            byte[] data = raw;
            String note = "";
            if (raw.length > 2 && (raw[0] & 0xff) == 0x1f && (raw[1] & 0xff) == 0x8b) {
                try {
                    java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream bos = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
                    java.util.zip.GZIPInputStream in =
                        new java.util.zip.GZIPInputStream(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(raw));
                    byte[] buf = new byte[8192];
                    int n;
                    while ((n = in.read(buf)) > 0 && bos.size() < 1024 * 1024) {
                        bos.write(buf, 0, n);
                    }
                    in.close();
                    data = bos.toByteArray();
                    note = " gzip->" + data.length;
                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                }
            }

            String ascii = new String(data, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1)
                .toLowerCase(Locale.US);
            StringBuilder hits = new StringBuilder();
            for (String k : PAYLOAD_KEYWORDS) {
                if (ascii.contains(k)) hits.append(k).append(',');
            }
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] payload cmd=" + cmd + " len=" + raw.length + note
                + " head=" + hexHead(data, 24) + " hits=" + hits);
            dumpPayload(cmd, raw);
        }
    }

    /** 把上报包原样落盘，便于离线解析 protobuf */
    private static void dumpPayload(String cmd, byte[] raw) {
        if (raw.length > 2 * 1024 * 1024) return;
        try {
            java.io.File dir = new java.io.File(
                "/storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/qqbypass_payload");
            if (!dir.isDirectory() && !dir.mkdirs()) return;
            String safe = cmd.replaceAll("[^A-Za-z0-9._-]", "_");
            java.io.File out = new java.io.File(dir,
                safe + "_" + System.currentTimeMillis() + ".bin");
            try (java.io.FileOutputStream fos = new java.io.FileOutputStream(out)) {
                fos.write(raw);
            }
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] payload dumped: " + out.getAbsolutePath());
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] payload dump failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- MediaDrm

    private static void hookMediaDrm() {
        try {
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(MediaDrm.class, "getPropertyByteArray",
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        Object name = param.args.length > 0 ? param.args[0] : null;
                        Object result = param.getResult();
                        if (result instanceof byte[]) {
                            byte[] b = (byte[]) result;
                            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] MediaDrm.getPropertyByteArray(" + name
                                + ") len=" + b.length + " head=" + hexHead(b, 8));
                        }
                    }
                });
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(MediaDrm.class, "getPropertyString",
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] MediaDrm.getPropertyString("
                            + (param.args.length > 0 ? param.args[0] : null) + ") -> "
                            + param.getResult());
                    }
                });
            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] Hooked MediaDrm property reads");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.logError("[PROBE] MediaDrm hook failed", t);
        }
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------------------- wlogin

    private static void hookWloginDeviceInfo() {
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("oicq.wlogin_sdk.request.w", "h",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                    for (Object arg : param.args) {
                        if (sb.length() > 0) sb.append(',');
                        sb.append(arg == null ? "null" : arg.getClass().getSimpleName());
                    }
                    XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] wlogin device-info h() args=[" + sb + "]");
                }
            });
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ utils

    private static void dumpIntent(String where, Intent intent) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("[PROBE] ").append(where)
            .append(" action=").append(intent.getAction())
            .append(" data=").append(intent.getDataString())
            .append(" comp=").append(intent.getComponent())
            .append(" flags=0x").append(Integer.toHexString(intent.getFlags()));

        Bundle extras = intent.getExtras();
        if (extras != null) {
            for (String key : extras.keySet()) {
                Object v;
                try {
                    v = extras.get(key);
                } catch (Throwable t) {
                    v = "<" + t.getClass().getSimpleName() + ">";
                }
                sb.append(" | ").append(key).append('=').append(stringify(v));
            }
        }
        XposedEntry.log(limit(sb.toString(), 4000));
    }

    private static String stringify(Object v) {
        if (v == null) return "null";
        if (v instanceof byte[]) return "byte[" + ((byte[]) v).length + "]";
        String s = String.valueOf(v);
        return limit(s, 300);
    }

    private static String hexHead(byte[] b, int n) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(n, b.length); i++) {
            sb.append(String.format(Locale.US, "%02x", b[i]));
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    private static String limit(String s, int n) {
        return s.length() <= n ? s : s.substring(0, n) + "...(" + s.length() + ")";
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/hooks/NetworkReportHook.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.hooks;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;

import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedBridge;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.XposedEntry;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.HookUtils;

/**
 * Network Report Interception Hook
 *
 * Intercepts and blocks security-related reporting to Tencent servers:
 * - trpc.o3.report.* (environment reports)
 * - trpc.o3.mobile_security.* (security checks)
 * - TuringFD risk detection
 * - Wlogin device fingerprint
 *
 * 9.3.50 适配说明：
 * - ChannelProxyExt.sendMessage 是抽象方法（hook 不了），实际发送走 sendMessageInner / send，已覆盖。
 * - MsfCore 类在进程早期可能还没加载，改为轮询重试 hook（2 秒一次，最长 30 秒），
 *   并尝试多个候选类名，避免 ClassNotFoundException 导致拦截失效。
 */
public class NetworkReportHook {

    private static final String[] BLOCK_COMMANDS = {
        "trpc.o3.report",
        "trpc.o3.mobile_security",
        "trpc.ilive_cdn.report",
        "OidbSvc.0xd79",  // Device report
        "wtlogin.device_lock",
        "turing",
        "riskCheckWup",
        "DeviceTokenV3"
    };

    /** 观测名单：这三条之前漏拦，先记录发送内容再决定拦截 */
    private static final String[] OBSERVE_COMMANDS = {
        "trpc.gc_indust.device_report.SsoHome.SsoHomeReport",
        "QQStranger.login_svr.SsoLoginInfoReport",
        "wtlogin.log_report"
    };

    private static boolean isObserveCommand(String command) {
        if (command == null) return false;
        for (String c : OBSERVE_COMMANDS) {
            if (command.contains(c)) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * 全量 wup 落盘：logcat 只有 1MB、且 observe 摘要只存前 64B，
     * 登录窗口真正的大 payload（如 602/642B log_report）尾部会被截掉。
     * 这里直接追加写文件（不经 FileLog 的去重/轮转），供离线逐字节分析。
     */
    private static void dumpWup(String command, byte[] wup) {
        if (wup == null || wup.length == 0) return;
        try {
            java.io.File f = new java.io.File(WUP_DUMP_PATH);
            java.io.File parent = f.getParentFile();
            if (parent != null && !parent.isDirectory()) parent.mkdirs();
            int cap = Math.min(wup.length, 16384);
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(cap * 2 + 128);
            sb.append(System.currentTimeMillis()).append(' ').append(command)
              .append(" len=").append(wup.length).append(" hex=");
            for (int i = 0; i < cap; i++) {
                int v = wup[i] & 0xff;
                sb.append(HEX_DIGITS[v >> 4]).append(HEX_DIGITS[v & 0xf]);
            }
            sb.append('\n');
            java.io.FileOutputStream fos = new java.io.FileOutputStream(f, true);
            try {
                fos.write(sb.toString().getBytes("UTF-8"));
            } finally {
                fos.close();
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            // 取证失败绝不能影响 QQ
        }
    }

    private static final char[] HEX_DIGITS = "0123456789abcdef".toCharArray();
    private static final String WUP_DUMP_PATH =
        "/storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/wup_dump.log";

    /** MsfCore 的候选类名（不同版本/进程可能不同） */
    private static final String[] MSF_CLASS_CANDIDATES = {
        "com.tencent.qphone.base.remote.MsfCore",
        "com.tencent.mobileqq.msf.core.MsfCore",
        "com.tencent.qphone.base.remote.MsfService"
    };

    public static void hook(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        // 【T15 修正】观测与拦截分离：
        //   OBSERVE_ONLY=true  → 挂全部 hook 但 shouldBlock() 恒 false（只记录不拦截）
        //   ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT=false → 完全跳过本次 hook 安装（旧行为，会丢失全部观测）
        // 现在即使不拦截也仍然观测，wup_dump 继续生成。
        if (!HookConfig.ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT && !HookConfig.NETWORK_OBSERVE_ONLY) return;

        XposedEntry.log("Initializing Network Report Hook (observeOnly="
            + HookConfig.NETWORK_OBSERVE_ONLY + ")");

        // Hook 1: ChannelProxyExt（sendMessage 抽象，由 sendMessageInner/send 覆盖）
        hookChannelProxyExt(lpparam);

        // Hook 2: MsfCore.sendSsoMsg（类晚加载 → 轮询重试）
        for (String className : MSF_CLASS_CANDIDATES) {
            hookMsfCoreWithRetry(className, 30000);
        }

        // Hook 3: TuringFD risk detection
        hookTuringFD(lpparam);

        // Hook 4: Wlogin device report
        hookWlogin(lpparam);

        // Hook 5: wtlogin Reporter / report_t（log_report 明文组装与落盘，纯观测）
        hookWloginReporter(lpparam);

        // Hook 6: QsecEst.d()（T8 定位：事件上报唯一加密通道入口，fekit native 0x1401b8）
        hookQsecEst(lpparam);
    }

    /**
     * T8 定位（docs/SUBAGENT_T8.md）：
     *   com.tencent.mobileqq.qsec.qsecest.QsecEst.d(Context, String, String) -> byte[]
     *   = libfekit.so JNI RegisterNatives@0x890d4 注册的 native 实现 sub_1401B8 (0x1401b8)
     * args[1]/args[2] = 明文输入；返回值 = 加密后上报报文（含 "00002" 信封）
     * 这是 MsfCore.sendSsoMsg 层看不见的事件上报通道 —— 观测 + 未来改写点。
     */
    /**
     * QSEC 通道模式（见 docs/QSEC_EXPERIMENT_PLAN.md）：
     *   文件 /storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/qsec_mode.txt
     *   observe(默认) / empty(空数组) / null / blank(同长全零) /
     *   forge(用 qsec_forge.bin 替换，长度可变，T9 已解出加密链可任意构造)
     */
    private static final String QSEC_MODE_PATH =
        "/storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/qsec_mode.txt";
    private static final String QSEC_FORGE_PATH =
        "/storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/qsec_forge.bin";
    private static volatile String qsecMode = null;
    private static byte[] qsecForge = null;
    private static long qsecModeReadAt = 0;

    private static String qsecMode() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (qsecMode == null || now - qsecModeReadAt > 3000L) {
            qsecModeReadAt = now;
            String v = null;
            try {
                java.io.File f = new java.io.File(QSEC_MODE_PATH);
                if (f.exists()) {
                    byte[] b = new byte[64];
                    java.io.FileInputStream in = new java.io.FileInputStream(f);
                    int n;
                    try { n = in.read(b); } finally { in.close(); }
                    if (n > 0) {
                        v = new String(b, 0, n, "UTF-8").trim().toLowerCase();
                    }
                }
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            }
            qsecMode = (v == null || v.isEmpty()) ? "observe" : v;
        }
        return qsecMode;
    }

    /** 读取 forge 报文（每次现读，支持运行期替换文件） */
    private static byte[] qsecForgeBytes() {
        try {
            java.io.File f = new java.io.File(QSEC_FORGE_PATH);
            if (!f.exists() || f.length() <= 0 || f.length() > 65536) return null;
            byte[] b = new byte[(int) f.length()];
            java.io.FileInputStream in = new java.io.FileInputStream(f);
            int n;
            try { n = in.read(b); } finally { in.close(); }
            if (n <= 0) return null;
            if (n == b.length) return b;
            byte[] o = new byte[n];
            System.arraycopy(b, 0, o, 0, n);
            return o;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static void hookQsecEst(final XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        final String cls = "com.tencent.mobileqq.qsec.qsecest.QsecEst";
        final long deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + 60000;
        new Thread(new Runnable() {
            @Override
            public void run() {
                ClassLoader[] loaders = new ClassLoader[]{
                    lpparam.classLoader,
                    NetworkReportHook.class.getClassLoader(),
                    ClassLoader.getSystemClassLoader()
                };
                while (System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) {
                    for (ClassLoader cl : loaders) {
                        if (cl == null) continue;
                        try {
                            Class<?> c = Class.forName(cls, false, cl);
                            if (c == null) continue;
                            for (Method m : c.getDeclaredMethods()) {
                                if (!"d".equals(m.getName())) continue;
                                Class<?>[] pt = m.getParameterTypes();
                                if (pt.length != 3) continue;
                                if (!pt[0].getName().equals("android.content.Context")) continue;
                                if (!pt[1].getName().equals("java.lang.String")) continue;
                                if (!pt[2].getName().equals("java.lang.String")) continue;
                                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, new XC_MethodHook() {
                                    @Override
                                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                                        try {
                                            Object a1 = param.args[1], a2 = param.args[2];
                                            Object r = param.getResult();
                                            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                                            sb.append("in1=").append(a1 == null ? "null"
                                                    : (a1.toString().length() > 300 ? a1.toString().substring(0, 300) + "..." : a1.toString()));
                                            sb.append(" | in2=").append(a2 == null ? "null"
                                                    : (a2.toString().length() > 300 ? a2.toString().substring(0, 300) + "..." : a2.toString()));
                                            if (r instanceof byte[]) {
                                                byte[] b = (byte[]) r;
                                                sb.append(" | out_len=").append(b.length);
                                                StringBuilder h = new StringBuilder(" | out_hex=");
                                                for (int i = 0; i < b.length && i < 64; i++) {
                                                    h.append(String.format("%02x", b[i]));
                                                }
                                                sb.append(h);
                                                dumpWup("QsecEst.d", b);   // 完整落盘
                                            } else {
                                                sb.append(" | out=").append(String.valueOf(r));
                                            }
                                            XposedEntry.log("QSEC_EST " + sb);
                                        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                                        }
                                    }
                                });
                                // 模式应用（before hook，改写返回值）
                                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, new XC_MethodHook() {
                                    @Override
                                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                                        String mode = qsecMode();
                                        if ("observe".equals(mode)) return;
                                        Object r = param.getResult();
                                        int len = (r instanceof byte[]) ? ((byte[]) r).length : 0;
                                        if ("empty".equals(mode)) {
                                            param.setResult(new byte[0]);
                                            XposedEntry.log("QSEC_MODE empty: " + len + "B -> 0B");
                                        } else if ("null".equals(mode)) {
                                            param.setResult(null);
                                            XposedEntry.log("QSEC_MODE null: " + len + "B -> null");
                                        } else if ("blank".equals(mode)) {
                                            param.setResult(new byte[len > 0 ? len : 1293]);
                                            XposedEntry.log("QSEC_MODE blank: " + len + "B -> zeros");
                                        } else if ("forge".equals(mode)) {
                                            byte[] f = qsecForgeBytes();
                                            if (f != null && f.length > 0) {
                                                param.setResult(f);
                                                XposedEntry.log("QSEC_MODE forge: " + len + "B -> " + f.length + "B forged");
                                            } else {
                                                XposedEntry.log("QSEC_MODE forge: qsec_forge.bin missing, passthrough");
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                });
                                XposedEntry.log("QsecEst.d hooked (event-report channel) mode=" + qsecMode());
                                return;
                            }
                        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                        }
                    }
                    try { Thread.sleep(2000); } catch (InterruptedException e) { return; }
                }
                XposedEntry.log("QsecEst.d not found in 60s (class may load later)");
            }
        }).start();
    }

    /**
     * wtlogin 上报观测（Java 层"根"的读取）：
     * - oicq.wlogin_sdk.report.Reporter：7 个 virtual（第 7 个含 "device" 字符串 = 设备上报组装点）
     * - oicq.wlogin_sdk.report.report_t：write_tofile（log_report 明文本地落盘）/ read_fromfile / delete_file
     * 方法名混淆未知 → 反射遍历 getDeclaredMethods 全量 hook，beforeHookedMethod 记录不阻断。
     */
    private static void hookWloginReporter(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        try {
            Class<?> reportT = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "oicq.wlogin_sdk.report.report_t", lpparam.classLoader);
            Method[] ms = reportT.getDeclaredMethods();
            for (Method m : ms) {
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        logReporterCall("report_t." + param.method.getName(), param);
                    }
                });
            }
            XposedEntry.log("WloginReporter armed: report_t (" + ms.length + " methods)");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("WloginReporter report_t failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
        try {
            Class<?> reporter = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "oicq.wlogin_sdk.report.Reporter", lpparam.classLoader);
            Method[] ms = reporter.getDeclaredMethods();
            for (Method m : ms) {
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(m, new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        logReporterCall("Reporter." + param.method.getName(), param);
                    }
                });
            }
            XposedEntry.log("WloginReporter armed: Reporter (" + ms.length + " methods)");
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("WloginReporter Reporter failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }

        // 明文组装点：Reporter.toJasonObj 返回 log_report 的 JSON 明文（加密前的最后形态）
        try {
            Class<?> reporter = XposedHelpers.findClass(
                "oicq.wlogin_sdk.report.Reporter", lpparam.classLoader);
            Method target = null;
            for (Method m : reporter.getDeclaredMethods()) {
                if ("toJasonObj".equals(m.getName()) || "toJsonObj".equals(m.getName())) {
                    target = m;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (target != null) {
                XposedBridge.hookMethod(target, new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        Object r = param.getResult();
                        if (r == null) return;
                        String s;
                        if (r instanceof String) {
                            s = (String) r;
                        } else if (r instanceof byte[]) {
                            byte[] b = (byte[]) r;
                            StringBuilder h = new StringBuilder();
                            for (int i = 0; i < b.length && i < 4096; i++) {
                                h.append(String.format("%02x", b[i]));
                            }
                            s = "bytes[" + b.length + "]=" + h;
                        } else {
                            s = String.valueOf(r);
                        }
                        XposedEntry.log("WLOGIN_JSON len=" + s.length() + " body=" + s);
                        dumpText("WLOGIN_JSON", s);
                    }
                });
                XposedEntry.log("WloginReporter armed: toJasonObj (plaintext JSON)");
            } else {
                XposedEntry.log("WloginReporter toJasonObj not found");
            }
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            XposedEntry.log("WloginReporter toJasonObj failed: " + t.getMessage());
        }
    }

    /** 明文文本落盘（JSON 等），追加写同一个 dump 文件 */
    private static void dumpText(String tag, String text) {
        if (text == null || text.isEmpty()) return;
        try {
            java.io.File f = new java.io.File(WUP_DUMP_PATH);
            java.io.File parent = f.getParentFile();
            if (parent != null && !parent.isDirectory()) parent.mkdirs();
            String line = System.currentTimeMillis() + " " + tag + " len=" + text.length()
                    + " text=" + text + "\n";
            java.io.FileOutputStream fos = new java.io.FileOutputStream(f, true);
            try {
                fos.write(line.getBytes("UTF-8"));
            } finally {
                fos.close();
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
        }
    }

    /** 记录一次 Reporter/report_t 调用：方法名 + 参数摘要（String 全文 / byte[] 前 64B hex） */
    private static void logReporterCall(String tag, XC_MethodHook.MethodHookParam param) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder("WLOGIN_REPORT ").append(tag);
        for (int i = 0; i < param.args.length; i++) {
            sb.append(" arg").append(i).append("=").append(describeArg(param.args[i]));
        }
        XposedEntry.log(sb.toString());
    }

    private static String describeArg(Object a) {
        if (a == null) return "null";
        if (a instanceof String) return "\"" + a + "\"";
        if (a instanceof byte[]) {
            byte[] b = (byte[]) a;
            StringBuilder h = new StringBuilder("bytes[").append(b.length).append("]=");
            for (int i = 0; i < b.length && i < 64; i++) {
                h.append(String.format("%02x", b[i]));
            }
            return h.toString();
        }
        return a.getClass().getSimpleName();
    }

    private static void hookChannelProxyExt(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        String[] methodNames = {"sendMessageInner", "send"};

        for (String methodName : methodNames) {
            HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.mobileqq.channel.ChannelProxyExt", methodName,
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        if (param.args.length < 1) return;

                        String command = (String) param.args[0];
                        if (shouldBlock(command)) {
                            XposedEntry.log("Blocked ChannelProxyExt: " + command);

                            // Inject fake response
                            try {
                                Object channelManager = XposedHelpers.callStaticMethod(
                                    XposedHelpers.findClass("com.tencent.mobileqq.channel.ChannelManager",
                                    lpparam.classLoader), "getInstance");

                                XposedHelpers.callMethod(channelManager, "onNativeReceive",
                                    command, new byte[0], 0L);
                            } catch (Throwable t) {
                                // Ignore if injection fails
                            }

                            param.setResult(null);
                        }
                    }
                });
        }

        XposedEntry.log("ChannelProxyExt.sendMessage is abstract, skipped (covered by sendMessageInner/send)");
    }

    /**
     * 轮询重试 hook MsfCore.sendSsoMsg：
     * 类可能晚于 hook 时机才加载，每 2 秒重试一次，直到成功或超时。
     * 注意：必须直接用 XposedBridge.hookAllMethods，不能用 HookUtils（它会吞掉异常，
     * 导致"假成功"日志）。只有真正 hook 成功才打 "Network interception armed"。
     */
    private static void hookMsfCoreWithRetry(final String className, final long timeoutMs) {
        final long deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMs;

        new Thread(new Runnable() {
            @Override
            public void run() {
                while (System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) {
                    try {
                        Class<?> clazz = XposedHelpers.findClass(className, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
                        XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(clazz, "sendSsoMsg",
                            new XC_MethodHook() {
                                @Override
                                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                                    if (param.args.length < 1) return;

                                    Object toServiceMsg = param.args[0];
                                    String command = (String) XposedHelpers.callMethod(toServiceMsg, "getServiceCmd");

                                    boolean blocked = shouldBlock(command);
                                    // 取证：观测名单 + 被拦命令都要拿到完整 wup（不改行为，只落盘）
                                    if (isObserveCommand(command) || blocked) {
                                        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                                        if (blocked) sb.append("[BLOCK] ");
                                        try {
                                            Object attr = XposedHelpers.callMethod(toServiceMsg, "getAttributes");
                                            sb.append("attrs=").append(String.valueOf(attr));
                                        } catch (Throwable t) { sb.append("attrs=?"); }
                                        try {
                                            byte[] wup = (byte[]) XposedHelpers.callMethod(toServiceMsg, "getWupBuffer");
                                            if (wup != null) {
                                                sb.append(" wup_len=").append(wup.length);
                                                StringBuilder hex = new StringBuilder();
                                                for (int i = 0; i < wup.length && i < 48; i++) {
                                                    hex.append(String.format("%02x", wup[i]));
                                                }
                                                sb.append(" wup48=").append(hex);
                                                dumpWup(command, wup);
                                            }
                                        } catch (Throwable t) { sb.append(" wup=?"); }
                                        XposedEntry.log("OBSERVE MsfCore cmd=" + command + " | " + sb);
                                    }

                                    if (blocked) {
                                        XposedEntry.log("Blocked MsfCore: " + command);

                                        // Return sequence number to simulate success
                                        try {
                                            int seq = (int) XposedHelpers.callMethod(toServiceMsg, "getRequestSsoSeq");
                                            param.setResult(seq);
                                        } catch (Throwable t) {
                                            param.setResult(0);
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            });
                        XposedEntry.log("Network interception armed: " + className + ".sendSsoMsg");
                        return; // 成功，停止重试
                    } catch (Throwable t) {
                        try {
                            Thread.sleep(2000);
                        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }).start();
    }

    private static void hookTuringFD(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        // 观测模式：之前 setResult(null) 阻断 Turing 操作，导致其检测任务异常
        // （ClassCastException）→ 上报缺失 → 服务端判"外挂或其他软件影响QQ正常使用"
        // （Turing 数据缺失 = 服务端判异常，见 hook_turingxq.cpp 注释）。
        // 现在只观测不阻断：让 Turing 正常上报。
        String[] turingClasses = {
            "com.tencent.turingfd.sdk.xq.Pomegranate",
            "com.tencent.turingfd.sdk.xq.Blueberry",
            "com.tencent.turingcam.oqKCa"
        };

        for (String className : turingClasses) {
            try {
                HookUtils.hookAllMethods(className, "a", new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        // 观测：记录 Turing 操作，不阻断
                        String cls = param.method.getDeclaringClass().getSimpleName();
                        String desc = "TuringFD observe " + cls + " args=" + param.args.length;
                        XposedEntry.log(desc);
                    }
                });
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                // 类可能未加载，静默
            }
        }
    }

    private static void hookWlogin(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        // Hook Wlogin device fingerprint collection
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("oicq.wlogin_sdk.request.w", "h",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    // Modify device fingerprint before sending
                    XposedEntry.log("Intercepted Wlogin device fingerprint");
                }
            });
    }

    private static boolean shouldBlock(String command) {
        if (command == null) return false;
        // T15：观测模式下永不拦截，只落盘
        if (HookConfig.NETWORK_OBSERVE_ONLY) return false;

        for (String blockCmd : BLOCK_COMMANDS) {
            if (command.contains(blockCmd)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/hooks/RootDetectionHook.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.hooks;

import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.XposedEntry;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.HookUtils;

import java.io.File;

/**
 * Root Detection Bypass Hook
 *
 * Bypasses 6 independent Java-layer root detection implementations:
 * 1. oicq.wlogin_sdk.request.w.h() - Wlogin SDK
 * 2. com.tencent.gathererga.core.UserInfoImpl.isRooted()
 * 3. org.light.device.LightDeviceUtils.isRooted()
 * 4. com.tencent.bugly.proguard.cp
 * 5. com.tenpay.charge.v2.util.ChargeV2Utils.isDeviceRooted()
 * 6. com.tencent.camerasdk.avreport.DeviceInfo
 *
 * Note: Native-layer detection (libfekit.so) requires native hooks
 */
public class RootDetectionHook {

    public static void hook(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        if (!HookConfig.ENABLE_ROOT_BYPASS) return;

        XposedEntry.log("Initializing Root Detection Bypass");

        // Hook 1: Wlogin SDK root check
        hookWloginRoot();

        // Hook 2: Gatherer GA root check
        hookGathererRoot();

        // Hook 3: Light Device Utils
        hookLightDeviceRoot();

        // Hook 4: Bugly crash SDK
        hookBuglyRoot();

        // Hook 5: Tenpay charge utils
        hookTenpayRoot();

        // Hook 6: Camera SDK
        hookCameraSDKRoot();
        hookGathererProvider();
        hookRiskLevelObserver();

        // Generic file existence checks for su binaries
        hookFileExists();

        // Hook Runtime.exec to prevent su detection
        hookRuntimeExec();

        // Hook PackageManager to hide root apps
        hookPackageManager();
   
    startLazyHookRetry();
 
    // 【V8 补全】懒加载类重试。
    // 部分 SDK 类（gathererga / camerasdk）在 handleLoadPackage 时还没加载，
    // 直接 hook 会 ClassNotFoundException。起后台线程周期性重试，直到类出现。
    startLazyHookRetry();
}

private static void startLazyHookRetry() {
    Thread t = new Thread(new Runnable() {
        @Override
        public void run() {
            String[][] targets = {
                {"com.tencent.gathererga.core.internal.provider.impl.UserInfoImpl", "isRooted"},
                {"com.tencent.gathererga.core.UserInfoProvider", "isRooted"},
                {"com.tencent.camerasdk.avreport.DeviceInfo", "isDeviceRooted"},
                {"com.tencent.qqnt.kernelgpro.nativeinterface.GProGuildSafetyRisk", "getRiskLevel"},
            };
            for (int round = 0; round < 60; round++) {   // 最多 60 轮，每轮 2 秒
                try { Thread.sleep(2000); } catch (InterruptedException e) { return; }
                for (String[] t : targets) {
                    try {
                        Class<?> c = de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers.findClass(t[0], HookConfig.getClassLoader());
                        if (c == null) continue;
                        // 已 hook 过的不重复
                        if (lazyHooked.contains(t[0] + "." + t[1])) continue;
                        HookUtils.hookAllMethods(t[0], t[1], new XC_MethodHook() {
                            @Override
                            protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                                Class<?> rt = param.method.getDeclaringClass();
                                String n = param.method.getName();
                                if ("getRiskLevel".equals(n)) {
                                    return;   // 观测型，不改值
                                }
                                param.setResult(Boolean.FALSE);
                                XposedEntry.log("[LAZY-HOOK] " + rt.getSimpleName() + "." + n + " -> false");
                            }
                        });
                        lazyHooked.add(t[0] + "." + t[1]);
                        XposedEntry.log("[LAZY-HOOK] attached " + t[0] + "." + t[1] + " (round " + round + ")");
                    } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                    }
                }
                if (lazyHooked.size() >= targets.length) return;
            }
        }
    }, "QNG-LazyHook");
    t.setDaemon(true);
    t.start();
}


    private static void hookWloginRoot() {
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("oicq.wlogin_sdk.request.w", "h",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    // This method collects device info including root status
                    // We let it run but will block the network report
                    XposedEntry.log("Wlogin root check intercepted");
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookGathererRoot() {
        HookUtils.hookMethod("com.tencent.gathererga.core.internal.provider.impl.UserInfoImpl", "isRooted",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(false);
                    XposedEntry.log("Gatherer isRooted: false");
                }
            });
    }

    // 【V8 补全】gatherer SDK 的接口定义（UserInfoProvider）。
// 实现类 UserInfoImpl 已 hook，但接口层可能被单独调用（多态），需一并处理。
private static void hookGathererProvider() {
    HookUtils.hookMethod("com.tencent.gathererga.core.UserInfoProvider", "isRooted",
        new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                param.setResult(false);
            }
        });
}

private static void hookLightDeviceRoot() {
        HookUtils.hookMethod("org.light.device.LightDeviceUtils", "isRooted",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(false);
                    XposedEntry.log("LightDevice isRooted: false");
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookBuglyRoot() {
        HookUtils.hookMethod("com.tencent.bugly.proguard.cp", "a",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(false);
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookTenpayRoot() {
        HookUtils.hookMethod("com.tenpay.charge.v2.util.ChargeV2Utils", "isDeviceRooted",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(false);
                    XposedEntry.log("Tenpay isDeviceRooted: false");
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookCameraSDKRoot() {
        // 【V8 修正】实际方法名是 isDeviceRooted，不是 isRooted（dex 实证）
        HookUtils.hookMethod("com.tencent.camerasdk.avreport.DeviceInfo", "isDeviceRooted",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(false);
                }
            });
    }

    /** V8 新增观测点：GProGuildSafetyRisk.getRiskLevel 返回服务端下发的风险等级。
     *  这是罕见能直接看到"服务端裁决结果"的接口——不篡改，只记录，
     *  用于确认服务端到底把我们判定为什么等级。 */
    private static void hookRiskLevelObserver() {
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.qqnt.kernelgpro.nativeinterface.GProGuildSafetyRisk",
            "getRiskLevel", new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    Object r = param.getResult();
                XposedEntry.log("[RISK-OBSERVE] GProGuildSafetyRisk.getRiskLevel -> " + r);
            }
        });
}

private static void hookFileExists() {
        // Hook File.exists() to hide su binaries and root manager apps
        HookUtils.hookMethod(File.class, "exists",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    File file = (File) param.thisObject;
                    String path = file.getAbsolutePath();

                    // Common su paths
                    if (isSuspiciousPath(path)) {
                        param.setResult(false);
                    }
                }
            });
        // T6 头号嫌疑：fekit Java 探测集用 File.canRead 探测 su
        // （native access deny 不覆盖 Java canRead 走的 stat 系 syscall）
        HookUtils.hookMethod(File.class, "canRead",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    File file = (File) param.thisObject;
                    String path = file.getAbsolutePath();
                    if (isSuspiciousPath(path)) {
                        XposedEntry.log("Blocked File.canRead: " + path);
                        param.setResult(false);
                    }
                }
            });
        HookUtils.hookMethod(File.class, "canExecute",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    File file = (File) param.thisObject;
                    String path = file.getAbsolutePath();
                    if (isSuspiciousPath(path)) {
                        XposedEntry.log("Blocked File.canExecute: " + path);
                        param.setResult(false);
                    }
                }
            });
        HookUtils.hookMethod(File.class, "canWrite",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    File file = (File) param.thisObject;
                    String path = file.getAbsolutePath();
                    if (isSuspiciousPath(path)) {
                        param.setResult(false);
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookRuntimeExec() {
        // Hook Runtime.exec to prevent command-based detection
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("java.lang.Runtime", "exec",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length > 0) {
                        String command = param.args[0].toString();

                        // Block root-detection commands
                        if (command.contains("su") ||
                            command.contains("magisk") ||
                            command.contains("/system/xbin/which") ||
                            command.contains("mount") && command.contains("grep")) {

                            XposedEntry.log("Blocked Runtime.exec: " + command);
                            // Return a dummy process that exits immediately
                            param.setResult(new ProcessBuilder("echo").start());
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    // 【V8 补全】需要隐藏的包名：root 管理器 + 我们的模块自身。
// QQ 的 PackageManager 扫描会看到 io.github.kida.qqnativeguard，
// 这本身就是"设备装了 hook 模块"的直接证据。
private static final java.util.Set<String> lazyHooked = java.util.Collections.synchronizedSet(new java.util.HashSet<String>());

private static final String[] HIDDEN_PKGS = {
    // Root 管理器
    "com.topjohnwu.magisk", "me.weishu.kernelsu", "com.kernelsu.kernelsu",
    "eu.chainfire.supersu", "me.bmax.apatch", "com.tsng.hidemyapplist",
    // Xposed / LSPosed
    "org.lsposed.manager", "de.robv.android.xposed.installer",
    "org.meowcat.edxposed.manager", "io.va.exposed",
    // 我们的模块自身
    "io.github.kida.qqnativeguard",
    "io.github.qqenhanced.bypass",
    // 其他 hook 框架
    "com.saurik.substrate", "re.frida.server",
};

private static boolean isHiddenPkg(String name) {
    if (name == null) return false;
    for (String h : HIDDEN_PKGS) {
        if (h.equals(name)) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

private static void hookPackageManager() {
    String pmCls = "android.app.ApplicationPackageManager";

    // 1) 单包查询：命中隐藏包时抛出 NameNotFoundException，让调用方认为"未安装"
    String[] singleQuery = {"getPackageInfo", "getApplicationInfo"};
    for (final String method : singleQuery) {
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods(pmCls, method, new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                if (param.args.length > 0 && param.args[0] instanceof String) {
                    String pkg = (String) param.args[0];
                    if (isHiddenPkg(pkg)) {
                        throw new android.content.pm.PackageManager.NameNotFoundException(pkg);
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // 2) 列表查询：从返回的 List 中移除隐藏包
    String[] listQuery = {"getInstalledPackages", "getInstalledApplications"};
    for (final String method : listQuery) {
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods(pmCls, method, new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                Object r = param.getResult();
                if (!(r instanceof java.util.List)) return;
                java.util.List<?> list = (java.util.List<?>) r;
                java.util.List<Object> kept = new java.util.ArrayList<>(list.size());
                for (Object item : list) {
                    String pkg = null;
                    if (item instanceof android.content.pm.PackageInfo) {
                        pkg = ((android.content.pm.PackageInfo) item).packageName;
                    } else if (item instanceof android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo) {
                        pkg = ((android.content.pm.ApplicationInfo) item).packageName;
                    }
                    if (pkg == null || !isHiddenPkg(pkg)) {
                        kept.add(item);
                    }
                }
                if (kept.size() != list.size()) {
                    param.setResult(kept);
                }
            }
        });
    }
}

    private static boolean isSuspiciousPath(String path) {
        // Comprehensive list of root-related paths
        String[] suspiciousPaths = {
            "/data/local/su",
            "/data/local/bin/su",
            "/data/local/xbin/su",
            "/sbin/su",
            "/su/bin/su",
            "/system/bin/su",
            "/system/bin/.ext/.su",
            "/system/sd/xbin/su",
            "/system/usr/we-need-root/su",
            "/system/xbin/su",
            "/system/app/Superuser.apk",
            "/system/app/SuperSU.apk",
            "/system/etc/init.d/99SuperSUDaemon",
            "/dev/com.koushikdutta.superuser.daemon/",
            "/system/xbin/daemonsu",
            // Magisk
            "/data/adb/magisk",
            "/sbin/.magisk",
            "/cache/magisk.log",
            "/data/magisk.img",
            "/data/adb/magisk.img",
            // KernelSU
            "/data/adb/ksu",
            "/data/adb/ksud"
        };

        for (String suspicious : suspiciousPaths) {
            if (path.contains(suspicious)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/hooks/RuntimeMonitorHook.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.hooks;

import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.XposedEntry;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.HookUtils;

/**
 * Runtime Monitor Bypass
 *
 * Handles Pandora RuntimeMonitor which monitors:
 * - Runtime.exec() calls for suspicious commands (ip, pm, getprop, sh)
 * - Command outputs via ProcessBuilder
 * - System command execution patterns
 *
 * Also handles various command processors:
 * - IPProcessor
 * - PackageManagerProcessor
 * - PropProcessor
 */
public class RuntimeMonitorHook {

    private static final String[] MONITORED_COMMANDS = {
        "ip", "pm", "getprop", "sh", "/system/bin/sh",
        "ps", "cat", "mount", "which", "su"
    };

    public static void hook(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        if (!HookConfig.ENABLE_RUNTIME_BYPASS) return;

        XposedEntry.log("Initializing Runtime Monitor Bypass");

        // Hook 1: Pandora RuntimeMonitor
        hookPandoraRuntimeMonitor();

        // Hook 2: Command processors
        hookCommandProcessors();

        // Hook 3: ProcessBuilder monitoring
        hookProcessBuilder();
    }

    private static void hookPandoraRuntimeMonitor() {
        // Disable RuntimeMonitor entirely
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.RuntimeMonitor", "monitor",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    // Block all runtime monitoring
                    param.setResult(null);
                }
            });

        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.RuntimeMonitor", "onExec",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(null);
                }
            });

        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.RuntimeMonitor", "shouldMonitor",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(false);
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookCommandProcessors() {
        // Hook IPProcessor
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.IPProcessor", "process",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(null);
                }
            });

        // Hook PackageManagerProcessor
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.PackageManagerProcessor", "process",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(null);
                }
            });

        // Hook PropProcessor
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.PropProcessor", "process",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(null);
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookProcessBuilder() {
        // Hook ProcessBuilder.start() to monitor command execution
        HookUtils.hookMethod(ProcessBuilder.class, "start",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    ProcessBuilder pb = (ProcessBuilder) param.thisObject;
                    java.util.List<String> command = pb.command();

                    if (command != null && !command.isEmpty()) {
                        String cmd = command.get(0);

                        if (HookConfig.PROBE) {
                            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
                            for (String part : command) {
                                if (sb.length() > 0) sb.append(' ');
                                sb.append(part);
                            }
                            String full = sb.toString();
                            if (full.length() > 400) full = full.substring(0, 400) + "...";
                            XposedEntry.log("[PROBE] ProcessBuilder: " + full);
                        }

                        // Log monitored commands
                        if (isMonitoredCommand(cmd)) {
                            XposedEntry.log("ProcessBuilder command intercepted: " + cmd);
                            // We don't block it, just log - blocking might break functionality
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static boolean isMonitoredCommand(String command) {
        if (command == null) return false;

        for (String monitored : MONITORED_COMMANDS) {
            if (command.contains(monitored)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/hooks/UnifiedHookCoordinator.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.hooks;

import android.util.Log;

import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.NativeBypass;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.XposedEntry;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.HookUtils;

/**
 * Unified Hook Coordinator
 *
 * This module coordinates between Java and Native hooks to provide
 * seamless protection across both layers.
 *
 * Java Hook → If failed → Try Native Hook
 * Native Hook → Notify Java layer
 */
public class UnifiedHookCoordinator {

    private static boolean nativeLayerAvailable = false;
    /** Native hooks 是否实际启用（受 HookConfig.ENABLE_NATIVE_HOOKS 控制） */
    private static boolean nativeLayerEnabled = false;

    public static void initialize(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        XposedEntry.log("=== Initializing Unified Hook Coordinator ===");

        boolean anyCoordinatorFeature = HookConfig.ENABLE_NATIVE_HOOKS
            || HookConfig.ENABLE_ROOT_BYPASS
            || HookConfig.ENABLE_XPOSED_BYPASS
            || HookConfig.ENABLE_DEBUG_BYPASS
            || HookConfig.ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF
            || HookConfig.ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT
            || HookConfig.ENABLE_RUNTIME_BYPASS;
        if (!anyCoordinatorFeature) {
            nativeLayerAvailable = NativeBypass.isLibraryLoaded();
            nativeLayerEnabled = false;
            XposedEntry.log("All coordinator features disabled; installing no Java/native hooks");
            return;
        }

        // Check Native layer availability
        nativeLayerAvailable = NativeBypass.isLibraryLoaded();
        nativeLayerEnabled = nativeLayerAvailable && HookConfig.ENABLE_NATIVE_HOOKS;

        if (nativeLayerEnabled) {
            XposedEntry.log("Native layer available - Full protection (80%)");
            initNativeIntegration();
        } else if (nativeLayerAvailable) {
            XposedEntry.log("Native hooks disabled by config - Java only (30%)");
        } else {
            XposedEntry.log("Native layer unavailable - Java only (30%)");
        }

        // Install coordinated hooks
        installCoordinatedHooks(lpparam);
    }

    /**
     * Initialize Native integration
     */
    private static void initNativeIntegration() {
        // 先注入配置（探测开关 / 伪造值），再安装 hooks
        NativeBypass.setNativeConfig(HookConfig.PROBE, HookConfig.FAKE_SERIAL,
            HookConfig.FAKE_SOC_SERIAL, HookConfig.FAKE_BOOT_STATE,
            HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_HOST, HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_USER,
            HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_DISPLAY, HookConfig.ROM_BUILD_FINGERPRINT);

        if (HookConfig.ENV_SPOOF) {
            NativeBypass.setEnvProfile(buildEnvProfile());
        }

        // 调用者归因（观测）：必须在 install_hooks 之前生效，否则早期访问没有归属
        NativeBypass.setAttrConfig(HookConfig.ATTR_PROBE, HookConfig.ATTR_DEEP);

        // Initialize Native hooks
        NativeBypass.initNativeHooks();

        // Register callback from Native layer
        registerNativeCallbacks();
    }

    /**
     * Register callbacks for Native → Java communication
     */
    private static String buildEnvProfile() {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        String[] fields = {
            HookConfig.ENV_RELEASE, HookConfig.ENV_SDK, HookConfig.ENV_BUILD_ID,
            HookConfig.ENV_DISPLAY, HookConfig.ENV_FINGERPRINT, HookConfig.ENV_DESCRIPTION,
            HookConfig.ENV_HOST, HookConfig.ENV_USER, HookConfig.ENV_DATE,
            HookConfig.ENV_INCREMENTAL, HookConfig.ENV_SECURITY_PATCH, HookConfig.ENV_OPPOROM,
            HookConfig.ENV_KERNEL_RELEASE, HookConfig.ENV_KERNEL_VERSION,
            HookConfig.ENV_MODEL, HookConfig.ENV_PRODUCT_NAME, HookConfig.ENV_DEVICE,
            HookConfig.ENV_BRAND, HookConfig.ENV_MANUFACTURER
        };
        for (int i = 0; i < fields.length; i++) {
            if (i > 0) sb.append('|');
            sb.append(fields[i] == null ? "" : fields[i]);
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    private static void registerNativeCallbacks() {
        // Native layer can notify Java layer through JNI
        // e.g., when a detection is blocked at Native level
    }

    /**
     * Install hooks with Java/Native coordination
     */
    private static void installCoordinatedHooks(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        // Hook critical detection points with fallback
        hookWithFallback(lpparam);

        // Setup bidirectional communication
        setupCommunicationChannel(lpparam);
    }

    /**
     * Hook with Java → Native fallback
     */
    private static void hookWithFallback(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        // Example: File existence check
        // Java layer hooks File.exists()
        // If it fails, Native layer hooks access()

        HookUtils.hookMethod(java.io.File.class, "exists",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    java.io.File file = (java.io.File) param.thisObject;
                    String path = file.getAbsolutePath();

                    // Check if this is a sensitive path
                    if (isSensitivePath(path)) {
                        param.setResult(false);
                        XposedEntry.log("[Java] Blocked file check: " + path);

                        // Notify Native layer (if available)
                        if (nativeLayerAvailable) {
                            notifyNativeLayer("file_blocked", path);
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    /**
     * Setup bidirectional communication
     */
    private static void setupCommunicationChannel(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        // Java can query Native status
        // Native can notify Java about detections

        if (nativeLayerEnabled) {
            // Check Native hook status periodically
            new Thread(() -> {
                try {
                    Thread.sleep(3000);
                    String status = NativeBypass.getHookStatus();
                    XposedEntry.log("[Coordinator] Native status: " + status);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    XposedEntry.logError("[Coordinator] Status check failed", e);
                }
            }).start();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Notify Native layer about Java detections
     */
    private static void notifyNativeLayer(String event, String data) {
        if (!nativeLayerEnabled) return;

        try {
            // JNI call to Native layer
            NativeBypass.notifyDetection(event, data);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            // Silent fail
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if path is sensitive
     */
    private static boolean isSensitivePath(String path) {
        if (path == null) return false;

        String[] sensitivePaths = {
            "/system/bin/su",
            "/system/xbin/su",
            "/data/adb/magisk",
            "/data/adb/lsposed"
        };

        for (String sensitive : sensitivePaths) {
            if (path.contains(sensitive)) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Get protection level
     */
    public static String getProtectionLevel() {
        if (nativeLayerEnabled && NativeBypass.isHooksInstalled()) {
            return "Full (80%)";
        } else if (nativeLayerEnabled) {
            return "Native loading (60%)";
        } else {
            return "Java only (30%)";
        }
    }
}
```

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/hooks/XposedDetectionHook.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.hooks;

import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;
import de.robv.android.xposed.callbacks.XC_LoadPackage;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.XposedEntry;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;
import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils.HookUtils;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileReader;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Xposed/LSPosed/Hook Framework Detection Bypass
 *
 * Mitigates detection of:
 * - LSPosed/Xposed installer package
 * - Xposed framework artifacts
 * - /proc/self/maps scanning for hook libraries
 * - Cydia Substrate detection
 * - ART method hook detection (limited effectiveness)
 *
 * Note: Native-layer detection (libfekit.so, libturingxq.so) requires native hooks
 * This module can only handle Java-layer checks and file I/O interception
 */
public class XposedDetectionHook {

    private static final String[] RISKY_PACKAGES = {
        // Xposed / LSPosed / Vector 管理器
        "de.robv.android.xposed.installer",
        "org.meowcat.edxposed.manager",
        "io.github.lsposed.manager",

        "io.github.lsposed",
        "io.github.vector.manager",

        "com.topjohnwu.magisk",

        "com.resukisu.resukisu",
        "com.rifsx.susfs",
        "org.sukisu.manager",
        // 改机 / 多开 / 修改器 / 自动化
        // "bin.mt.plus" 不列入：MT 管理器是正常工具，且用户要求保持可见（硬约束）

        "com.lbe.parallel",
        "com.parallel.space",
        "com.qihoo.magic",
        "com.dualspace",
        "com.dual.space",
        "com.multi.parallel",
        "com.applisto.appcloner",
        "com.gg.mod",
        "com.speed.script",
        "org.autojs",
        "com.autojs",
        "com.toucher",
        "com.and.gk",
        "com.scriptelf",
        "com.np.manager",
        // 远控
        // "com.netease.uuremote" 不列入：UU远程是正常工具，且用户要求保持可见（硬约束）
        "com.oray.sunlogin",
        "com.teamviewer",
        // 代理 / 抓包
        "com.github.kr328.clash",
        "io.nekohasekai.sagernet",
        "com.v2ray.ang",
        "com.guoshi.httpcanary",
        "com.httpcanary",
        "com.minhui.networkcapture",
        // Xposed QQ 增强模块与工具链 —— 本机真实装着的这些必须一并隐藏。
        // 依据 ColorOS 自己的 OplusRiskAppCache mRiskApps 清单核对后补齐。
        "me.yxp.qfun",
        "com.luckyzyx.luckytool",

        "io.github.mekhontsev.wallpaperfit",
```
