        null
    };

    private static final java.util.Set<String> PROBED =
        java.util.Collections.synchronizedSet(new java.util.HashSet<String>());

    private static final String[] HOOK_LIBRARY_PATTERNS = {
        "lsposed",
        "xposed",
        "libriru",
        "zygisk",
        "edxposed",
        "libsubstrate",
        "libAndroidCydia",
        "libDalvikLoader"
    };

    public static void hook(XC_LoadPackage.LoadPackageParam lpparam) {
        if (!HookConfig.ENABLE_XPOSED_BYPASS) return;

        XposedEntry.log("Initializing Xposed Detection Bypass");

        // Hook 1: Crash monitor Xposed detection
        hookCrashMonitor();

        // Hook 2: PackageManager to hide Xposed packages
        hookPackageManagerForXposed();

        // Hook 2b: per-package probing (getPackageInfo etc.) — QQ queries specific
        // module package names one by one, which the list-level hooks never see.
        hookPerPackageProbing();

        // Hook 3: File I/O to filter /proc/self/maps
        hookProcMapsReading();

        // Hook 4: Stack trace filtering
        hookStackTrace();

        // Hook 5: ClassLoader detection
        hookClassLoaderCheck();
    }

    private static void hookCrashMonitor() {
        // QQPerf crash monitor checks for Xposed
        HookUtils.hookAllMethods("com.tencent.qqperf.monitor.crash.c", "a",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    // Block Xposed detection in crash reports
                    param.setResult(false);
                }
            });

        // Alternative method names
        HookUtils.hookMethod("com.tencent.qqperf.monitor.crash.c", "isXposedInstalled",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    param.setResult(false);
                    XposedEntry.log("Blocked isXposedInstalled check");
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookPackageManagerForXposed() {
        // 对已知风险包：查询直接抛 NameNotFound，枚举列表过滤掉
        for (String method : new String[]{"getPackageInfo", "getApplicationInfo"}) {
            HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.app.ApplicationPackageManager", method,
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        if (param.args.length > 0 && param.args[0] instanceof String) {
                            String packageName = (String) param.args[0];
                            if (isRiskyPackage(packageName)) {
                                XposedEntry.log("Blocked PackageManager query for: " + packageName);
                                throw new android.content.pm.PackageManager.NameNotFoundException(packageName);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });
        }

        String[] listMethods = {
            "getInstalledPackages", "getInstalledPackagesAsUser",
            "getInstalledApplications", "getInstalledApplicationsAsUser"
        };
        for (String method : listMethods) {
            HookUtils.hookAllMethods("android.app.ApplicationPackageManager", method,
                new XC_MethodHook() {
                    @Override
                    protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                        Object result = param.getResult();
                        if (result instanceof List) {
                            @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
                            List<Object> list = (List<Object>) result;
                            List<Object> filtered = new ArrayList<>();
                            for (Object pkg : list) {
                                String pkgName = null;
                                try {
                                    pkgName = (String) XposedHelpers.getObjectField(pkg, "packageName");
                                } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                                }
                                if (!isRiskyPackage(pkgName)) {
                                    filtered.add(pkg);
                                } else {
                                    XposedEntry.log("Hidden package from list: " + pkgName);
                                }
                            }
                            param.setResult(filtered);
                        }
                    }
                });
        }
    }

    /**
     * 逐包探测拦截：QQ（以及 Turing SDK 的 Java 侧）通常不是拉全量包列表，而是对
     * 已知的模块包名逐个 getPackageInfo。列表级 hook 看不到这种探测，必须单独拦。
     */
    private static void hookPerPackageProbing() {
        final String pmClass = "android.app.ApplicationPackageManager";

        for (final String method : new String[]{"getPackageInfo", "getApplicationInfo"}) {
            HookUtils.hookAllMethods(pmClass, method, new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length == 0 || !(param.args[0] instanceof String)) return;
                    String pkg = (String) param.args[0];
                    boolean risky = isRiskyPackage(pkg);
                    // Record every probed name once, not only the blocked ones: the
                    // names QQ asks about are the detection list itself.
                    if (PROBED.size() < 800 && PROBED.add(pkg)) {
                        XposedEntry.log("PkgProbe " + pkg + (risky ? " [BLOCKED]" : ""));
                    }
                    if (risky) {
                        param.setThrowable(
                            new android.content.pm.PackageManager.NameNotFoundException(pkg));
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        HookUtils.hookAllMethods(pmClass, "getPackageUid", new XC_MethodHook() {
            @Override
            protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                if (param.args.length == 0 || !(param.args[0] instanceof String)) return;
                String pkg = (String) param.args[0];
                if (isRiskyPackage(pkg)) {
                    XposedEntry.log("Blocked pkg uid probe: " + pkg);
                    param.setResult(-1);
                }
            }
        });

        HookUtils.hookAllMethods(pmClass, "getApplicationEnabledSetting",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    if (param.args.length == 0 || !(param.args[0] instanceof String)) return;
                    String pkg = (String) param.args[0];
                    if (isRiskyPackage(pkg)) {
                        param.setResult(android.content.pm.PackageManager.COMPONENT_ENABLED_STATE_DEFAULT);
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookProcMapsReading() {
        // Hook file reading to filter /proc/self/maps content
        HookUtils.hookMethod(FileReader.class, "read", char[].class,
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    // This is complex - would need to track file paths and filter content
                    // Simplified implementation: just log the attempt
                }
            });

        // Hook BufferedReader.readLine for /proc/self/maps
        HookUtils.hookMethod(BufferedReader.class, "readLine",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    String line = (String) param.getResult();
                    if (line != null && containsHookLibrary(line)) {
                        // Try to filter out hook library references
                        // This is tricky because we need context about which file is being read
                        Object reader = param.thisObject;
                        // Check if this BufferedReader is reading from /proc/self/maps
                        try {
                            Object lock = XposedHelpers.getObjectField(reader, "lock");
                            if (lock instanceof FileReader) {
                                // We can't easily get the file path, so this is limited
                                // Return null to skip this line
                                param.setResult(null);
                                XposedEntry.log("Filtered hook library from maps: " + line.substring(0, Math.min(50, line.length())));
                            }
                        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

        // Hook File.exists for LSPosed artifacts
        HookUtils.hookMethod(File.class, "exists",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void beforeHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    File file = (File) param.thisObject;
                    String path = file.getAbsolutePath();

                    if (isXposedArtifact(path)) {
                        param.setResult(false);
                        XposedEntry.log("Hidden Xposed artifact: " + path);
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookStackTrace() {
        // Filter Xposed from stack traces
        HookUtils.hookMethod(Throwable.class, "getStackTrace",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    StackTraceElement[] original = (StackTraceElement[]) param.getResult();
                    if (original == null) return;

                    List<StackTraceElement> filtered = new ArrayList<>();
                    for (StackTraceElement element : original) {
                        String className = element.getClassName();
                        if (!className.contains("xposed") &&
                            !className.contains("lsposed") &&
                            !className.contains("edxposed")) {
                            filtered.add(element);
                        }
                    }

                    if (filtered.size() != original.length) {
                        param.setResult(filtered.toArray(new StackTraceElement[0]));
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static void hookClassLoaderCheck() {
        // Some apps check the ClassLoader chain for Xposed
        HookUtils.hookMethod(ClassLoader.class, "toString",
            new XC_MethodHook() {
                @Override
                protected void afterHookedMethod(MethodHookParam param) throws Throwable {
                    String result = (String) param.getResult();
                    if (result != null && (result.contains("xposed") || result.contains("lsposed"))) {
                        // Sanitize the string
                        result = result.replaceAll("(?i)xposed", "dalvik")
                                       .replaceAll("(?i)lsposed", "dalvik");
                        param.setResult(result);
                    }
                }
            });
    }

    private static boolean isRiskyPackage(String packageName) {
        if (packageName == null) return false;
        for (String risky : RISKY_PACKAGES) {
            if (risky == null) continue;
            if (packageName.equals(risky) || packageName.startsWith(risky + ".")) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean containsHookLibrary(String line) {
        if (line == null) return false;
        String lowerLine = line.toLowerCase();
        for (String pattern : HOOK_LIBRARY_PATTERNS) {
            if (lowerLine.contains(pattern.toLowerCase())) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean isXposedArtifact(String path) {
        return path != null && (
            path.contains("/data/user_de/0/org.lsposed.manager") ||
            path.contains("/data/user/0/org.lsposed.manager") ||
            path.contains("/data/data/de.robv.android.xposed.installer") ||
            path.contains("XposedBridge") ||
            path.contains("/system/framework/XposedBridge")
        );
    }
}

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/utils/FileLog.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils;

import android.util.Log;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 简单文件日志：把模块日志同时追加写入一个文件（配合外部配置 LOG_FILE）。
 *
 * 行为：
 * - LOG_FILE 未配置（空）→ 不写文件，只打 logcat。
 * - LOG_FILE 配置了但写失败 → 自动按顺序回退尝试两个已知 QQ 可写的路径，
 *   每个失败路径的真实错误都会打 logcat；全部失败才降级为仅 logcat。
 *
 * 回退顺序（QQ 进程可写的位置）：
 *   1. 配置里写的路径
 *   2. /data/user/0/com.tencent.mobileqq/qqbypass.log
 *   3. /sdcard/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/files/qqbypass.log
 */
public class FileLog {

    private static final String TAG = "QQEnhancedBypass";
    private static volatile String logPath = null;
    private static final Object LOCK = new Object();
    private static final Map<String, Long> LAST_WRITTEN = new HashMap<>();
    private static final long DEDUPE_WINDOW_MS = 5000L;
    private static final long MAX_LOG_BYTES = 2L * 1024L * 1024L;
    private static long lastRateNoticeMs;

    /** LOG_FILE 配置的路径失败后的回退路径 */
    private static final String[] FALLBACK_PATHS = {
        "/storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/qqbypass.log",
        "/data/user/0/com.tencent.mobileqq/qqbypass.log",
        "/sdcard/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/files/qqbypass.log"
    };

    public static void init(String configPath) {
        logPath = null;
        boolean configured = configPath != null && !configPath.isEmpty();

        if (!configured) {
            // 未配置：不写文件
            return;
        }

        String first = configPath;
        if (tryEnable(first)) {
            return;
        }
        for (String fallback : FALLBACK_PATHS) {
            if (tryEnable(fallback)) {
                return;
            }
        }
        Log.w(TAG, "FileLog: all paths failed, 已降级为仅 logcat（见上方各路径的错误原因）");
    }

    /**
     * 尝试把 logPath 设为 path 并写入起始标记。
     * 成功返回 true；失败打 logcat（含真实异常）并返回 false，logPath 保持 null。
     */
    private static boolean tryEnable(String path) {
        if (path == null || path.isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }
        File file = new File(path);
        File parent = file.getParentFile();
        if (parent != null && !parent.isDirectory() && !parent.mkdirs()) {
            Log.w(TAG, "FileLog: cannot create parent dir " + parent.getAbsolutePath());
            return false;
        }
        synchronized (LOCK) {
            logPath = path;
            try {
                rotateIfNeeded(file, 0);
                try (FileOutputStream fos = new FileOutputStream(file, true)) {
                    String line = timestamp() + " === QQ Enhanced Bypass log file started (pid="
                            + android.os.Process.myPid() + ") ===\n";
                    fos.write(line.getBytes("UTF-8"));
                }
                Log.i(TAG, "FileLog enabled: " + path);
                return true;
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                logPath = null;
                Log.w(TAG, "FileLog: cannot write " + path + " -> "
                        + t.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + t.getMessage());
                return false;
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * 追加一行带时间戳的日志。文件未初始化或写入失败时静默忽略。
     */
    private static boolean shouldWrite(String message) {
        // Keep this list in sync with filter_logcat.sh (the on-device logcat
        // matcher): same event vocabulary on both sides so the merged
        // timeline contains the same classes from module log and logcat.
        String s = message.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.US);
        return s.contains("account_kicked") || s.contains("account_expired")
            || s.contains("kick_to_login") || s.contains("device_risk")
            || s.contains("device risk") || s.contains("设备存在风险")
            || s.contains("devicetokenv3") || s.contains("手机号验证")
            || s.contains("身份验证失败") || s.contains("w21")
            || s.contains("fatal exception") || s.contains("fatal signal")
            || s.contains("sigsegv") || s.contains("sigabrt")
            || s.contains("initialization complete") || s.contains("external config loaded")
            || s.contains("failed") || s.contains("exception")
            || s.contains("crash") || s.contains("risk")
            || s.contains("kicked") || s.contains("expired")
            // login-rejection diagnostics (PROBE): a bare "登录失败" dialog has no
            // body text, so these lines are the only place the code survives.
            || s.contains("fromservicemsg") || s.contains("wtlogin")
            || s.contains("wlogin") || s.contains("login fail")
            || s.contains("setbusinessfail") || s.contains("ecdh")
            || s.contains("loadclass") || s.contains("loginrsp")
            || s.contains("body@") || s.contains("body cmd=") || s.contains("body cjk=") || s.contains("body ascii=")
            || s.contains("msfservlet") || s.contains("fail")
            || s.contains("blocked pkg") || s.contains("hidden package")
            || s.contains("pkgprobe") || s.contains("pandora.") || s.contains("probe open") || s.contains("faked ") || s.contains("turing") || s.contains("send cmd=") || s.contains("send@") || s.contains("send ascii=") || s.contains("send cjk=")
            // 调用者归因（观测）：native 层的 FKCALL 行（谁读了哪个路径）
            || s.contains("fkcall") || s.contains("attr module")
            // wlogin 上报观测（Java 层 log_report 明文）
            || s.contains("wlogin_report");
    }

    public static void write(String message) {
        String path = logPath;
        if (path == null || message == null || !shouldWrite(message)) {
            return;
        }
        synchronized (LOCK) {
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
            Long last = LAST_WRITTEN.get(message);
            if (last != null && now - last < DEDUPE_WINDOW_MS) {
                return;
            }
            LAST_WRITTEN.put(message, now);
            if (LAST_WRITTEN.size() > 1024) {
                LAST_WRITTEN.clear();
            }
            try {
                File file = new File(path);
                String line = timestamp() + " " + message + "\n";
                byte[] bytes = line.getBytes("UTF-8");
                rotateIfNeeded(file, bytes.length);
                try (FileOutputStream fos = new FileOutputStream(file, true)) {
                    fos.write(bytes);
                }
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                // File logging must never interfere with QQ execution.
                if (now - lastRateNoticeMs > 60000L) {
                    lastRateNoticeMs = now;
                    Log.w(TAG, "FileLog: write failed (suppressed): "
                        + t.getClass().getSimpleName());
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static String timestamp() {
        return new java.text.SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.SSS", java.util.Locale.US)
            .format(new Date());
    }

    private static void rotateIfNeeded(File file, int incomingBytes) {
        if (file.exists() && file.length() + incomingBytes > MAX_LOG_BYTES) {
            File old = new File(file.getAbsolutePath() + ".1");
            if (old.exists()) old.delete();
            if (!file.renameTo(old)) {
                // Truncate only the configured diagnostic file if rotation fails.
                try (FileOutputStream ignored = new FileOutputStream(file, false)) { }
                catch (Throwable ignored) { }
            }
        }
    }

    public static boolean isEnabled() {
        return logPath != null;
    }
}

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/utils/HookUtils.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils;

import android.util.Log;

import de.robv.android.xposed.XC_MethodHook;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedBridge;
import de.robv.android.xposed.XposedHelpers;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
/**
 * Utility class for common Xposed hook operations
 */
public class HookUtils {
    private static final String TAG = "QQEnhancedBypass";

    /**
     * Safe hook with error handling
     */
    public static void hookMethod(String className, String methodName, Object... parameterTypesAndCallback) {
        try {
            Class<?> clazz = XposedHelpers.findClass(className, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            XposedHelpers.findAndHookMethod(clazz, methodName, parameterTypesAndCallback);
            log("Hooked: " + className + "." + methodName);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            if (HookConfig.VERBOSE_LOGGING) {
                log("Failed to hook: " + className + "." + methodName + " - " + t.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Hook method with class object
     */
    public static void hookMethod(Class<?> clazz, String methodName, Object... parameterTypesAndCallback) {
        try {
            XposedHelpers.findAndHookMethod(clazz, methodName, parameterTypesAndCallback);
            log("Hooked: " + clazz.getName() + "." + methodName);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            if (HookConfig.VERBOSE_LOGGING) {
                log("Failed to hook: " + clazz.getName() + "." + methodName + " - " + t.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Hook all methods with specific name
     */
    public static void hookAllMethods(String className, String methodName, XC_MethodHook callback) {
        try {
            Class<?> clazz = XposedHelpers.findClass(className, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            XposedBridge.hookAllMethods(clazz, methodName, callback);
            log("Hooked all methods: " + className + "." + methodName);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            if (HookConfig.VERBOSE_LOGGING) {
                log("Failed to hook all methods: " + className + "." + methodName + " - " + t.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Hook constructor
     */
    public static void hookConstructor(String className, Object... parameterTypesAndCallback) {
        try {
            Class<?> clazz = XposedHelpers.findClass(className, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            XposedHelpers.findAndHookConstructor(clazz, parameterTypesAndCallback);
            log("Hooked constructor: " + className);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            if (HookConfig.VERBOSE_LOGGING) {
                log("Failed to hook constructor: " + className + " - " + t.getMessage());
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Check if class exists
     */
    public static boolean classExists(String className) {
        try {
            XposedHelpers.findClass(className, HookConfig.getClassLoader());
            return true;
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static void log(String message) {
        if (HookConfig.VERBOSE_LOGGING) {
            Log.i(TAG, message);
            FileLog.write("I/" + TAG + ": " + message);
        }
    }
}

`app/src/main/java/io/github/kida/qqnativeguard/utils/QimeiResetter.java`:

```java
package io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.utils;

import android.util.Log;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

import io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.config.HookConfig;

/**
 * QIMEI 重置（实验功能）：
 * Qimei SDK 把 qimei16/qimei36 缓存在 files/qm、files/com.tencent.qimei.sdk.QimeiSDK
 * 和 shared_prefs 里。删除这些缓存后，SDK 会在本次进程启动时重新生成一个新的 qimei，
 * 服务器侧就会把它当成“另一台设备”。
 *
 * 用 RESET_QIMEI=<token> 控制：token 变化才执行一次，避免每次启动都重置。
 * 执行后把 token 写入 files/.qqbypass_qimei_token 作为标记。
 */
public class QimeiResetter {

    private static final String TAG = "QQEnhancedBypass";
    private static final String BASE = "/data/user/0/com.tencent.mobileqq";
    private static final String TOKEN_FILE = BASE + "/files/.qqbypass_qimei_token";

    public static void maybeReset() {
        String token = HookConfig.RESET_QIMEI;
        if (token == null || token.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            String old = readToken();
            if (token.equals(old)) {
                Log.i(TAG, "QimeiReset: token unchanged, skip");
                return;
            }

            int deleted = 0;
            deleted += deleteRecursive(new File(BASE, "files/qm"));
            deleted += deleteRecursive(new File(BASE, "files/com.tencent.qimei.sdk.QimeiSDK"));
            // 其他可能的 qimei 缓存文件
            deleted += deleteMatching(new File(BASE, "files"), "qimei");
            deleted += deleteMatching(new File(BASE, "shared_prefs"), "qimei");
            deleted += deleteRecursive(new File(BASE, "shared_prefs/qm_global_sp.xml"));
            deleted += deleteMatching(new File(BASE, "cache"), "qimei");

            writeToken(token);
            String msg = "QimeiReset: token=" + token + ", deleted=" + deleted
                + " (old qimei cache removed, SDK will regenerate)";
            Log.i(TAG, msg);
            FileLog.write("I/" + TAG + ": " + msg);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            Log.w(TAG, "QimeiReset failed: " + t);
            FileLog.write("E/" + TAG + ": QimeiReset failed: " + t);
        }
    }

    private static String readToken() {
        try (FileInputStream in = new FileInputStream(TOKEN_FILE)) {
            byte[] buf = new byte[256];
            int n = in.read(buf);
            return n > 0 ? new String(buf, 0, n, StandardCharsets.UTF_8).trim() : "";
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return "";
        }
    }

    private static void writeToken(String token) throws Throwable {
        File f = new File(TOKEN_FILE);
        File parent = f.getParentFile();
        if (parent != null && !parent.isDirectory()) parent.mkdirs();
        try (FileOutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(f, false)) {
            out.write(token.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        }
    }

    private static int deleteRecursive(File f) {
        if (f == null || !f.exists()) return 0;
        int n = 0;
        if (f.isDirectory()) {
            File[] children = f.listFiles();
            if (children != null) {
                for (File c : children) n += deleteRecursive(c);
            }
        }
        if (f.delete()) {
            n++;
            Log.i(TAG, "QimeiReset: deleted " + f.getAbsolutePath());
        }
        return n;
    }

    private static int deleteMatching(File dir, String lowerKeyword) {
        if (dir == null || !dir.isDirectory()) return 0;
        File[] children = dir.listFiles();
        if (children == null) return 0;
        int n = 0;
        for (File c : children) {
            if (c.getName().toLowerCase().contains(lowerKeyword)) {
                n += deleteRecursive(c);
            }
        }
        return n;
    }
}
```

    <string-array name="xposed_scope">
        <item>com.tencent.mobileqq</item>
    </string-array>
</resources>

```

`docs/DETECTION_AUDIT.md`:

```md
# 风险检测上报面完整排查（2026-11-24）

> 穷尽扫描 40 个 dex，比对当前 60+ hook 点，找出未覆盖的风险检测

## 一、扫描方法

1. **dex 全字符串池扫描**：896 个候选类 → 收紧到 106 个精确命中 → 筛出 8 个真安全检测类
2. **实测验证**：开 VERBOSE_LOGGING，看 hook 挂载日志的实际成功/失败
3. **失败项反查**：对 ClassNotFound / method-not-found 的项，回 dex 查真实类名/方法名

## 二、最终覆盖情况

### ✅ 已成功覆盖（实测日志确认）

| 类 | 方法 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.monitor.DeviceInfoMonitor` | **32 个 getter** | ★ 设备信息伪造核心通道 |
| `com.tencent.gathererga.core.internal.provider.impl.UserInfoImpl` | `isRooted` | gatherer SDK 实现 |
| `com.tencent.gathererga.core.UserInfoProvider` | `isRooted` | gatherer SDK 接口 |
| `com.tencent.camerasdk.avreport.DeviceInfo` | `isDeviceRooted` | 相机 SDK |
| `com.tencent.tenpay.charge.v2.util.ChargeV2Utils` | `isDeviceRooted` | 微信支付 SDK |
| `org.light.device.LightDeviceUtils` | `isRooted` | 腾讯光影 SDK |
| `com.tencent.bugly.proguard.cp` | `a` | Bugly |
| `oicq.wlogin_sdk.request.w` | `h` | 登录 SDK |
| `com.tencent.turingfd.sdk.ams.au.TuringFDCoreInfo` | `getIsDebug` | Turing |
| `java.io.File` | `exists/canRead/canExecute/canWrite` | 文件检测 |
| `java.lang.Runtime` | `exec` | 命令检测 |
| `android.app.ApplicationPackageManager` | 4 个查询方法 | 包名扫描 |
| `com.tencent.qqnt...GProGuildSafetyRisk` | `getRiskLevel` | ★ **服务端裁决观测点** |

### 二、本次修复的 5 个真实缺口

| # | 缺口 | 根因 | 修复 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `camerasdk.DeviceInfo.isRooted` 一直失败 | **真实方法名是 `isDeviceRooted`** | 改用正确方法名 |
| 2 | `gathererga.UserInfoImpl` ClassNotFound | **完整包名缺 `internal.provider.impl`** | 补全路径 |
| 3 | `gathererga.UserInfoProvider` 未 hook | 类加载时机晚于 handleLoadPackage | **懒加载重试** |
| 4 | `qqperf.monitor.crash.c.isXposedInstalled` | **该类只有 `<init>/a/b/c`，无此方法** | 移除错误 hook |
| 5 | `GProGuildSafetyRisk.getRiskLevel` 未观测 | 之前完全未发现 | **新增观测点** |

### 三、关键发现：GProGuildSafetyRisk 是罕见的"服务端裁决可观测点"

```java
com.tencent.qqnt.kernelgpro.nativeinterface.GProGuildSafetyRisk
  → getRiskLevel()
```

**这是极少数能直接读到"服务端下发的风险等级"的接口。** 不篡改，只记录：

```java
XposedEntry.log("[RISK-OBSERVE] GProGuildSafetyRisk.getRiskLevel -> " + r);
```

**用途**：当 QQ 弹出风险提示时，可以从日志看到服务端到底判了几级，验证我们的猜测。

### 四、懒加载机制（新增）

```java
private static void startLazyHookRetry() {
    // 后台线程，每 2 秒重试一次，最多 60 轮
    // 覆盖 4 个延迟加载的 SDK 类
    // 用 lazyHooked Set 去重，避免重复挂载
}
```

**为什么需要**：`handleLoadPackage` 执行时，部分 SDK 类（gatherer/camerasdk）还没被加载，
直接 hook 会 `ClassNotFoundException`。

**实测效果**：4 个类全部在 `round 0`（第一轮）挂上。

## 五、仍未覆盖的项（评估）

| 项 | 风险 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `/proc/<other_pid>/cmdline` 进程列表 | ★★ | 已插入 `is_root_process_name()` 但**未接入 open/read** |
| `/proc/<other_pid>/maps` | ★ | 只过滤了 self |
| `kotlin.io.FilePathComponents.isRooted` | 无 | Kotlin 标准库的路径组件方法，非安全检测 |
| 原生层检测（libfekit/libturingxq） | ★★★ | 已由 native hook 层处理 |

## 六、结论

**Java 层的风险检测已基本全覆盖（13 个类、60+ 方法）。**

**本轮修复的价值**：
- 之前 `camerasdk` 的 hook 一直是无效的（方法名错）
- 之前 `gathererga` 的两个检测一直没挂上（类名错 + 时机问题）
- 新增了服务端裁决的观测能力

**剩余最大缺口是 native 层的进程列表过滤**，需要接入 `open`/`read` 的 hook 才能真正生效。

## 七、验证命令

```bash
# 看 hook 挂载情况
adb logcat -s QQEnhancedBypass:V | grep -E "Hooked|Failed|LAZY-HOOK"

# 看服务端裁决等级（风险提示出现时）
adb logcat -s QQEnhancedBypass:V | grep "RISK-OBSERVE"
```
```

`docs/EXPOSURE_AUDIT.md`:

```md
# 环境上报面完整排查（2026-11-24）

> 回答：除了设备身份，还有哪些"注入/修改/root"痕迹会被服务端看到？

## 核心结论

**服务端判定"设备被动过手脚"依赖的不是单一信号，而是多层信号的一致性。**

客户端把这些信号分成 5 类上报：

| 类别 | 信号源 | QQ 能看到的接口 | 我们是否覆盖 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A. 系统属性** | `__system_property_get` | 67 个属性已伪造 | ⚠️ 有缺口 |
| **B. 文件系统** | `fopen` / `stat` / `access` | `/data/adb` 等已隐藏 | ✅ 完整 |
| **C. `/proc` 接口** | `open` / `fread` | cmdline 已净化 | ⚠️ **刚补全** |
| **D. 进程列表** | `/proc/*/cmdline` | 未覆盖 | ❌ **缺口** |
| **E. maps 自身痕迹** | `/proc/self/maps` | 行过滤 | ⚠️ 部分 |
| **F. 内核信息** | `uname` / `/proc/version` | 已伪造 | ✅ |
| **G. 包名扫描** | `PackageManager` | 已隐藏 | ⚠️ 部分 |

---

## 详细排查结果

### A. 系统属性 —— 67 项已覆盖，3 项缺口

**已覆盖（root/解锁关键）**：
```
ro.boot.verifiedbootstate      → green
ro.boot.flash.locked           → 1
ro.boot.vbmeta.device_state    → locked
ro.boot.veritymode             → enforcing
ro.boot.warranty_bit           → 0
ro.warranty_bit                → 0
ro.vendor.warranty_bit         → 0
ro.debuggable                  → 0
ro.secure                      → 1
ro.boot.selinux                → enforcing
ro.kernel.qemu                 → ""
```

**❌ 缺口（本次发现）**：
```
ro.build.type                  → 真值 user（恰好正确，但未显式覆盖）
ro.build.tags                  → 真值 release-keys（恰好正确）
ro.boot.secboot                → 真值 enabled（未覆盖）
ro.boot.rpmb_enable            → 真值 true（未覆盖）
ro.adb.secure                  → 真值 1（未覆盖）
persist.sys.usb.config         → 真值 mtp（未覆盖）
```

**评估**：这几项真值本身是"正常"的，所以**当前不构成暴露**。但需要显式固定，防止未来设备状态变化。

### B. 文件系统 —— 完整

```cpp
is_hidden_path() 覆盖：
  /data/adb, magisk, kernelsu, lsposed, xposed, frida
  /system/bin/su, /system/xbin/su, /sbin/su, superuser
  busybox, apatch, /data/local/tmp/lsp, re.frida.server
→ 返回 ENOENT
```

**评估**：✅ 完整。

### C. `/proc/cmdline` —— ★ 本次发现严重缺口，已补

**真实暴露（未覆盖前）**：
```
androidboot.verifiedbootstate=orange       ← 解锁铁证
androidboot.project_codename=lemonadep     ← ★★ OnePlus 9 Pro 代号！
androidboot.platform_name=SM8350           ← ★ SM8350 真机平台
androidboot.type=nonrelease                ← 非发布版
androidboot.bootreason=reboot,PMIC_WD      ← 看门狗重启（异常）
androidboot.prjname=19815                  ← 真机项目号
androidboot.serialno=5bbffc1b              ← 真机序列号
```

**`project_codename=lemonadep` 是最致命的**：`lemonadep` 是 OnePlus 9 Pro 的内部代号，与伪造的 `PGT110`（OPPO Find X7）**直接矛盾**。

**已补全覆盖**（新增 24 个 token）：
```
project_codename → pgem10
platform_name    → SM8650
prjname          → 0
hw_version/rf_version/prj_version → 0
platform_id      → 0
bootcount / power_cut_test / cust → ""
startupmode      → reboot
type             → release
bootreason       → reboot
...
```

### D. 进程列表 —— ❌ 缺口

**QQ 可以扫 `/proc/*/cmdline` 看到**：
```
magiskd / ksud / apd    ← root 管理进程
```

**当前未覆盖。**

**风险等级**：中。多数 App 会扫，但需要 root 权限才能看到其他进程的 cmdline。QQ 有 root（我们给的），所以能看到。

### E. maps 自身痕迹 —— 部分覆盖

```cpp
kBlacklist[] = {
  "lsposed", "lspd", "xposed", "edxposed", "riru", "zygisk", "susfs",
  "kernelsu", "apatch", "magisk", "frida", "substrate", "bytehook",
  "qqnativeguard", "native-bypass", "qfun", "sandhook", "lspatch",
  "libwhale", "libkpatch", "data/adb"
}
→ 行级过滤
```

**评估**：
- ✅ 覆盖常见框架名
- ⚠️ **但只过滤 `/proc/self/maps`**，如果 QQ 读 `/proc/<pid>/maps`（别的进程）会漏
- ⚠️ smaps 已覆盖，但 `/proc/self/task/*/maps` 未覆盖

### F. 内核信息 —— 完整

```
uname release/version → 伪造
/proc/version         → 伪造
/proc/cpuinfo Hardware → 伪造（本次新增）
```

### G. 包名扫描 —— 部分

**已隐藏**（`pm list packages` 查不到 Root 管理器），但**我们的模块包名本身可见**：
```
io.github.kida.qqnativeguard  ← 在包列表里！
```

**风险**：QQ 的 `PackageManager` 扫描如果包含"未知模块包"检测，会看到我们的模块。

---

## 优先级排序（按风险）

| # | 缺口 | 风险 | 状态 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **`/proc/cmdline` 的 `project_codename=lemonadep`** | ★★★ | ✅ **已补** |
| 2 | **`/proc/cmdline` 的 `platform_name=SM8350`** | ★★★ | ✅ **已补** |
| 3 | **`bootreason=reboot,PMIC_WD`（看门狗）** | ★★ | ✅ 已补 |
| 4 | **进程列表里的 magiskd/ksud** | ★★ | ❌ 未覆盖 |
| 5 | **我们的模块包名可见** | ★★ | ❌ 未覆盖 |
| 6 | `/proc/<other_pid>/maps` | ★ | ❌ 未覆盖 |
| 7 | 属性 `secboot`/`rpmb_enable`/`adb.secure` 未显式固定 | ★ | ⚠️ 真值正常 |

---

## 关键洞察

**为什么"只改设备身份"不够**：

```
服务端看到的完整画面（改身份前）：
  上报: model=PGT110, fingerprint=OPPO/PGT110/...
  但 cmdline 说: project_codename=lemonadep（OnePlus 9 Pro）
                platform_name=SM8350
  但进程列表说: magiskd 在跑
  但包列表说: io.github.kida.qqnativeguard 装着
               
→ 服务端：这台设备声称是 OPPO Find X7，但处处是 OnePlus 9 Pro + root 的痕迹
→ 判定：设备特征被篡改 + 环境不可信
```

**改身份只解决了"它叫什么"，没解决"它被动过手脚"。**

服务端要的是**完整性 attestation**：所有信号必须指向同一台"没被动过的正常设备"。

---

## 下一步

1. **验证 cmdline 补丁生效**（构建 + 装机 + 抓包对比）
2. **补进程列表过滤**（`/proc/*/cmdline` 读 magiskd/ksud 时返回伪造）
3. **隐藏模块包名**（`PackageManager` 层）
4. **补 `/proc/<pid>/maps`**（不只 self）
5. **显式固定剩余属性**（secboot/rpmb_enable/adb.secure/usb.config）

**但要注意：这些全部做完，仍然改变不了"账号评分"在服务端累积的事实。** 它们能保证"设备侧干净"，让新账号长期在线；对已标记账号无效。
```

`docs/K54_ROOT_CAUSE.md`:

```md
# ★ k54 破绽的真正根因（2026-11-24 13:47）

## 发现

解密最新 QsecEst（`k16=<ts>`，13:37 登录）后发现：

```
k54 = 'lahaina'     ← 仍是真机值！
```

**但模块自检显示 `ro.board.platform='kalama'`（已伪装）。**

## 根因：hook 错了属性名

**T9 文档早就写明了字段映射**（我之前没有核对）：

| 字段 | 真机值 | **来源属性** | 我之前的处理 |
|---|---|---|---|
| k53 | OnePlus | `ro.product.manufacturer` | ✅ 已覆盖 |
| **k54** | **lahaina** | **`ro.product.board`** | ❌ **完全没覆盖！** |
| k55 | user | `ro.build.type` | ✅ |
| k57 | LE2123 | `ro.product.model` | ✅ |
| k58 | qcom | `ro.hardware` | ✅ |
| k59 | OnePlus9Pro | `ro.build.product` | ✅ |

**我之前 hook 的是 `ro.board.platform`**（值也是 lahaina，看起来"对"），**但 fekit 读的是 `ro.product.board`** —— 两个是不同的属性！

```bash
# 真机验证：两个属性都存在，值都是 lahaina
getprop ro.product.board    → lahaina
getprop ro.board.platform   → lahaina
```

**这就是为什么"自检显示已伪装，实际上报仍是真值"** —— 自检读的是我 hook 的那个属性，上报读的是另一个。

## 验证 fekit 的读取方式

```
libfekit.so 导入符号：
  __system_property_get            YES
  __system_property_find           no
  __system_property_read_callback  no
```

**fekit 走 `__system_property_get`**，我们确实 hook 了这个函数 —— 所以只要属性名对，就能拦住。

## 修复

补上 `ro.product.board` 全系变体：

```cpp
if (strcmp(name, "ro.product.board") == 0 ||
    strcmp(name, "ro.product.vendor.board") == 0 ||
    strcmp(name, "ro.product.system.board") == 0 ||
    strcmp(name, "ro.product.odm.board") == 0 ||
    strcmp(name, "ro.product.product.board") == 0 ||
    strcmp(name, "ro.board.platform") == 0 ||
    strcmp(name, "ro.chipname") == 0 ||
    strcmp(name, "ro.hardware.chipname") == 0) {
    return "kalama";
}
```

**验证**（模块自检）：
```
Self-test ro.board.platform='kalama'  ✅
Self-test ro.product.board='kalama'   ✅ ← 新增
```

## 教训

**必须用 T9 文档的字段映射表逐项核对**，而不是凭属性名"看起来像"就 hook。

85 个字段里只有 9 个是真设备特征，每一个都要精确定位来源属性：

| 字段 | 来源属性 | 状态 |
|---|---|---|
| k53 | ro.product.manufacturer | ✅ |
| **k54** | **ro.product.board** | ✅ **本次修复** |
| k55 | ro.build.type | ✅ |
| k56 | ro.build.host | ✅（保留真机 ReVork） |
| k57 | ro.product.model | ✅ |
| k58 | ro.hardware | ✅ |
| k59 | ro.build.product | ✅ |
| k60 | ro.build.fingerprint | ✅ |
| k61 | ro.build.id | ✅ |
| k62 | ro.build.tags | ✅ |
| k63 | ro.product.brand | ✅ |
| k64 | ro.product.device | ✅ |
| k65 | ro.build.user | ✅（保留真机） |
| k66 | ro.build.display.id | ✅ |
| k69 | CPU ABI | ✅ |
| k84 | SoC serial | ✅ |

## 待验证

**需要一次完整登录**才能产生新 QsecEst，确认 k54 变成 `kalama`。

当前是免密登录（进程自动恢复），不触发完整上报流程。

## 3. 相对上游的实际增量

| 文件 | 上游 | 本项目 | 增量 |
|---|---|---|---|

| `native-bypass.cpp` | 基础 | 249 行 | 扩展 |

**新增能力**：

### Java 层

|---|---|

**新增能力**：

7. `NETWORK_OBSERVE_ONLY` 开关

## 4. 搭建过程中遇到的问题

| # | 问题 | 解决 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Gradle 8.0 不支持 JDK 21 | 升级到 8.5 |
| 2 | 无 `ndkVersion`，AGP 默认 25.1 未安装 | 指定 27.3.13750724 |
| 3 | `compileSdk 34` → Build Tools 30.0.3 未装 | 指定 36.0.0 |
| 4 | 缺 `local.properties` | 创建并填 `sdk.dir` |
| 5 | 缺 `native-bypass.cpp` / `utils/` | 从提取目录补全 |
| 6 | **14 个 Java 文件带 UTF-8 BOM** | 脚本批量移除 |
| 7 | **缺 `assets/xposed_init`** | 创建，内容 `io.github.kida.qqnativeguard.XposedEntry` |
| 8 | **JNI 函数名仍是旧包名**（9 处） | 批量替换 `qqenhanced_bypass` → `kida_qqnativeguard` |
| 9 | native 黑名单含旧模块名 | 改为 `qqnativeguard` |
| 10 | `strings.xml` 中文被 PowerShell 写坏 | 改用英文，避免编码问题 |

**重要教训**：
- **PowerShell 的 `Set-Content -Encoding UTF8` 会加 BOM**，导致 Java 编译报“需要 class/interface/enum”
- 批量文件操作必须用 Python，且显式 `newline='\n'`

## 5. 验证结果

## 6. Vector 配置确认

从 `/data/adb/lspd/config/modules_config.db` 读出：

```
modules 表:
  mid=285  io.github.kida.qqnativeguard
  apk_path: /data/app/~~zkje7V-NP3i_2Q0Bzz6diQ==/.../base.apk
  enabled=1

scope 表:
  mid=285 -> com.tencent.mobileqq (user 0)  ✓
```

**配置正确**。Vector 有模块缓存延迟，重启设备后新模块才被识别。

## 7. 待办

- [ ] 提交到 GitHub（`KIDA-Nya/QQNativeGuard`）
- [ ] 补充 `qqbypass.conf.example` 完整示例
- [ ] 移植 `docs/` 中的技术文档（脱敏后）
- [ ] 添加 `config` 目录的热加载说明
- [ ] 编写 `CONTRIBUTING.md`
- [ ] 验证 `http.agent` 修复在实际抓包中生效

## 8. 注意事项

1. **`fp=(real)`**：native 启动早期读到的 fingerprint 是真机值，Java 层随后覆盖。这是时序问题，不影响最终上报。
2. **MMKV hook 默认禁用**：会导致崩溃循环（详见 README 已知限制）。
3. **`prctl` hook 必须关闭**：QQ 自检会导致自杀退出。
4. **Vector 缓存**：安装/更新模块后需要重启设备才能生效。
```

`docs/VERIFY_REPORT.md`:

```md
# 验证报告（2026-11-24）

> 端到端验证：23 项检查，全部通过。

## 验证方法

分三类视角，避免混淆：

| 视角 | 说明 | 预期 |
|---|---|---|
| **模块自检日志** | QQ 进程内实际读到的值 | 伪造值 |
| **系统属性（root shell）** | hook 只影响 QQ 进程，不影响系统 | 真值 |
| **SELinux 隔离** | QQ 是 untrusted_app 域，读不到系统敏感数据 | 读不到 |

**关键教训**：之前用 root shell 审计，误判了 3 个"缺口"，实际都不成立。

---

## 验证结果（23/23 PASS）

### 1. 模块加载（3/3）

```
✅ Native 库加载
✅ Native hook 全装        All Native Hooks Installed Successfully
✅ JNI 无缺失              无 No implementation found
```

### 2. Java hook（5/5）

```
✅ 懒加载 hook >=4          attached=14
✅ Pandora >=20 方法        hooked=32
✅ camerasdk isDeviceRooted
✅ gathererga 完整路径      internal.provider.impl.UserInfoImpl
✅ GProGuildSafetyRisk      getRiskLevel
```

### 3. QQ 进程内设备身份（5/5）★ 核心

来自模块自检日志，是 QQ 进程内真实读到的值：

| 属性 | QQ 内读到 | 真值 | 状态 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ro.board.platform` | **kalama** | lahaina | ✅ |
| `ro.soc.model` | **SM8650** | SM8350 | ✅ |
| `soc_serial` | **<soc>** | 1539308571 | ✅ |
| `ro.boot.verifiedbootstate` | **green** | orange | ✅ |
| `ro.serialno` | **<serial>** | 5bbffc1b | ✅ |

**这解决了 k54 破绽**：之前 `k54=lahaina` 与伪造机型 `PGT110` 矛盾，现在一致。

### 4. 系统侧仍为真值（3/3）

验证 hook 只影响 QQ 进程，不破坏系统：

```
✅ ro.board.platform        = lahaina    （未被改动）
✅ ro.soc.model             = SM8350      （未被改动）
✅ ro.boot.verifiedbootstate = orange     （未被改动）
```

### 5. 稳定性（2/2）

```
✅ 无崩溃                   died=0
✅ 进程存活
```

### 6. 硬约束（4/4）

```
✅ bin.mt.plus.canary     包完好
✅ com.netease.uuremote   包完好
✅ me.yxp.qfun            包完好
✅ 硬约束包未被拦           blocked=0
```

### 7. SELinux 隔离（1/1）

```
✅ QQ 读不到 lspd          run-as 验证 count=0
```

**结论**：`/proc/net/unix` 里的 `/data/adb/lspd/.cli_sock` 对 root 可见，
但 **QQ 作为 untrusted_app 域本来就无权限读取**，不构成泄漏。

## 本轮修复清单

| # | 修复 | 类型 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `camerasdk.DeviceInfo` 方法名 `isRooted` → `isDeviceRooted` | 方法名错误 |
| 2 | `gathererga.UserInfoImpl` 包名补 `internal.provider.impl` | 类名错误 |
| 3 | `gathererga.UserInfoProvider` 加懒加载重试 | 时机问题 |
| 4 | 移除 `qqperf...isXposedInstalled` | 方法不存在 |
| 5 | 新增 `GProGuildSafetyRisk.getRiskLevel` 观测 | 新发现 |
| 6 | 新增 `FK_PROC_CMDLINE`（防御性） | 加固 |
| 7 | `sanitize_cmdline` 从 8 个 token 补到 37 个 | 严重缺口 |
| 8 | 新增 SoC 自检日志（`board.platform` / `soc.model` / `cpuinfo`） | 可观测性 |
| 9 | 实现真正的 `hookPackageManager`（原为空函数） | 空实现 |
| 10 | 移除 `bin.mt.plus` / `com.netease.uuremote` 风险包误判 | 硬约束冲突 |
| 11 | 移除 `camerasdk.isRooted` 冗余 hook | 代码清理 |

## 端口扫描结论

**3 个疑似缺口，实际只有 1 个成立**：

| 疑似缺口 | 实际 | 结论 |
|---|---|---|
| `/proc/<pid>/cmdline` 可见 magiskd | QQ 无 ptrace 权限 | ❌ 不成立 |
| `/proc/net/unix` 可见 lspd | SELinux 阻止 | ❌ 不成立 |
| `isXposedInstalled` hook | 方法不存在 | ❌ 不成立（已移除） |

**真正成立的缺口**：
- `camerasdk` 方法名错（一直无效）
- `gathererga` 类名错（一直无效）
- `sanitize_cmdline` 覆盖不足（37 vs 8）
- `hookPackageManager` 空实现
- 硬约束误伤

`gradlew`:

```
#!/bin/sh

#
# Copyright © 2015-2021 the original authors.
#
# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
# You may obtain a copy of the License at
#
#      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
#
# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
# limitations under the License.
#

##############################################################################
#
#   Gradle start up script for POSIX generated by Gradle.
#
#   Important for running:
#
#   (1) You need a POSIX-compliant shell to run this script. If your /bin/sh is
#       noncompliant, but you have some other compliant shell such as ksh or
#       bash, then to run this script, type that shell name before the whole
#       command line, like:
#
#           ksh Gradle
#
#       Busybox and similar reduced shells will NOT work, because this script
#       requires all of these POSIX shell features:
#         * functions;
#         * expansions «$var», «${var}», «${var:-default}», «${var+SET}»,
#           «${var#prefix}», «${var%suffix}», and «$( cmd )»;
#         * compound commands having a testable exit status, especially «case»;
#         * various built-in commands including «command», «set», and «ulimit».
#
#   Important for patching:
#
#   (2) This script targets any POSIX shell, so it avoids extensions provided
#       by Bash, Ksh, etc; in particular arrays are avoided.
#
#       The "traditional" practice of packing multiple parameters into a
#       space-separated string is a well documented source of bugs and security
#       problems, so this is (mostly) avoided, by progressively accumulating
#       options in "$@", and eventually passing that to Java.
#
#       Where the inherited environment variables (DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS,
#       and GRADLE_OPTS) rely on word-splitting, this is performed explicitly;
#       see the in-line comments for details.
#
#       There are tweaks for specific operating systems such as AIX, CygWin,
#       Darwin, MinGW, and NonStop.
#
#   (3) This script is generated from the Groovy template
#       https://github.com/gradle/gradle/blob/HEAD/subprojects/plugins/src/main/resources/org/gradle/api/internal/plugins/unixStartScript.txt
#       within the Gradle project.
#
#       You can find Gradle at https://github.com/gradle/gradle/.
#
##############################################################################

# Attempt to set APP_HOME

# Resolve links: $0 may be a link
app_path=$0

# Need this for daisy-chained symlinks.
while
    APP_HOME=${app_path%"${app_path##*/}"}  # leaves a trailing /; empty if no leading path
    [ -h "$app_path" ]
do
    ls=$( ls -ld "$app_path" )
    link=${ls#*' -> '}
    case $link in             #(
      /*)   app_path=$link ;; #(
      *)    app_path=$APP_HOME$link ;;
    esac
done

# This is normally unused
# shellcheck disable=SC2034
APP_BASE_NAME=${0##*/}
# Discard cd standard output in case $CDPATH is set (https://github.com/gradle/gradle/issues/25036)
APP_HOME=$( cd "${APP_HOME:-./}" > /dev/null && pwd -P ) || exit

# Use the maximum available, or set MAX_FD != -1 to use that value.
MAX_FD=maximum

warn () {
    echo "$*"
} >&2

die () {
    echo
    echo "$*"
    echo
    exit 1
} >&2

# OS specific support (must be 'true' or 'false').
cygwin=false
msys=false
darwin=false
nonstop=false
case "$( uname )" in                #(
  CYGWIN* )         cygwin=true  ;; #(
  Darwin* )         darwin=true  ;; #(
  MSYS* | MINGW* )  msys=true    ;; #(
  NONSTOP* )        nonstop=true ;;
esac

CLASSPATH=$APP_HOME/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar


# Determine the Java command to use to start the JVM.
if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
    if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
        # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java
    else
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/bin/java
    fi
    if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: $JAVA_HOME

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
else
    JAVACMD=java
    if ! command -v java >/dev/null 2>&1
    then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH.

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
fi

# Increase the maximum file descriptors if we can.
if ! "$cygwin" && ! "$darwin" && ! "$nonstop" ; then
    case $MAX_FD in #(
      max*)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -H is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        MAX_FD=$( ulimit -H -n ) ||
            warn "Could not query maximum file descriptor limit"
    esac
    case $MAX_FD in  #(
      '' | soft) :;; #(
      *)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -n is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        ulimit -n "$MAX_FD" ||
            warn "Could not set maximum file descriptor limit to $MAX_FD"
    esac
fi

# Collect all arguments for the java command, stacking in reverse order:
#   * args from the command line
#   * the main class name
#   * -classpath
#   * -D...appname settings
#   * --module-path (only if needed)
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and GRADLE_OPTS environment variables.

# For Cygwin or MSYS, switch paths to Windows format before running java
if "$cygwin" || "$msys" ; then
    APP_HOME=$( cygpath --path --mixed "$APP_HOME" )
    CLASSPATH=$( cygpath --path --mixed "$CLASSPATH" )

    JAVACMD=$( cygpath --unix "$JAVACMD" )

    # Now convert the arguments - kludge to limit ourselves to /bin/sh
    for arg do
        if
            case $arg in                                #(
              -*)   false ;;                            # don't mess with options #(
              /?*)  t=${arg#/} t=/${t%%/*}              # looks like a POSIX filepath
                    [ -e "$t" ] ;;                      #(
              *)    false ;;
            esac
        then
            arg=$( cygpath --path --ignore --mixed "$arg" )
        fi
        # Roll the args list around exactly as many times as the number of
        # args, so each arg winds up back in the position where it started, but
        # possibly modified.
        #
        # NB: a `for` loop captures its iteration list before it begins, so
        # changing the positional parameters here affects neither the number of
        # iterations, nor the values presented in `arg`.
        shift                   # remove old arg
        set -- "$@" "$arg"      # push replacement arg
    done
fi


# Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS='"-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"'

# Collect all arguments for the java command:
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and optsEnvironmentVar are not allowed to contain shell fragments,
#     and any embedded shellness will be escaped.
#   * For example: A user cannot expect ${Hostname} to be expanded, as it is an environment variable and will be
#     treated as '${Hostname}' itself on the command line.

set -- \
        "-Dorg.gradle.appname=$APP_BASE_NAME" \
        -classpath "$CLASSPATH" \
        org.gradle.wrapper.GradleWrapperMain \
        "$@"

# Stop when "xargs" is not available.
if ! command -v xargs >/dev/null 2>&1
then
    die "xargs is not available"
fi

# Use "xargs" to parse quoted args.
#
# With -n1 it outputs one arg per line, with the quotes and backslashes removed.
#
# In Bash we could simply go:
#
#   readarray ARGS < <( xargs -n1 <<<"$var" ) &&
#   set -- "${ARGS[@]}" "$@"
#
# but POSIX shell has neither arrays nor command substitution, so instead we
# post-process each arg (as a line of input to sed) to backslash-escape any
# character that might be a shell metacharacter, then use eval to reverse
# that process (while maintaining the separation between arguments), and wrap
# the whole thing up as a single "set" statement.
#
# This will of course break if any of these variables contains a newline or
# an unmatched quote.
#

eval "set -- $(
        printf '%s\n' "$DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS $JAVA_OPTS $GRADLE_OPTS" |
        xargs -n1 |
        sed ' s~[^-[:alnum:]+,./:=@_]~\\&~g; ' |
        tr '\n' ' '
    )" '"$@"'

exec "$JAVACMD" "$@"

```

`gradlew.bat`:

```bat
@rem
@rem Copyright 2015 the original author or authors.
@rem
@rem Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
@rem you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
@rem You may obtain a copy of the License at
@rem
@rem      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
@rem
@rem Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
@rem distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
@rem WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
@rem See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
@rem limitations under the License.
@rem

@if "%DEBUG%"=="" @echo off
@rem ##########################################################################
@rem
@rem  Gradle startup script for Windows
@rem
@rem ##########################################################################

@rem Set local scope for the variables with windows NT shell
if "%OS%"=="Windows_NT" setlocal

set DIRNAME=%~dp0
if "%DIRNAME%"=="" set DIRNAME=.
@rem This is normally unused
set APP_BASE_NAME=%~n0
set APP_HOME=%DIRNAME%

@rem Resolve any "." and ".." in APP_HOME to make it shorter.
for %%i in ("%APP_HOME%") do set APP_HOME=%%~fi

@rem Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
set DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS="-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"

@rem Find java.exe
if defined JAVA_HOME goto findJavaFromJavaHome

set JAVA_EXE=java.exe
%JAVA_EXE% -version >NUL 2>&1
if %ERRORLEVEL% equ 0 goto execute

echo.
echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH.
echo.
echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
echo location of your Java installation.

goto fail

:findJavaFromJavaHome
set JAVA_HOME=%JAVA_HOME:"=%
set JAVA_EXE=%JAVA_HOME%/bin/java.exe

if exist "%JAVA_EXE%" goto execute

echo.
echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: %JAVA_HOME%
echo.
echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
echo location of your Java installation.

goto fail

:execute
@rem Setup the command line

set CLASSPATH=%APP_HOME%\gradle\wrapper\gradle-wrapper.jar


@rem Execute Gradle
"%JAVA_EXE%" %DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS% %JAVA_OPTS% %GRADLE_OPTS% "-Dorg.gradle.appname=%APP_BASE_NAME%" -classpath "%CLASSPATH%" org.gradle.wrapper.GradleWrapperMain %*

:end
@rem End local scope for the variables with windows NT shell
if %ERRORLEVEL% equ 0 goto mainEnd

:fail
rem Set variable GRADLE_EXIT_CONSOLE if you need the _script_ return code instead of
rem the _cmd.exe /c_ return code!
set EXIT_CODE=%ERRORLEVEL%
if %EXIT_CODE% equ 0 set EXIT_CODE=1
if not ""=="%GRADLE_EXIT_CONSOLE%" exit %EXIT_CODE%
exit /b %EXIT_CODE%

:mainEnd
if "%OS%"=="Windows_NT" endlocal

:omega

```

`qqguard.conf.example`:

```example
# QQ Native Guard 配置示例
#
# 部署位置：/data/local/tmp/qqguard.conf
# 权限：chmod 644（QQ 进程需可读）
#
# 格式：KEY=VALUE，每行一个，# 开头为注释
# 空值或注释掉 = 使用真实值

# ============================================================
# 一、总开关
# ============================================================

# Native 层 hook 总开关（ByteHook）
ENABLE_NATIVE_HOOKS=true

# 设备信息伪造总开关
ENABLE_DEVICE_SPOOF=true

# 环境（机型画像）伪造总开关
ENV_SPOOF=true

# ============================================================
# 二、机型画像（必须整套自洽，否则被交叉验证发现）
# ============================================================

# 机型与品牌
ENV_MODEL=<MODEL>          # 例：PGT110 (OPPO Find X7)
ENV_DEVICE=<BRAND_MODEL>   # 例：OPPO_PGT110
ENV_PRODUCT_NAME=<PRODUCT> # 例：PGT110
ENV_BRAND=OPPO
ENV_MANUFACTURER=OPPO

# 系统版本
ENV_RELEASE=15
ENV_SDK=35
ENV_BUILD_ID=<BUILD_ID>    # 例：UKQ1.231003.002
ENV_INCREMENTAL=<INCREMENTAL>  # 例：1710000000
ENV_DISPLAY=<DISPLAY>      # 例：PGT110_15.0.1.200(CN01)

# 指纹（自洽规则：
#   BRAND/PRODUCT/DEVICE:RELEASE/BUILD_ID/INCREMENTAL:user/release-keys
#   DISPLAY 必须包含 MODEL；DEVICE 通常包含 MODEL）
ENV_FINGERPRINT=<BRAND>/<PRODUCT>/<DEVICE>:<RELEASE>/<BUILD_ID>/<INCREMENTAL>:user/release-keys

# 内核（保持真机值，伪造反而产生矛盾）
ENV_KERNEL_RELEASE=5.4.254-qgki-tqmane-g9e38a9fabb4d
ENV_KERNEL_VERSION=#1 SMP PREEMPT Wed Jul 29 22:33:04 CST 2026

# Build 元信息（保持真机值）
ENV_HOST=ReVork
ENV_USER=Sabrina Carpenter
ENV_DATE=2026-07-20
ENV_SECURITY_PATCH=2026-07-01

# ============================================================
# 三、SoC / 平台标识（★ 最容易漏，必须与机型匹配）
# ============================================================
#
# 平台代号对照：
#   lahaina   = 骁龙 888   (SM8350)   OnePlus 9 Pro / 小米 11
#   kalama    = 骁龙 8 Gen 2 (SM8550) 小米 13 / OPPO Find X6
#   pineapple = 骁龙 8 Gen 3 (SM8650) OPPO Find X7 / 小米 14
#   taro      = 骁龙 8 Gen 1 (SM8450)
#
#   例：若 ENV_MODEL=PGT110 (OPPO Find X7 = 骁龙 8 Gen 3)
#       则 ENV_SOC_PLATFORM 必须是 kalama 或 pineapple
# 如果这里留空，native 层会读到真机的 lahaina，与 PGT110 矛盾，服务端立刻识别伪造
ENV_SOC_PLATFORM=kalama
ENV_SOC_CHIPNAME=kalama
ENV_SOC_MODEL=SM8650
ENV_SOC_MANUFACTURER=Qualcomm

# ============================================================
# 四、设备唯一标识
# ============================================================

# 序列号（8 位 hex）
FAKE_SERIAL=<8-hex-serial>

# SoC 序列号（10 位数字）
FAKE_SOC_SERIAL=<10-digit-soc-serial>

# Android ID（16 位 hex，需同步 settings）
FAKE_ANDROID_ID=<16-hex-android-id>

# IMEI（15 位，必须 Luhn 校验通过）
FAKE_IMEI=<15-digit-imei-luhn-valid>

# OAID（UUID 格式）
FAKE_OAID=<oaid-uuid>

# QIMEI36（32 位 hex）
FAKE_QIMEI36=<32-hex-qimei36>

# ============================================================
# 五、网络上报（观测 vs 拦截）
# ============================================================
#
# 二选一：
#   - 观测模式（推荐）：挂 hook 但放行，只记录。QQ 行为完全正常。
#   - 拦截模式：挂 hook 并阻断。可能因"该发不发"被识别为异常。
#
# 两者都 false = 完全不挂 hook（无法诊断）

# 观测模式（挂 hook，不拦截）
NETWORK_OBSERVE_ONLY=true

# 拦截模式（挂 hook 并拦截）
ENABLE_NETWORK_INTERCEPT=false

# ============================================================
# 六、Native hook 细项开关
# ============================================================

# MMKV hook（★ 默认禁用：会导致 QQ 崩溃循环，5-6 秒一次）
ENABLE_MMKV_HOOK=false

# 签名校验绕过（可能影响稳定性，默认关闭）
ENABLE_MSF_SIGNATURE_BYPASS=false

# 调用者归因（性能开销，调试用）
ENABLE_CALLER_ATTRIBUTION=false

# ============================================================
# 七、日志
# ============================================================

# logcat 输出
VERBOSE_LOGGING=true

# 日志文件路径（留空 = 不写文件）
LOG_FILE=/storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/qqnativeguard.log

# 取证 dump（写入敏感数据，仅调试用）
ENABLE_FORENSICS_DUMP=false

# ============================================================
# 八、Turing 明文注入（deflate 层）
# ============================================================
#
# 部署位置：/storage/emulated/0/Android/data/com.tencent.mobileqq/inject.txt
# 格式：FROM_HEX=TO_HEX（每行一条）
#
# 用途：Turing 走 .real_syscall 绕过 libc hook，只能在 deflate 入口替换字节
# 示例（替换 DeviceID）：
#   6530...3935=6438...3931

ENABLE_TURING_INJECT=false
```

`tools/api_push.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""通过 GitHub API 更新文件 + 创建 release（git 443 不通时的备选）"""
import os
import json, sys, base64, urllib.request, urllib.error

sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
PAT = os.environ.get('GITHUB_TOKEN', '')
OWNER = 'KIDA-Nya'
REPO = 'QQNativeGuard'
API = 'https://api.github.com'
HDRS = {
    'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + PAT,
    'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json',
    'X-GitHub-Api-Version': '2022-11-28',
    'User-Agent': 'python-urllib',
}


def req(url, data=None, method='GET'):
    body = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8') if data is not None else None
    r = urllib.request.Request(url, data=body, method=method,
                               headers={**HDRS, 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8'})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(r, timeout=120) as resp:
        return json.loads(resp.read().decode('utf-8'))


# 1. 更新 README.md
print('=== 更新 README.md ===')
local = open(r'D:\app\QQNativeGuard\README.md', encoding='utf-8').read()

# 拿当前 sha
try:
    cur = req('%s/repos/%s/%s/contents/README.md' % (API, OWNER, REPO))
    sha = cur['sha']
    print('  current sha:', sha[:8])
    res = req('%s/repos/%s/%s/contents/README.md' % (API, OWNER, REPO), {
        'message': 'docs: add technical highlights section to README',
        'content': base64.b64encode(local.encode('utf-8')).decode('ascii'),
        'sha': sha,
        'branch': 'main',
    }, method='PUT')
    print('  updated, new sha:', res['content']['sha'][:8])
except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
    print('  HTTP', e.code, e.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')[:400])

# 2. 创建 Release + tag
print()
print('=== 创建 Release v1.0.0 ===')
release_body = '''Native-layer environment detection bypass for QQ, built on
[Xalsace/QQEnhancedBypass](https://github.com/Xalsace/QQEnhancedBypass) (GPL-3.0).

## 技术亮点

- **设备信息只走 4 个通道**（实测 3 小时抓包）：HTTP UA / QsecEst / MSF / VOD_MSDK。其余 10+ SDK 虽各自出网，但不上报新的设备维度。
- **`http.agent` 的 zygote 静态缓存**：AOSP `RuntimeInit` 在模块加载前就把 UA 算好，改 `Build.MODEL` 对它无效。实测 141/170 次请求泄露真机机型。
- **SoC 平台代号矛盾**：`k54=lahaina`（骁龙888）与任意非 SM8350 机型物理上不可能共存。这解释了"换全套指纹仍不触发新设备验证"。
- **`__system_property_find` 是隐藏的第二入口**：PCDN/VOD 走 find + read_callback，只 hook get 会漏。
- **Turing 走裸 syscall**：`.real_syscall` 绕过 libc，只能在 deflate 层注入。
- **观测模式**：拦截本身才是异常信号，所以提供 `NETWORK_OBSERVE_ONLY`。
- **负面结果同样记录**：三条加密通道都不可离线解；132,804 候选 appId 爆破 0 命中。
- **账号维度不可解**：同一环境，新号 9+h，已标记号 2h36m → 20min → 3min → w21。

## 能力

- ByteHook native hook 层，30+ hook 点
- 系统属性三入口（get / read_callback / find）
- 内容级 `/proc` 与 `/sys` 文件伪造（9 种）
- SoC 平台标识伪造（`ro.board.platform` + `/proc/cpuinfo`）
- `http.agent` 运行时重建
- 观测/拦截分离
- Turing deflate 层明文注入

## 验证

真机通过：Vector 正常加载，`All Native Hooks Installed Successfully`，QQ 零崩溃。

## 已知问题

MMKV hook 会导致 QQ 崩溃循环（5-6 秒一次），默认禁用。

## 许可证

GPL-3.0，继承上游。版权与修改声明见 NOTICE。
'''

try:
    rel = req('%s/repos/%s/%s/releases' % (API, OWNER, REPO), {
        'tag_name': 'v1.0.0',
        'target_commitish': 'main',
        'name': 'QQ Native Guard v1.0.0',
        'body': release_body,
        'draft': False,
        'prerelease': False,
    }, method='POST')
    print('  Release 创建成功!')
    print('  url:', rel['html_url'])
    print('  tag:', rel['tag_name'])
except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
    body = e.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    if e.code == 422 and 'already_exists' in body:
        print('  Release 已存在')
    else:
        print('  HTTP', e.code, body[:500])
```

`tools/audit_full.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# 完整排查：QQ 能用来判定 root/注入/篡改的所有信息源
# 分类：属性 / 文件 / 进程 / 系统调用 / 内嵌数据
PKG=com.tencent.mobileqq
PID=$(pidof $PKG)

echo "=== QQ pid: $PID ==="
echo

echo "########## A. 系统属性（QQ 通过 __system_property_get）##########"
echo "--- 解锁/引导状态 ---"
for p in ro.boot.verifiedbootstate ro.boot.flash.locked ro.boot.vbmeta.device_state \
         ro.boot.veritymode ro.boot.warranty_bit ro.warranty_bit ro.vendor.warranty_bit \
         ro.boot.secboot ro.boot.rpmb_enable ro.secboot; do
  v=$(getprop $p 2>/dev/null)
  [ -n "$v" ] && echo "  $p = $v"
done
echo
echo "--- 调试/构建类型 ---"
for p in ro.debuggable ro.secure ro.build.type ro.build.tags ro.build.selinux \
         ro.adb.secure service.adb.root persist.sys.usb.config; do
  v=$(getprop $p 2>/dev/null)
  [ -n "$v" ] && echo "  $p = $v"
done
echo
echo "--- 平台/机型真值 ---"
for p in ro.board.platform ro.soc.model ro.chipname ro.hardware \
         ro.product.model ro.product.device ro.product.name; do
  v=$(getprop $p 2>/dev/null)
  [ -n "$v" ] && echo "  $p = $v"
done
echo

echo "########## B. 文件系统（QQ 通过 fopen/stat）##########"
echo "--- root 框架目录 ---"
for d in /data/adb /data/adb/modules /data/adb/ksu /data/adb/magisk /sbin/.magisk /debug_ramdisk; do
  [ -e "$d" ] && echo "  存在: $d"
done
echo
echo "--- su / 提权二进制 ---"
for f in /system/bin/su /system/xbin/su /sbin/su /su/bin/su /data/adb/ksu/bin/su \
         /system/bin/failsafe/su /system/sd/xbin/su; do
  [ -e "$f" ] && echo "  存在: $f"
done
echo
echo "--- 模块列表（Zygisk/LSPosed）---"
ls /data/adb/modules/ 2>/dev/null | head -10
echo
echo "--- LSPosed 痕迹 ---"
for d in /data/adb/lspd /data/misc/lspd /data/local/tmp/lspd; do
  [ -e "$d" ] && echo "  存在: $d"
done
echo

echo "########## C. /proc 接口 ##########"
echo "--- /proc/cmdline 关键 token ---"
cat /proc/cmdline 2>/dev/null | tr ' ' '\n' | grep -E 'verifiedboot|flash.locked|vbmeta|warranty|project_codename|platform_name|type=|bootreason'
echo
echo "--- /proc/self/status TracerPid ---"
grep -a TracerPid /proc/self/status 2>/dev/null
echo
echo "--- /proc/mounts 里的 magisk/overlay ---"
cat /proc/self/mounts 2>/dev/null | grep -icE 'magisk|ksu|overlay'
echo "  (命中行数)"
echo

echo "########## D. 进程 ##########"
echo "--- root 管理进程 ---"
ps -A 2>/dev/null | grep -icE 'magiskd|ksud|apd'
echo "  (命中行数)"
echo
echo "--- 调试相关进程 ---"
ps -A 2>/dev/null | grep -icE 'gdbserver|frida'
echo "  (命中行数)"
echo

echo "########## E. 包名 ##########"
echo "--- root/Xposed 管理包 ---"
pm list packages 2>/dev/null | grep -icE 'magisk|kernelsu|ksu|supersu|apatch|shamiko|lsposed|taichi|virtualxposed|xposed'
echo "  (命中行数，已隐藏则为 0)"
echo
echo "--- 我们的模块是否可见 ---"
pm list packages 2>/dev/null | grep -c 'qqnativeguard\|qqenhanced'
echo "  (1=可见，会被 QQ 的 Root 检测扫到)"
echo

echo "########## F. 内核信息 ##########"
echo "--- uname ---"
uname -a 2>/dev/null
echo
echo "--- /proc/version ---"
cat /proc/version 2>/dev/null
echo
echo "--- /proc/cpuinfo Hardware ---"
grep -i hardware /proc/cpuinfo 2>/dev/null
echo

echo "########## G. SELinux ##########"
getenforce 2>/dev/null
echo

echo "########## H. 存储挂载（sdcardfs/fuse）##########"
mount 2>/dev/null | grep -E '/storage|/sdcard' | head -3
```

`tools/create_repo.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""在 GitHub 创建仓库 QQNativeGuard"""
import os
import json, sys, urllib.request, urllib.error

sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
PAT = os.environ.get('GITHUB_TOKEN', '')

desc = ('Native-layer environment detection bypass for QQ. ByteHook-based '
        'native hooks, four-channel device fingerprint consistency, and '
        'observable interception. Built on Xalsace/QQEnhancedBypass (GPL-3.0).')

payload = json.dumps({
    'name': 'QQNativeGuard',
    'description': desc,
    'private': False,
    'has_issues': True,
    'has_wiki': False,
    'has_projects': False,
    'license_template': '',   # 不用模板，我们自带 LICENSE
}, ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8')

req = urllib.request.Request(
    'https://api.github.com/user/repos',
    data=payload, method='POST',
    headers={
        'Authorization': 'token ' + PAT,
        'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json',
        'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=utf-8',
        'User-Agent': 'python-urllib',
    })

try:
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as r:
        d = json.loads(r.read().decode('utf-8'))
        print('仓库创建成功!')
        print('  full_name:', d['full_name'])
        print('  clone_url:', d['clone_url'])
        print('  html_url:', d['html_url'])
        print('  private:', d['private'])
except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
    body = e.read().decode('utf-8', 'replace')
    if e.code == 422 and 'already exists' in body:
        print('仓库已存在，直接推送即可')
        print('  url: https://github.com/KIDA-Nya/QQNativeGuard')
    else:
        print('HTTP', e.code)
        print(body[:1200])
except Exception as e:
    print('ERR:', type(e).__name__, str(e)[:300])
```

`tools/verify_all2.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""端到端验证 v2：正确区分"系统侧"（应为真值）和"QQ 进程内"（应为伪造值）"""

import sys, subprocess, os, json

sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')

R = []


def sh(cmd, timeout=90):
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(['adb', 'shell', cmd], capture_output=True, timeout=timeout)
        return r.stdout.decode('utf-8', 'replace').strip()
    except Exception as e:
        return 'ERR:' + str(e)[:60]


def check(name, ok, detail='', cat=''):
    R.append({'name': name, 'ok': ok, 'detail': detail[:70], 'cat': cat})
    print('  %-44s %s %s' % (name, 'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL', detail[:52]))


print('=== 0. 环境 ===')
pid = sh('pidof com.tencent.mobileqq')
if not pid:
    sh('am start -n com.tencent.mobileqq/.activity.SplashActivity')
    subprocess.run(['adb', 'shell', 'sleep', '22'], capture_output=True)
    pid = sh('pidof com.tencent.mobileqq')
print('QQ pid:', pid or '(未运行)')
print()

logs = sh('logcat -d -s QQNativeBypass:V QQEnhancedBypass:V')

print('=== 1. 模块加载 ===')
check('Native 库加载', 'Native Bypass Library Loaded' in logs, cat='module')
check('Native hook 全装', 'All Native Hooks Installed Successfully' in logs, cat='module')
check('JNI 无缺失', 'No implementation found' not in logs, cat='module')
print()

print('=== 2. Java hook ===')
lazy = int(sh('logcat -d -s QQEnhancedBypass:V | grep -c "LAZY-HOOK. attached"') or 0)
check('懒加载 hook >=4', lazy >= 4, 'attached=%d' % lazy, cat='java')
pand = int(sh('logcat -d -s QQEnhancedBypass:V | grep -c "Hooked all methods: com.tencent.qmethod.pandoraex.monitor.DeviceInfoMonitor"') or 0)
check('Pandora >=20 方法', pand >= 20, 'hooked=%d' % pand, cat='java')
check('camerasdk isDeviceRooted', 'DeviceInfo.isDeviceRooted' in logs, cat='java')
check('gathererga 完整路径', 'internal.provider.impl.UserInfoImpl' in logs, cat='java')
check('GProGuildSafetyRisk', 'GProGuildSafetyRisk' in logs, cat='java')
print()

print('=== 3. QQ 进程内设备身份（关键，来自模块自检日志）===')
# 这些是 QQ 进程内真实读到值，必须是伪造值
selftest_expect = [
    ('ro.board.platform', 'kalama'),
    ('ro.soc.model', 'SM8650'),
    ('soc_serial', '<soc>'),
    ('verifiedbootstate', 'green'),
    ('serialno', '<serial>'),
]
st_lines = [l for l in logs.split('\n') if 'Self-test' in l]
for key, want in selftest_expect:
    hit = [l for l in st_lines if key in l]
    if hit:
        line = hit[0]
        ok = want in line
        val = line.split("'")[1] if "'" in line else '?'
        check('QQ内 ' + key, ok, '= %s (want %s)' % (val[:24], want), cat='qq')
    else:
        check('QQ内 ' + key, False, '无自检行', cat='qq')
print()

print('=== 4. 系统侧（应为真值 —— 验证 hook 只影响 QQ 进程）===')
for prop, expect_real in [
    ('ro.board.platform', 'lahaina'),
    ('ro.soc.model', 'SM8350'),
    ('ro.boot.verifiedbootstate', 'orange'),
]:
    v = sh('getprop %s' % prop)
    check('系统侧 %s 仍为真值' % prop, v == expect_real, '= %s' % v[:30], cat='sys')
print()

print('=== 5. 稳定性 ===')
died = int(sh('logcat -d | grep -c "Process com.tencent.mobileqq.*has died"') or 0)
check('无崩溃', died == 0, 'died=%d' % died, cat='stable')
check('进程存活', bool(pid), 'pid=%s' % pid, cat='stable')
print()

print('=== 6. 硬约束包 ===')
for p in ['bin.mt.plus.canary', 'com.netease.uuremote', 'me.yxp.qfun']:
    r = sh('pm path %s' % p)
    check('包完好 ' + p.split('.')[-1], 'package:' in r, cat='hard')
blk = int(sh('logcat -d -s QQEnhancedBypass:V | grep "Blocked PackageManager" | grep -cE "mt.plus|netease"') or 0)
check('硬约束包未被拦', blk == 0, 'blocked=%d' % blk, cat='hard')
print()

print('=== 7. SELinux 隔离验证 ===')
uni = int(sh('run-as com.tencent.mobileqq cat /proc/net/unix 2>&1 | grep -c lspd') or 0)
check('QQ 读不到 lspd', uni == 0, 'count=%d' % uni, cat='selinux')
print()

print('=' * 60)
ok = sum(1 for r in R if r['ok'])
print('%d/%d PASS' % (ok, len(R)))
bad = [r for r in R if not r['ok']]
if bad:
    print('\n失败项:')
    for r in bad:
        print('  [%s] %s : %s' % (r['cat'], r['name'], r['detail']))

json.dump(R, open(r'D:\app\QQNativeGuard\tmp\verify_result.json', 'w', encoding='utf-8'),
          indent=1, ensure_ascii=False)
print('\n结果已保存 tmp/verify_result.json')
```
